```cpp
bool SehWorldPaused() {
    __try {
        const auto world = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(g_fn.getWorld());
        return world && Load<int>(world + 0x1458) > 0;
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
}

bool SehWorldLoaded(std::uintptr_t world) {
    __try {
        return Load<std::uintptr_t>(world + 0xE8) && g_tp.worldIsLoaded(reinterpret_cast<void*>(world));
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
}

// World and player unchanged? (otherwise save switch/reload: abort, never jump back)
bool SehSameIdentity() {
    __try {
        const auto world = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(g_fn.getWorld());
        return world && world == g_run.world && Load<std::uintptr_t>(world + 0x98) == g_run.player && g_fn.acpGate1();
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
}

void TpFinish(TpPhase phase, const char* text, std::uint64_t now) {
    g_run.phase = phase;
    g_run.finished = now;
    g_tpPhase.store(static_cast<int>(phase));
    TpSetText(text, now + 5000);
}

// One step of the jump sequence (every 100 ms). Returns whether this call wrote to the game.
bool TeleportStep(std::uint64_t now) {
    // Accept a request
    if (g_tpRequest.exchange(false)) {
        if (g_run.phase == TpPhase::Hold || g_run.phase == TpPhase::Watch || g_run.phase == TpPhase::ReturnHold) return false;
        TeleportInfo t{};
        SehTeleport(&t);
        char buf[160];
        if (g_tpState.load() != 1 || !t.valid) {
            TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, "Teleport not available.", now);
            return false;
        }
        const bool quest = g_tpRequestKind.load() == static_cast<int>(TpTarget::Quest);
        if (quest && !t.questAvailable) {
            TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, "Quest teleport not available for this game version.", now);
            return false;
        }
        const TpBlock block = quest ? t.questBlock : t.block;
        if ((block == TpBlock::Paused || block == TpBlock::Unavailable) && now < g_tpWaitUntil.load()) {  // map/menu open: keep the request
            g_tpRequest.store(true);
            TpSetText("Teleport ready - close the map to jump.", now + 60000);
            return false;
        }
        if (block != TpBlock::None) {
            std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "Teleport not possible: %s", TpBlockText(block));
            TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, buf, now);
            return false;
        }
        const float dist = quest ? t.questDistance : t.distance;
        const float maxDist = g_tpMaxDistance.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        if (dist > maxDist) {
            std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%s is %.0f m away - the range limit is %.0f m (see the teleport card).",
                          quest ? "Quest target" : "Marker", dist, maxDist);
            TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, buf, now);
            return false;
        }
        g_run = TpRun{};
        g_run.world = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(g_fn.getWorld());
        g_run.player = Load<std::uintptr_t>(g_run.world + 0x98);
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
            g_run.target[i] = quest ? t.questTarget[i] : t.target[i];
            g_run.origin[i] = t.player[i];
        }
        if (quest ? t.questEntity : t.markerEntity) {  // target is a character/object: land 2 m in front of it, not inside
            const float dx = t.player[0] - g_run.target[0], dy = t.player[1] - g_run.target[1];
            const float d = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
            if (d > 0.5f) {
                g_run.target[0] += dx / d * 2.0f;
                g_run.target[1] += dy / d * 2.0f;
            }
        }
        // Deep water at the target: place on the water surface instead of 1 m above the marker Z
        float level = 0.0f;
        if (g_extState.load() == 1 && SehWaterAt(g_run.world, g_run.target, false, &level)) {
            g_run.water = true;
            g_run.target[2] = level;
            g_run.zOff = 0.0f;
        }
        g_run.distance = dist;
        g_run.mounted = t.mounted;
        g_run.start = g_run.lastPlace = g_run.lastStep = now;
        g_run.places = 1;
        if (!SehTpPlace(g_run.player, g_run.target[0], g_run.target[1], g_run.target[2] + g_run.zOff, g_run.mounted)) {
            g_faulted.store(true);
            TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, "Error during the teleport - game functions disabled. Restart the game without saving.", now);
            return true;
        }
        g_run.phase = TpPhase::Hold;
        g_tpPhase.store(static_cast<int>(TpPhase::Hold));
        TpSetText("Teleporting...", now + 60000);
        return true;
    }

    const TpPhase ph = g_run.phase;
    if (ph != TpPhase::Hold && ph != TpPhase::Watch && ph != TpPhase::ReturnHold) return false;
    if (now - g_run.lastStep < 100) return false;
    g_run.lastStep = now;
    if (!SehSameIdentity()) {
        TpFinish(TpPhase::Aborted, "Teleport stopped (loading or save changed).", now);
        return false;
    }
    float pos[3];
    if (!SehTpPos(g_run.player, pos)) {
        TpFinish(TpPhase::Aborted, "Teleport stopped (player not readable).", now);
        return false;
    }
    const float* goal = ph == TpPhase::ReturnHold ? g_run.origin : g_run.target;
    const float zOff = ph == TpPhase::ReturnHold ? 0.3f : g_run.zOff;
    const float dz = pos[2] - goal[2];
    const float dx = pos[0] - goal[0], dy = pos[1] - goal[1];
    const float dxy = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

    if (ph == TpPhase::Hold || ph == TpPhase::ReturnHold) {
        // Hold until the world at the target is loaded: at least 1 s after the jump and 0.5 s continuously "loaded"
        const bool loaded = SehWorldLoaded(g_run.world);
        if (!loaded) g_run.loadedSince = 0;
        else if (g_run.loadedSince == 0) g_run.loadedSince = now;
        if (now - g_run.start >= 1000 && g_run.loadedSince && now - g_run.loadedSince >= 500) {
            // Water target: after loading, check the exact water height and re-place once (coarse grid near shores)
            if (ph == TpPhase::Hold && g_run.water && !g_run.refined) {
                g_run.refined = true;
                float level = 0.0f;
                if (g_extState.load() == 1 && SehWaterAt(g_run.world, g_run.target, true, &level) &&
                    std::fabs(level - g_run.target[2]) > 0.3f) {
                    g_run.target[2] = level;
                    g_run.lastPlace = now;
                    if (!SehTpPlace(g_run.player, g_run.target[0], g_run.target[1], level, g_run.mounted)) {
                        g_faulted.store(true);
                        TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, "Error during the teleport - game functions disabled. Restart the game without saving.", now);
                    }
                    return true;
                }
            }
            g_run.phase = TpPhase::Watch;
            g_tpPhase.store(static_cast<int>(TpPhase::Watch));
            if (!g_run.returning) {
                char landed[96];
                std::snprintf(landed, sizeof(landed), g_run.water ? "Teleported %.0f m (water surface)." : "Teleported %.0f m.",
                              g_run.distance);
                TpSetText(landed, now + 5000);
            }
            g_run.watchStart = now;
            return false;
        }
        if (now - g_run.start > 20000 || g_run.places >= 60) {
            if (ph == TpPhase::ReturnHold) {
                TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, "Teleport failed - could not settle at the start position.", now);
                return false;
            }
            g_run.returning = true;
            g_run.phase = TpPhase::ReturnHold;
        g_tpPhase.store(static_cast<int>(TpPhase::ReturnHold));
            g_run.start = g_run.lastPlace = now;
            g_run.places = 1;
            g_run.loadedSince = 0;
            if (!SehTpPlace(g_run.player, g_run.origin[0], g_run.origin[1], g_run.origin[2] + 0.3f, g_run.mounted)) {
                g_faulted.store(true);
                TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, "Error during the teleport - game functions disabled. Restart the game without saving.", now);
            }
            TpSetText("Teleport did not settle - returning to the start position...", now + 60000);
            return true;
        }
        // If the character sinks well below the target (terrain not there yet): place it on the target again
        if (dz < -3.0f && now - g_run.lastPlace >= 250) {
            g_run.lastPlace = now;
            ++g_run.places;
            if (!SehTpPlace(g_run.player, goal[0], goal[1], goal[2] + zOff, g_run.mounted)) {
                g_faulted.store(true);
                TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, "Error during the teleport - game functions disabled. Restart the game without saving.", now);
            }
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    // Watch: observe for 3 s. Long fall -> go back; the user walks away -> done.
    if (dz < -40.0f) {
        if (g_run.returning) {
            TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, "Teleport failed - still falling at the start position.", now);
            return false;
        }
        g_run.returning = true;
        g_run.phase = TpPhase::ReturnHold;
        g_tpPhase.store(static_cast<int>(TpPhase::ReturnHold));
        g_run.start = g_run.lastPlace = now;
        g_run.places = 1;
        g_run.loadedSince = 0;
        if (!SehTpPlace(g_run.player, g_run.origin[0], g_run.origin[1], g_run.origin[2] + 0.3f, g_run.mounted)) {
            g_faulted.store(true);
            TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, "Error during the teleport - game functions disabled. Restart the game without saving.", now);
        }
        TpSetText("No ground at the marker - returning to the start position...", now + 60000);
        return true;
    }
    if (now - g_run.watchStart >= 3000 || dxy > 25.0f) {
        if (g_run.returning) {
            TpFinish(TpPhase::Failed, "Teleport failed - you are back at the start position.", now);
        } else {  // success message is already shown since landing; just finish the state
            g_run.phase = TpPhase::Done;
            g_run.finished = now;
            g_tpPhase.store(static_cast<int>(TpPhase::Done));
        }
    }
    return false;
}

// --- Analysis round 8: time scale of an entity (experimental) ---
// The entity's own factor a (time info at +238h, a at +0); 1.0 without time info. Read only.
float TimeScaleOf(std::uintptr_t e) {
    const auto ti = Load<std::uintptr_t>(e + 0x238);
    return ti ? Load<float>(ti) : 1.0f;
}

// Is the mount valid? (world, the game's animal controller with type check, back-link)
bool MountValid(std::uintptr_t m, std::uintptr_t world) {
    if (!m || Load<std::uintptr_t>(m + 0x60) != world) return false;
    const auto ctrl = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(g_ext.animalCtrlOfEnt(reinterpret_cast<void*>(m)));
    return ctrl && Load<std::uintptr_t>(ctrl) == g_base + kVtblAnimalCtrl && Load<std::uintptr_t>(ctrl + 0x850) == m;
}

// One step: at most one set per call. Only factors > 0. A factor set by someone else (script) is never
// overwritten. off = reset everything. Returns whether it wrote to the game.
bool SehSpeedStep(bool off) {
    __try {
        if (!g_fn.acpGate1()) return false;
        const auto world = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(g_fn.getWorld());
        if (!world) return false;
        if (world != g_tsWorld) {  // new world / reloaded: the old entities no longer exist
            g_tsWorld = world;
            g_tsPlayer = g_tsMount = 0;
            g_tsPlayerSet = g_tsMountSet = 1.0f;
        }
        const auto player = Load<std::uintptr_t>(world + 0x98);
        if (!player || Load<std::uintptr_t>(player + 0x60) != world) return false;
        // The player's mount
        std::uintptr_t mount = 0;
        if (void** hp = g_tp.riderMountHandle(reinterpret_cast<void*>(player + 0x1F8), true)) {
            const auto m = DerefHandle(reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(*hp));
            if (MountValid(m, world)) mount = m;
        }
        // Requested values
        const float mountWant = off ? 1.0f : g_mountScale.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        float playerWant = 1.0f;
        if (!off && !mount) {
            const float f = g_swimScale.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
            if (f != 1.0f)
                if (void* st = g_tp.charStateObj(reinterpret_cast<void*>(player)); st && g_tp.charStateIsActive(st, 0x11)) playerWant = f;
        }
        // 1. reset the old mount (dismounted / switched)
        if (g_tsMount && g_tsMount != mount) {
            const auto old = g_tsMount;
            g_tsMount = 0;
            if (MountValid(old, world) && TimeScaleOf(old) == g_tsMountSet) {
                g_tsMountSet = 1.0f;
                g_ext.entSetTimeScale(reinterpret_cast<void*>(old), 1.0f);
                return true;
            }
            g_tsMountSet = 1.0f;
        }
        // 2. mount
        if (mount) {
            const float cur = TimeScaleOf(mount);
            const bool ours = g_tsMount == mount && cur == g_tsMountSet;
            if (cur != mountWant && (ours || cur == 1.0f) && mountWant > 0.0f) {
                g_ext.entSetTimeScale(reinterpret_cast<void*>(mount), mountWant);
                g_tsMount = mountWant != 1.0f ? mount : 0;
                g_tsMountSet = mountWant;
                return true;
            }
        }
        // 3. player (only while swimming on the surface)
        if (g_tsPlayer && g_tsPlayer != player) g_tsPlayer = 0;  // different player: do not touch the old entity
        const float cur = TimeScaleOf(player);
        const bool ours = g_tsPlayer == player && cur == g_tsPlayerSet;
        if (cur != playerWant && (ours || cur == 1.0f) && playerWant > 0.0f) {
            g_ext.entSetTimeScale(reinterpret_cast<void*>(player), playerWant);
            g_tsPlayer = playerWant != 1.0f ? player : 0;
            g_tsPlayerSet = playerWant;
            return true;
        }
        if (!ours && g_tsPlayer == player) g_tsPlayer = 0;  // a script took over
        return false;
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        g_extState.store(2);
        g_tsPlayer = g_tsMount = 0;
        return false;
    }
}

// Read the weather and reconcile it with the user's request (analysis round 7): pause randomness + set target, or release.
// Mirrors the game's own script action (target + pause, undone at the end). Never count below 0.
void SehWeather(GameInfo* r) {
    __try {
        if (!g_fn.acpGate1()) return;
        const auto world = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(g_fn.getWorld());
        const std::uintptr_t tod = CurrentTod();
        if (!world || !tod || Load<std::uintptr_t>(tod + 0x240) != g_base + kWeatherState) return;
        void* mgr = reinterpret_cast<void*>(tod);
        const auto climate = Load<std::uintptr_t>(world + 0x108);
        r->climateOff = climate == 0 || Load<unsigned char>(climate + 0xA4) == 0;
        const bool paused = g_fn.wxIsRandPaused(mgr);
        const bool wantHold = g_wxWantHold.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);

        if (wantHold && !r->climateOff) {
            if (!g_wxWeHold) {
                if (!paused) {  // take over as long as no script holds the weather
                    g_fn.wxSetRandPaused(mgr, true);
                    g_wxWeHold = true;
                    g_wxLastTarget = -1.0f;
                }
            } else if (!paused) {  // world rebuilt (counter reset): take over again
                g_fn.wxSetRandPaused(mgr, true);
                g_wxLastTarget = -1.0f;
            }
            const float want = g_wxWantValue.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
            if (g_wxWeHold && want != g_wxLastTarget) {
                g_fn.wxSetTarget(mgr, want, g_wxSeconds.load(std::memory_order_relaxed));
                g_wxLastTarget = want;
            }
        } else if (g_wxWeHold) {
            if (g_fn.wxIsRandPaused(mgr)) g_fn.wxSetRandPaused(mgr, false);  // hand back to the game
            g_wxWeHold = false;
            g_wxLastTarget = -1.0f;
        }
        r->weatherHeld = g_wxWeHold;
        r->weatherScripted = !g_wxWeHold && g_fn.wxIsRandPaused(mgr);
        r->weather = g_fn.wxGetCurrent(mgr);
        r->weatherValid = true;
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        g_faulted.store(true);
    }
}

void FormatResult(const PendingAction& a, const ActOut& o, char* buf, std::size_t n) {
    switch (o.r) {
        case ActResult::NotReady: std::snprintf(buf, n, "Not possible right now (menu, loading or cutscene) - try again in the game world."); return;
        case ActResult::NoPlayer: std::snprintf(buf, n, "No player loaded."); return;
        case ActResult::MultiplierZero: std::snprintf(buf, n, "XP gain multiplier is 0 %% - the game would grant nothing."); return;
        case ActResult::NoTod: std::snprintf(buf, n, "No time of day in this area."); return;
        case ActResult::Fault: std::snprintf(buf, n, "Error during the game call - game functions disabled for this session."); return;
        case ActResult::AlreadyThere: std::snprintf(buf, n, "Already level %u or higher.", o.before); return;
        case ActResult::Ok: break;
    }
    switch (a.action) {
        case Action::SetTime: {
            const int h = static_cast<int>(a.hours) % 24;
            const int m = static_cast<int>((a.hours - static_cast<float>(static_cast<int>(a.hours))) * 60.0f + 0.5f) % 60;
            std::snprintf(buf, n, "Time set to %02d:%02d.", h, m);
            return;
        }
        case Action::GrantXp:
            if (o.after == o.before) std::snprintf(buf, n, "The game granted no XP (maximum level reached or level cap active?).");
            else std::snprintf(buf, n, "+%u XP (now %u XP).", o.after - o.before, o.after);
            return;
        case Action::LevelAtLeast:
            if (o.after == o.before) std::snprintf(buf, n, "Level unchanged (%u) - level cap active?", o.after);
            else std::snprintf(buf, n, "Level %u -> %u (ability points for each level were granted by the game).", o.before, o.after);
            return;
        case Action::GrantPoints:
            std::snprintf(buf, n, "+%u ability point(s) (now %u available).", o.sent, o.after);
            return;
    }
}

void Publish(const char* text) {
    // The detour holds g_actMutex. Publish without blocking; otherwise next time.
    std::unique_lock lock(g_resultMutex, std::try_to_lock);
    if (!lock.owns_lock()) {
        if (text != g_unpublished) std::snprintf(g_unpublished, sizeof(g_unpublished), "%s", text);
        g_hasUnpublished = true;
        return;
    }
    std::snprintf(g_resultText, sizeof(g_resultText), "%s", text);
    ++g_resultSeq;
    g_hasUnpublished = false;
}

// Executes a pending action. true = something in the game was changed (read again afterwards).
bool RunPendingAction(std::uint64_t now) {
    std::unique_lock lock(g_actMutex, std::try_to_lock);
    if (!lock.owns_lock()) return false;
    if (g_hasUnpublished) Publish(g_unpublished);
    if (!g_pending.set) return false;
    const PendingAction a = g_pending;
    g_pending.set = false;
    char buf[192];
    if (now > a.expires) {
        std::snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "Not executed - not possible right now (menu, loading or cutscene?). Try again in the game world.");
        Publish(buf);
        return false;
    }
    ActOut o;
    SehAct(&a, &o);
    if (o.r == ActResult::NotReady) {
        g_pending = a;  // gate currently closed (menu, loading screen): retry until it expires
        return false;
    }
    FormatResult(a, o, buf, sizeof(buf));
    Publish(buf);
    return o.r == ActResult::Ok;
}

}  // namespace

void SetEnabled(bool enabled) { g_enabled.store(enabled, std::memory_order_release); }

BridgeState State() { return g_state.load(); }

const char* StateText(BridgeState s) {
    switch (s) {
        case BridgeState::Disabled: return "off";
        case BridgeState::WaitingForAcp: return "waiting for a 'Hacked Animus' save";
        case BridgeState::Ready: return "active";
        case BridgeState::Unsupported: return "not available for this game version";
        case BridgeState::Failed: return "disabled after an error (see log)";
    }
    return "?";
}

const char* TimeStopText(TimeStop s) {
    switch (s) {
        case TimeStop::None: return "running";
        case TimeStop::QuestPause: return "stopped by the current quest";
        case TimeStop::CameraStill: return "paused while the camera is still (up to 15 s)";
        case TimeStop::WorldPaused: return "game paused";
    }
    return "?";
}

void LifecycleTick(bool acpActive, bool anyHost) {
    BridgeState s = g_state.load();
    if (s == BridgeState::Unsupported || s == BridgeState::Failed) {
        // final for this session
    } else if (g_faulted.load()) {
        s = BridgeState::Failed;
    } else if (!g_enabled.load()) {
        s = BridgeState::Disabled;
    } else if (s != BridgeState::Ready) {
        if (anyHost) {
            s = BridgeState::Unsupported;  // the self-test host is not the game
        } else if (acpActive) {
            // Strict reading: verify only once the game reports ACP active.
            wchar_t exe[MAX_PATH]{};
            GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, exe, MAX_PATH);
            const auto st = platform::StatFile(exe);
            g_base = reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(GetModuleHandleW(nullptr));
            std::string detail;
            if (!st || st->size != kExeSize) {
                s = BridgeState::Unsupported;
                platform::log::Write("Game functions: unknown game build - off");
            } else if (const int bad = VerifyAndResolve(detail); bad != 0) {
                s = BridgeState::Unsupported;
                platform::log::Write("Game functions: " + std::to_string(bad) + " checksum(s) mismatched: " + detail + "- off");
            } else {
                s = BridgeState::Ready;
                platform::log::Write("Game functions: " + std::to_string(Table().size()) + "/" +
                                     std::to_string(Table().size()) + " checksums ok");
                std::string tpDetail;
                if (const int tpBad = VerifyAndResolveTp(tpDetail); tpBad == 0) {
                    g_tpState.store(1);
                    platform::log::Write("Teleport (8a): " + std::to_string(TpTable().size()) + "/" +
                                         std::to_string(TpTable().size()) + " checksums ok");
                } else {
                    g_tpState.store(2);
                    platform::log::Write("Teleport: " + std::to_string(tpBad) + " checksum(s) mismatched: " + tpDetail + "- teleport off");
                }
                std::string extDetail;
                if (const int extBad = VerifyAndResolveExt(extDetail); extBad == 0) {
                    g_extState.store(1);
                    platform::log::Write("Round 8 (water, quest target, time scale): " + std::to_string(ExtTable().size()) + "/" +
                                         std::to_string(ExtTable().size()) + " checksums ok, " + std::to_string(ExtCalls().size()) +
                                         " call targets ok");
                } else {
                    g_extState.store(2);
                    platform::log::Write("Round 8: " + std::to_string(extBad) + " checksum(s) mismatched: " + extDetail + "- off");
                }
            }
        } else {
            s = BridgeState::WaitingForAcp;
        }
    }
    g_state.store(s);
    if (s != g_loggedState) {
        if (s == BridgeState::Failed) platform::log::Write("Game functions: exception during access - off for this session");
        g_loggedState = s;
    }
}

void OnPreStep(bool acpActive) noexcept {
    if (g_state.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != BridgeState::Ready || g_faulted.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) return;
    const std::uint64_t now = GetTickCount64();
    const bool speedOk = acpActive && g_extState.load() == 1 && g_tpState.load() == 1;
    if (!g_enabled.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
        // Game functions turned off: only reset time scales that are still set
        if (speedOk && g_tsActive.load() && now >= g_tsNext) {
            g_tsNext = now + 100;
            SehSpeedStep(true);
            g_tsActive.store(g_tsPlayer != 0 || g_tsMount != 0);
        }
        return;
    }
    if (acpActive && g_tpEnabled.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) && g_tpState.load() == 1) {
        g_paused.store(SehWorldPaused());
        g_pausedTick.store(now);
    }
    const bool tpWrote = acpActive && g_tpEnabled.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) && g_tpState.load() == 1 && TeleportStep(now);
    bool wrote = tpWrote;
    if (!tpWrote && acpActive && RunPendingAction(now)) {
        g_nextRead.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);  // show the result immediately
        wrote = true;
    }
    if (!wrote && speedOk && now >= g_tsNext) {  // at most one writing game call per PreStep
        g_tsNext = now + 100;
        SehSpeedStep(false);
        g_tsActive.store(g_tsPlayer != 0 || g_tsMount != 0);
    }
    if (acpActive) ClockTick(now);
    if (g_faulted.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) return;
    if (now < g_nextRead.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) return;
    g_nextRead.store(now + 200, std::memory_order_relaxed);

    GameInfo r{};
    const bool ok = acpActive && SehReadGame(&r);
    const bool hold = g_holdOn.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (ok && hold) SehHold(g_holdHours.load(std::memory_order_relaxed), r.hours);
    r.holding = hold;
    if (ok) SehWeather(&r);
    if (ok && g_tpEnabled.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) && g_tpState.load() == 1) SehTeleport(&r.teleport);
    r.ownClock = !hold && g_clockActive.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    std::unique_lock lock(g_infoMutex, std::try_to_lock);
    if (!lock.owns_lock()) return;
    if (ok) {
        r.tick = now;
        g_info = r;
    } else {
        g_info.valid = false;
    }
}

bool Read(GameInfo& out) {
    std::lock_guard lock(g_infoMutex);
    out = g_info;
    return out.valid && GetTickCount64() - out.tick < 2000 && g_state.load() == BridgeState::Ready;
}

void Request(Action a, int value, float hours, float xpMultiplierPct) {
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(g_resultMutex);
        g_resultIsTime = a == Action::SetTime;
    }
    if (g_state.load() != BridgeState::Ready || !g_enabled.load()) {
        std::lock_guard lock(g_resultMutex);
        std::snprintf(g_resultText, sizeof(g_resultText), "Game functions are not active (%s).", StateText(g_state.load()));
        ++g_resultSeq;
        return;
    }
    std::lock_guard lock(g_actMutex);
    g_pending.set = true;
    g_pending.action = a;
    g_pending.value = value;
    g_pending.hours = hours < 0.0f ? 0.0f : hours >= 24.0f ? 23.99f : hours;
    g_pending.xpMultiplierPct = xpMultiplierPct;
    g_pending.expires = GetTickCount64() + 5000;
}

void SetHold(bool on, float hours) {
    g_holdHours.store(hours < 0.0f ? 0.0f : hours >= 24.0f ? 23.99f : hours, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    g_holdOn.store(on, std::memory_order_relaxed);
}

void SetTeleportEnabled(bool enabled) { g_tpEnabled.store(enabled, std::memory_order_relaxed); }

void TeleportRequest(TpTarget target, bool waitForMap) {
    g_tpRequestKind.store(static_cast<int>(target));
    g_tpWaitUntil.store(waitForMap ? GetTickCount64() + 20000 : 0);
    TpSetText("Teleporting...", GetTickCount64() + 60000);  // visible immediately, not only at the next game step
    g_tpRequest.store(true);
}

void SetTeleportMaxDistance(float meters) { g_tpMaxDistance.store(meters, std::memory_order_relaxed); }

void SetTeleportOptions(bool allowCombat, bool allowMounted) {
    g_tpAllowCombat.store(allowCombat, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    g_tpAllowMounted.store(allowMounted, std::memory_order_relaxed);
}

bool TeleportBusy() {
    const auto ph = static_cast<TpPhase>(g_tpPhase.load());
    return g_tpRequest.load() || ph == TpPhase::Hold || ph == TpPhase::Watch || ph == TpPhase::ReturnHold;
}

bool TeleportStatus(char* buf, std::size_t n) {
    std::lock_guard lock(g_tpTextMutex);
    if (!g_tpText[0] || GetTickCount64() > g_tpTextUntil) return false;
    std::snprintf(buf, n, "%s", g_tpText);
    return true;
}

const char* TpBlockText(TpBlock b) {
    switch (b) {
        case TpBlock::None: return "ready";
        case TpBlock::Unavailable: return "not available right now";
        case TpBlock::NotLocalPlayer: return "player not found";
        case TpBlock::NoMarker: return "no custom marker set on the map";
        case TpBlock::MarkerUnreadable: return "marker found, position not readable";
        case TpBlock::OtherWorld: return "marker is on another map";
        case TpBlock::Loading: return "loading";
        case TpBlock::WorldNotLoaded: return "world still loading";
        case TpBlock::Paused: return "game paused";
        case TpBlock::FastTravelLocked: return "fast travel is blocked here (mission, arena, ...)";
        case TpBlock::QuestBlocks: return "the current quest forbids travel";
        case TpBlock::InCombat: return "in combat";
        case TpBlock::EagleControl: return "controlling Senu";
        case TpBlock::Mounted: return "mounted / on a boat - dismount first";
        case TpBlock::TooFar: return "target too far away (over 30 km)";
        case TpBlock::TooClose: return "already at the target";
        case TpBlock::NoQuestTracked: return "no quest is tracked";
        case TpBlock::NoQuestTarget: return "the tracked quest shows no target on the map";
        case TpBlock::QuestOtherRegion: return "the quest target is in another region";
    }
    return "?";
}

void SetWeather(bool hold, float intensity, float seconds) {
    g_wxWantValue.store(intensity < 0.0f ? 0.0f : intensity > 1.0f ? 1.0f : intensity, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    g_wxSeconds.store(seconds < 0.0f ? 0.0f : seconds, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    g_wxWantHold.store(hold, std::memory_order_relaxed);
}

void SetOwnClock(bool on, const float minutes[4]) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) g_clockMin[i].store(minutes[i], std::memory_order_relaxed);
    g_clockOn.store(on, std::memory_order_relaxed);
}

void SetSpeedBoost(float swimFactor, float mountFactor) {
    if (g_tsShutdown.load()) return;
    auto clamp = [](float f) { return f < 1.0f ? 1.0f : f > 4.0f ? 4.0f : f; };
    g_swimScale.store(clamp(swimFactor), std::memory_order_relaxed);
    g_mountScale.store(clamp(mountFactor), std::memory_order_relaxed);
}

bool GamePausedNow() { return g_paused.load() && GetTickCount64() - g_pausedTick.load() < 500; }

bool SpeedBoostActive() { return g_tsActive.load(); }

bool SpeedBoostAvailable() { return g_state.load() == BridgeState::Ready && g_extState.load() == 1 && g_tpState.load() == 1; }

void ReleaseSpeedBoost() {
    g_tsShutdown.store(true);
    g_swimScale.store(1.0f);
    g_mountScale.store(1.0f);
}

std::string LastResult(std::uint32_t* seq, bool* isTime) {
    std::lock_guard lock(g_resultMutex);
    if (seq) *seq = g_resultSeq;
    if (isTime) *isTime = g_resultIsTime;
    return g_resultText;
}

}  // namespace acp::game

```

`src/game/game_bridge.h`:

```h
#pragma once

// Bridge to the game's own functions (based on private static analysis notes, not included).
// Stage 7a: read (time of day, reason time is blocked, level, XP, ability points).
// Stage 7b/7c: set/hold time of day, grant XP/level/ability points - only through the game's own functions.
// All game access happens exclusively in OnPreStep() (called from the UPLAY_GetNextEvent detour, engine task "PreStep"),
// and only if: feature enabled, checksums ok, game reports ACP active, the game's own gate is true, vtbl checks ok.

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>

namespace acp::game {

enum class BridgeState : std::uint8_t {
    Disabled,      // option off
    WaitingForAcp, // waiting for "Hacked Animus" in the game
    Ready,         // checksums ok, reading
    Unsupported,   // different game build / checksum mismatch
    Failed,        // exception during an access - off for this session
};

enum class TimeStop : std::uint8_t { None, QuestPause, CameraStill, WorldPaused };

// --- Teleport (analysis round 6), stage 8a: read only (marker, distance, blocking reason) ---
enum class TpBlock : std::uint8_t {
    None, Unavailable, NotLocalPlayer, NoMarker, OtherWorld, Loading, WorldNotLoaded, Paused, FastTravelLocked,
    QuestBlocks, InCombat, EagleControl, Mounted, TooFar, TooClose, MarkerUnreadable,
    NoQuestTracked, NoQuestTarget, QuestOtherRegion,
};
enum class TpTarget : std::uint8_t { Marker, Quest };
struct TeleportInfo {
    bool valid = false;           // measurement available (teleport checksums ok, ACP active)
    TpBlock block = TpBlock::Unavailable;
    int markerSource = 0;         // 0 none, 1 UIAfterlifeManager block, 2 CustomMarkerModule only (no position)
    bool blockFlagM6A = false;    // diagnostics: ready flag M+6A
    bool moduleSet = false;       // diagnostics: module reports a marker is set
    int posMode = 0;              // diagnostics, variant 2: 0 none, 1 final position, 2 target position, 3 unreadable
    int playerBits = 0;           // player diagnostics: 1 pointer, 2 world back-link, 4 control object, 8 "local" per game
    bool mounted = false;         // attached/mounted (mount, boat)
    int combatDiag = 0;           // combat detection diagnostics (0..3)
    bool markerEntity = false;    // marker is attached to a character/object (land next to it)
    float target[3] = {0, 0, 0};
    float player[3] = {0, 0, 0};
    float distance = 0;           // 3D in meters
    float distanceXY = 0;
    // Analysis round 8: quest target (nearest active map icon of the tracked quest)
    bool questAvailable = false;  // round 8 functions verified
    TpBlock questBlock = TpBlock::Unavailable;
    float questTarget[3] = {0, 0, 0};
    float questDistance = 0;
    int questIcons = 0;           // diagnostics: quest icons / how many of them are usable
    int questUsable = 0;
    bool questEntity = false;     // target is attached to a character/object (land next to it)
};
const char* TpBlockText(TpBlock b);

// Stage 8b: jump to the marker. The render thread requests, OnPreStep executes (state machine, at most one
// writing game call per PreStep). Test limit: kTpStageMaxDistance.
// Range (render thread, from settings): 300 m, 2 km or the whole map (30 km, the game's limit).
void SetTeleportMaxDistance(float meters);
// Experimental exceptions (render thread, from settings): allow in combat or while mounted.
void SetTeleportOptions(bool allowCombat, bool allowMounted);
// waitForMap: quick teleport via hotkey - if the game is currently paused (map open), wait up to 20 s and
// jump as soon as the map is closed.
void TeleportRequest(TpTarget target = TpTarget::Marker, bool waitForMap = false);
// true while a jump is in progress (jumping, holding, observing, jumping back) - unloading waits for it.
bool TeleportBusy();
// Progress for the on-screen notice: text and whether it should be shown now (running or recently finished).
bool TeleportStatus(char* buf, std::size_t n);

struct GameInfo {
    bool valid = false;        // values come from the current ACP state
    float hours = 0;           // 0..24
    bool displayOverride = false;  // a script displays a different time of day
    TimeStop stop = TimeStop::None;
    bool acpTimeBlocked = false;   // a script makes the game ignore the ACP day lengths (default speed)
    bool holding = false;          // "Hold time of day" active
    bool ownClock = false;         // our own time-lapse clock is currently driving the time
    // Weather (analysis round 7): intensity 0 = clear .. 1 = storm
    bool weatherValid = false;
    float weather = 0;
    bool weatherHeld = false;      // we are holding the weather (randomness paused)
    bool weatherScripted = false;  // a script/mission currently controls the weather
    bool climateOff = false;       // climate system off in this world (weather not controllable)
    bool hasPlayer = false;
    std::uint32_t level = 0;
    std::uint32_t xp = 0;
    std::uint32_t abilityPoints = 0;
    TeleportInfo teleport;
    std::uint64_t tick = 0;    // GetTickCount64 of the measurement
};

// Render thread: apply the option from the settings.
void SetEnabled(bool enabled);
// Render thread: teleport measurement on/off (opt-in, in addition to the game functions).
void SetTeleportEnabled(bool enabled);
// Lifecycle thread: verify the game functions once enabled and ACP is active; log state changes.
void LifecycleTick(bool acpActive, bool anyHost);
// UPLAY_GetNextEvent detour (game task PreStep): the only place with game access. noexcept, no allocation, no log.
void OnPreStep(bool acpActive) noexcept;

BridgeState State();
const char* StateText(BridgeState s);
const char* TimeStopText(TimeStop s);
// Render thread: latest measurement (false if no valid one exists).
bool Read(GameInfo& out);

// --- Stage 7b/7c: actions (render thread queues, OnPreStep executes; at most one per game update) ---
enum class Action : std::uint8_t {
    GrantXp,        // value = XP (before multiplier; compensated with xpMultiplierPct)
    LevelAtLeast,   // value = target level (upwards only, via XP)
    GrantPoints,    // value = ability points
    SetTime,        // hours = time of day 0..24
};
// Queues an action (replaces one not yet executed). Expires after 5 s.
void Request(Action a, int value, float hours, float xpMultiplierPct);
// Hold the time of day (sets it again as soon as it drifts by more than one game minute).
void SetHold(bool on, float hours);
// Own time-lapse clock: only while a script blocks the ACP day lengths, the overlay advances the clock itself
// at the speed of the ACP values. minutes[i] = real-time minutes per phase (morning, noon, afternoon, night),
// already adjusted for "Length of the day".
void SetOwnClock(bool on, const float minutes[4]);
// Weather: hold = keep the weather at intensity (0..1) (transition over seconds), false = hand it back to the game.
// Reconciled in OnPreStep (set again after the world reloads). Not stored in the save game.
void SetWeather(bool hold, float intensity, float seconds);
// true if the game is currently paused (map/menu open); measured in PreStep, at most 0.5 s old.
bool GamePausedNow();
// Analysis round 8 (experimental): time scale only for the player character while swimming on the surface, or only
// for the player's own mount (1.0 = off, 1.0..2.0). OnPreStep sets and resets it (dismount, surfacing, turning off).
void SetSpeedBoost(float swimFactor, float mountFactor);
// true while any factor other than 1 is set (unloading resets it first).
bool SpeedBoostActive();
// Round 8 functions verified and ready (for display).
bool SpeedBoostAvailable();
// Unloading: have all factors reset to 1 and accept no new requests.
void ReleaseSpeedBoost();
// Result of the last action (empty text = none yet). seq counts up.
std::string LastResult(std::uint32_t* seq = nullptr, bool* isTime = nullptr);

}  // namespace acp::game
```

`src/hook/dx11_hooks.cpp`:

```cpp
// Detours for IDXGISwapChain::Present, Present1 and ResizeBuffers.
#include <dxgi1_2.h>

#include "MinHook.h"
#include "frame_pacer.h"
#include "hooks.h"

namespace acp::hook {
namespace {

using Present_t = HRESULT(STDMETHODCALLTYPE*)(IDXGISwapChain*, UINT, UINT);
using Present1_t = HRESULT(STDMETHODCALLTYPE*)(IDXGISwapChain1*, UINT, UINT, const DXGI_PRESENT_PARAMETERS*);
using ResizeBuffers_t = HRESULT(STDMETHODCALLTYPE*)(IDXGISwapChain*, UINT, UINT, UINT, DXGI_FORMAT, UINT);

Present_t g_oPresent = nullptr;
Present1_t g_oPresent1 = nullptr;
ResizeBuffers_t g_oResizeBuffers = nullptr;

thread_local int t_presentDepth = 0;

// SEH protection without C++ objects in the same frame.
bool SehOnPresent(IDXGISwapChain* sc) {
    __try {
        OnPresent(sc);
        return true;
    } __except (EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) {
        return false;
    }
}

void MaybeDoOverlayWork(IDXGISwapChain* sc, UINT flags) {
    if (flags & (DXGI_PRESENT_TEST | DXGI_PRESENT_DO_NOT_SEQUENCE)) return;
    if (t_presentDepth > 0) return;  // nested call (Present1 -> Present or similar)
    const Phase phase = G().phase.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    ++t_presentDepth;
    if (phase == Phase::Running && !G().faulted.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
        if (!SehOnPresent(sc)) {
            // After an SEH exception locks may still be held: overlay permanently off, lock nothing anymore.
            G().faulted = true;
            G().overlayOpen = false;
        }
    } else if (phase == Phase::ShuttingDown && G().teardownRequested.load() && !G().teardownDone.load()) {
        TeardownRender();
    }
    --t_presentDepth;
}

// Frame rate limiter: only on the outermost, real Present (not on test/nested calls).
void MaybePace(bool outer) {
    if (!outer || G().phase.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != Phase::Running || G().faulted.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) return;
    const HWND game = G().gameWindow.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    pacing::BeforePresent(game == nullptr || GetForegroundWindow() == game);
}

bool IsOuter(UINT flags) { return t_presentDepth == 0 && !(flags & (DXGI_PRESENT_TEST | DXGI_PRESENT_DO_NOT_SEQUENCE)); }

HRESULT STDMETHODCALLTYPE HkPresent(IDXGISwapChain* sc, UINT syncInterval, UINT flags) {
    InFlight guard(G().inFlightPresent);
    const bool outer = IsOuter(flags);
    MaybeDoOverlayWork(sc, flags);
    MaybePace(outer);
    return g_oPresent(sc, syncInterval, flags);
}

HRESULT STDMETHODCALLTYPE HkPresent1(IDXGISwapChain1* sc, UINT syncInterval, UINT flags,
                                      const DXGI_PRESENT_PARAMETERS* params) {
    InFlight guard(G().inFlightPresent);
    const bool outer = IsOuter(flags);
    MaybeDoOverlayWork(sc, flags);
    MaybePace(outer);
    return g_oPresent1(sc, syncInterval, flags, params);
}

HRESULT STDMETHODCALLTYPE HkResizeBuffers(IDXGISwapChain* sc, UINT count, UINT w, UINT h, DXGI_FORMAT fmt, UINT flags) {
    InFlight guard(G().inFlightResize);
    if (!G().faulted.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
        // A running overlay frame briefly holds the back buffer; afterwards no reference of ours remains.
        std::lock_guard lock(G().renderMutex);
    }
    return g_oResizeBuffers(sc, count, w, h, fmt, flags);
}

bool Create(void* target, void* detour, void** original, const char* name, std::string& error) {
    const MH_STATUS st = MH_CreateHook(target, detour, original);
    if (st != MH_OK) {
        error += std::string(name) + ": " + MH_StatusToString(st) + " ";
        return false;
    }
    MH_QueueEnableHook(target);
    RegisterHookTarget(target);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace

bool CreateDxgiHooks(const SwapChainVtbl& vt, std::string& error) {
    bool ok = Create(vt.present, reinterpret_cast<void*>(&HkPresent), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_oPresent), "Present", error);
    ok = Create(vt.resizeBuffers, reinterpret_cast<void*>(&HkResizeBuffers), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_oResizeBuffers),
                "ResizeBuffers", error) && ok;
    if (vt.present1 && vt.present1 != vt.present) {
        Create(vt.present1, reinterpret_cast<void*>(&HkPresent1), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_oPresent1), "Present1", error);
    }
    return ok;
}

}  // namespace acp::hook

```

`src/hook/frame_pacer.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "frame_pacer.h"

#include <windows.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <atomic>
#include <vector>

#ifndef CREATE_WAITABLE_TIMER_HIGH_RESOLUTION
#define CREATE_WAITABLE_TIMER_HIGH_RESOLUTION 0x00000002
#endif

namespace acp::hook::pacing {
namespace {

std::atomic<int> g_fgLimit{0};
std::atomic<int> g_bgLimit{0};

// Present thread only
struct State {
    std::int64_t freq = 0;
    std::int64_t next = 0;       // next frame deadline (QPC)
    std::int64_t lastFrame = 0;  // QPC of the previous Present
    HANDLE timer = nullptr;
    bool timerTried = false;
    Stats stats;
    float window[64] = {};       // for the FPS average
} g;

std::int64_t Qpc() {
    LARGE_INTEGER v;
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&v);
    return v.QuadPart;
}

// Waits until target (QPC): coarsely via a high-resolution timer, the last ~1.5 ms busy-waiting (precise, no stutter).
void WaitUntil(std::int64_t target) {
    if (!g.timerTried) {
        g.timerTried = true;
        g.timer = CreateWaitableTimerExW(nullptr, nullptr, CREATE_WAITABLE_TIMER_HIGH_RESOLUTION, TIMER_ALL_ACCESS);
        if (!g.timer) g.timer = CreateWaitableTimerExW(nullptr, nullptr, 0, TIMER_ALL_ACCESS);  // older Windows versions
    }
    const std::int64_t spin = g.freq * 15 / 10000;  // 1.5 ms
    std::int64_t now = Qpc();
    if (target - now > spin && g.timer) {
        LARGE_INTEGER due;
        due.QuadPart = -static_cast<LONGLONG>((target - now - spin) * 10000000 / g.freq);  // 100 ns units, relative
        if (SetWaitableTimerEx(g.timer, &due, 0, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, 0)) WaitForSingleObject(g.timer, 100);
    }
    while ((now = Qpc()) < target) YieldProcessor();
}

void Record(std::int64_t now) {
    if (g.lastFrame != 0) {
        const float ms = static_cast<float>(static_cast<double>(now - g.lastFrame) * 1000.0 / static_cast<double>(g.freq));
        Stats& s = g.stats;
        const int idx = (s.head + s.count) % kHistory;
        s.history[idx] = ms;
        if (s.count < kHistory) ++s.count;
        else s.head = (s.head + 1) % kHistory;
        s.frameMs = ms;
    }
    g.lastFrame = now;
}

}  // namespace

void SetLimits(int foregroundFps, int backgroundFps) {
    g_fgLimit.store(std::clamp(foregroundFps, 0, 1000), std::memory_order_relaxed);
    g_bgLimit.store(std::clamp(backgroundFps, 0, 1000), std::memory_order_relaxed);
}

void BeforePresent(bool foreground) {
    if (g.freq == 0) {
        LARGE_INTEGER f;
        QueryPerformanceFrequency(&f);
        g.freq = f.QuadPart;
    }
    int limit = foreground ? g_fgLimit.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) : g_bgLimit.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (!foreground && limit == 0) limit = g_fgLimit.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    g.stats.limit = limit;

    if (limit > 0) {
        const std::int64_t period = g.freq / limit;
        const std::int64_t now = Qpc();
        if (g.next == 0 || now - g.next > period * 4 || g.next - now > period * 2) {
            g.next = now + period;  // start, after loading pauses or a limit change: restart the cadence instead of catching up
        } else {
            WaitUntil(g.next);
            const std::int64_t after = Qpc();
            // Stay on cadence; if the frame was late anyway, count from now (no frame bursts to catch up).
            g.next = after - g.next > period ? after + period : g.next + period;
        }
    } else {
        g.next = 0;
    }
    Record(Qpc());
}

void GetStats(Stats& out) {
    Stats& s = g.stats;
    // FPS over the most recent ~0.5 s, average and 1 % low over the whole history
    float sum = 0, recent = 0;
    int recentN = 0;
    std::vector<float> ms;
    ms.reserve(static_cast<std::size_t>(s.count));
    for (int i = s.count - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
        const float v = s.history[(s.head + i) % kHistory];
        sum += v;
        ms.push_back(v);
        if (recent < 500.0f) {
            recent += v;
            ++recentN;
        }
    }
    s.fps = recent > 0 ? 1000.0f * static_cast<float>(recentN) / recent : 0.0f;
    s.avgMs = s.count ? sum / static_cast<float>(s.count) : 0.0f;
    s.low1Fps = OnePercentLowFps(ms.data(), static_cast<int>(ms.size()));
    out = s;
}

float OnePercentLowFps(const float* ms, int n) {
    if (n <= 0) return 0.0f;
    std::vector<float> v(ms, ms + n);
    std::sort(v.begin(), v.end(), [](float a, float b) { return a > b; });  // longest frame times first
    const int k = std::max(1, n / 100);
    float sum = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < k; ++i) sum += v[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)];
    const float avg = sum / static_cast<float>(k);
    return avg > 0 ? 1000.0f / avg : 0.0f;
}

}  // namespace acp::hook::pacing

```

`src/hook/frame_pacer.h`:

```h
#pragma once

// Frame rate limiter and frame time statistics in the Present detour (like RTSS/Special K: wait before the real Present).
// No intervention in the game: only waiting on the render thread. All functions except SetLimits() run only on the
// thread that calls Present.

#include <cstdint>

namespace acp::hook::pacing {

// Any thread: target FPS (0 = off) in the foreground, and when the game is not the active window.
void SetLimits(int foregroundFps, int backgroundFps);

// Present thread, outermost Present call, right before the real Present: waits until the next frame deadline and
// measures the frame time. foreground: game window is active.
void BeforePresent(bool foreground);

constexpr int kHistory = 240;
struct Stats {
    float fps = 0;        // average over approx. 0.5 s
    float frameMs = 0;    // last frame time
    float avgMs = 0;      // average over the history
    float low1Fps = 0;    // 1 % low over the history
    int limit = 0;        // active target (0 = off)
    int count = 0;        // valid entries in history
    int head = 0;         // index of the oldest entry
    float history[kHistory] = {};
};
// Present thread (the overlay draws on the same thread): current state.
void GetStats(Stats& out);

// Pure helper (testable): 1 % low in FPS from frame times in ms.
float OnePercentLowFps(const float* ms, int n);

}  // namespace acp::hook::pacing

```

`src/hook/hooks.h`:

```h
#pragma once

// Shared state and interfaces of the overlay DLL.

#include <windows.h>

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <mutex>
#include <array>
#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "pad_filter.h"
#include "param_table.h"

struct IDXGISwapChain;

namespace acp::hook {

enum class Phase : std::uint8_t { Loading, Running, ShuttingDown, Inert };

struct Globals {
    std::atomic<Phase> phase{Phase::Loading};
    HMODULE module = nullptr;
    HANDLE unloadEvent = nullptr;
    std::atomic<HWND> gameWindow{nullptr};
    std::atomic<bool> overlayOpen{false};
    std::atomic<std::uint32_t> keyToggles{0};  // counted by the window thread, consumed in Present
    std::atomic<std::uint32_t> toggleKey{0x10071};  // VK | Shift<<16 | Ctrl<<17 | Alt<<18 (default Shift+F2)
    std::atomic<std::uint32_t> tpKey{0};            // quick teleport to the marker (0 = off), same format as toggleKey
    std::atomic<std::uint32_t> keyTeleports{0};     // counted by the window thread, consumed in Present
    std::atomic<bool> keyCapture{false};            // panel is capturing a new key: hotkey suspended
    std::atomic<bool> faulted{false};         // exception in Present: overlay permanently off

    std::atomic<int> inFlightPresent{0};
    std::atomic<int> inFlightResize{0};
    std::atomic<int> inFlightWndProc{0};
    std::atomic<int> inFlightXInput{0};
    std::atomic<int> inFlightUplay{0};

    std::mutex renderMutex;           // init, frame, resize, teardown
    std::recursive_mutex ctxMutex;    // every ImGui access (render and window thread)
    std::atomic<bool> imguiReady{false};
    std::atomic<bool> teardownRequested{false};
    std::atomic<bool> teardownDone{false};
};
Globals& G();

// Only sets a flag + event; the lifecycle thread does the work. Safe from any context.
void RequestUnload();

struct InFlight {
    explicit InFlight(std::atomic<int>& c) noexcept : c_(c) { c_.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel); }
    ~InFlight() { c_.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel); }
    InFlight(const InFlight&) = delete;
    InFlight& operator=(const InFlight&) = delete;
    std::atomic<int>& c_;
};

// --- vtable_probe.cpp ---
struct SwapChainVtbl {
    void* present = nullptr;
    void* resizeBuffers = nullptr;
    void* present1 = nullptr;
};
bool ProbeSwapChainVtable(SwapChainVtbl& out, std::string& error);

// --- dx11_hooks.cpp ---
bool CreateDxgiHooks(const SwapChainVtbl& vt, std::string& error);  // MH_CreateHook + MH_QueueEnableHook

// --- overlay.cpp (render thread) ---
void OnPresent(IDXGISwapChain* sc);  // in the Present detour during phase Running
void TeardownRender();               // release ImGui + D3D objects (render thread or lifecycle)

// --- wndproc_hook.cpp ---
bool InstallWndProc(HWND hwnd);
bool RestoreWndProc();  // false if a foreign subclass sits on top of ours

// --- xinput_hook.cpp ---
bool CreateXInputHook(bool anyHost, std::string& info);  // MH_CreateHook + queue; false = no hook (fallback)
bool XInputHookActive();                                 // the game calls the hook regularly
std::uint32_t ConsumePadToggles();
bool ReadPad(input::PadRaw& raw);  // render thread: true if a pad is connected
void SetPadGate(input::PadGate gate);

// --- uplay_hook.cpp (Uplay options channel, live values in a "Hacked Animus" save) ---
enum class UplayMode : std::uint8_t { Off = 0, Log = 1, Inject = 2 };
bool CreateUplayHooks(UplayMode mode, bool anyHost, std::string& info);  // MH_CreateHook + Queue
void UplayOnValues(const std::array<ParamValue, kParamCount>& values);   // I/O thread: queue changed values
void UplayPushAll(const std::array<ParamValue, kParamCount>& values);    // queue all values
// Lifecycle thread: save-game values reported by the game (empty if nothing new)
std::vector<std::pair<ParamId, ParamValue>> UplayTakeGameValues();
bool UplayHooked();
int UplayGameState();  // -1 unknown, 0 not accepting, 1 ACP active
void UplayDrainLog();  // lifecycle thread: write the capture to the log

// --- lifecycle.cpp ---
DWORD WINAPI LifecycleThread(LPVOID);

// Hook targets for the chain check on unload
void RegisterHookTarget(void* target);

}  // namespace acp::hook
```
