`src/hook/postfx.cpp`:

```cpp
// Full-screen pass: copy the back buffer, write it back through the filter shader. All of the game's
// affected pipeline state is saved and restored.
#include "postfx.h"

#include <windows.h>
#include <d3d11.h>
#include <d3dcompiler.h>
#include <dxgi.h>

#include <cstring>

namespace acp::hook {
namespace {

constexpr char kShader[] = R"(
cbuffer P : register(b0) {
    float grain; float vignette; float brightness; float temperature;
    float tint; float saturation; float time; float sharpen;
};
Texture2D src : register(t0);
SamplerState smp : register(s0);
struct VSO { float4 pos : SV_Position; float2 uv : TEXCOORD0; };
VSO vs(uint id : SV_VertexID) {
    VSO o;
    o.uv = float2((id << 1) & 2, id & 2);
    o.pos = float4(o.uv * float2(2, -2) + float2(-1, 1), 0, 1);
    return o;
}
float hash(float2 p) { return frac(sin(dot(p, float2(12.9898, 78.233))) * 43758.5453); }
float3 px(int2 p, int2 maxp) { return src.Load(int3(clamp(p, int2(0, 0), maxp), 0)).rgb; }
float4 ps(VSO i) : SV_Target {
    uint w, h;
    src.GetDimensions(w, h);
    const int2 maxp = int2(w, h) - 1;
    const int2 p = int2(i.pos.xy);
    float3 c = px(p, maxp);
    if (sharpen > 0.0) {
        // Contrast-adaptive sharpening (after AMD FidelityFX CAS): strong on soft edges, weak on hard ones (no halos).
        float3 n = px(p + int2(0, -1), maxp), s = px(p + int2(0, 1), maxp);
        float3 e = px(p + int2(1, 0), maxp), wv = px(p + int2(-1, 0), maxp);
        float3 mn = min(c, min(min(n, s), min(e, wv)));
        float3 mx = max(c, max(max(n, s), max(e, wv)));
        float3 amp = sqrt(saturate(min(mn, 1.0 - mx) / max(mx, 1e-4)));
        float3 wt = amp * (-1.0 / lerp(8.0, 4.5, sharpen));
        c = max((c + (n + s + e + wv) * wt) / (1.0 + 4.0 * wt), 0.0);
    }
    c *= brightness;
    float t = temperature / 100.0;
    c.r *= 1.0 + 0.18 * t;
    c.b *= 1.0 - 0.18 * t;
    float g = tint / 100.0;
    c.g *= 1.0 + 0.12 * g;
    c.rb *= 1.0 - 0.06 * g;
    float l = dot(c, float3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
    c = lerp(l.xxx, c, max(0.0, 1.0 + saturation / 100.0));
    float2 d = i.uv - 0.5;
    c *= saturate(1.0 - dot(d, d) * vignette * 2.2);
    float n = hash(i.uv * 1024.0 + time);
    c += (n - 0.5) * grain * 0.18;
    return float4(c, 1.0);
}
)";

struct Resources {
    ID3D11Device* device = nullptr;  // no reference held, for comparison only
    ID3D11VertexShader* vs = nullptr;
    ID3D11PixelShader* ps = nullptr;
    ID3D11Buffer* cb = nullptr;
    ID3D11SamplerState* sampler = nullptr;
    ID3D11RasterizerState* rs = nullptr;
    ID3D11BlendState* blend = nullptr;
    ID3D11DepthStencilState* ds = nullptr;
    ID3D11Texture2D* copy = nullptr;
    ID3D11ShaderResourceView* copySrv = nullptr;
    D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC copyDesc{};
    bool failed = false;
} g;

template <typename T>
void SafeRelease(T*& p) {
    if (p) {
        p->Release();
        p = nullptr;
    }
}

DXGI_FORMAT TypedFormat(DXGI_FORMAT f) {
    switch (f) {
        case DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_TYPELESS: return DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM;
        case DXGI_FORMAT_B8G8R8A8_TYPELESS: return DXGI_FORMAT_B8G8R8A8_UNORM;
        case DXGI_FORMAT_R10G10B10A2_TYPELESS: return DXGI_FORMAT_R10G10B10A2_UNORM;
        case DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_TYPELESS: return DXGI_FORMAT_R16G16B16A16_FLOAT;
        default: return f;
    }
}

bool CreateStatic(ID3D11Device* dev) {
    ID3DBlob* vsb = nullptr;
    ID3DBlob* psb = nullptr;
    ID3DBlob* err = nullptr;
    bool ok = SUCCEEDED(D3DCompile(kShader, sizeof(kShader) - 1, "acp_postfx", nullptr, nullptr, "vs", "vs_4_0", 0, 0, &vsb, &err));
    SafeRelease(err);
    ok = ok && SUCCEEDED(D3DCompile(kShader, sizeof(kShader) - 1, "acp_postfx", nullptr, nullptr, "ps", "ps_4_0", 0, 0, &psb, &err));
    SafeRelease(err);
    ok = ok && SUCCEEDED(dev->CreateVertexShader(vsb->GetBufferPointer(), vsb->GetBufferSize(), nullptr, &g.vs));
    ok = ok && SUCCEEDED(dev->CreatePixelShader(psb->GetBufferPointer(), psb->GetBufferSize(), nullptr, &g.ps));
    SafeRelease(vsb);
    SafeRelease(psb);

    D3D11_BUFFER_DESC bd{};
    bd.ByteWidth = 32;
    bd.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DYNAMIC;
    bd.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_CONSTANT_BUFFER;
    bd.CPUAccessFlags = D3D11_CPU_ACCESS_WRITE;
    ok = ok && SUCCEEDED(dev->CreateBuffer(&bd, nullptr, &g.cb));

    D3D11_SAMPLER_DESC sd{};
    sd.Filter = D3D11_FILTER_MIN_MAG_MIP_POINT;
    sd.AddressU = sd.AddressV = sd.AddressW = D3D11_TEXTURE_ADDRESS_CLAMP;
    sd.MaxLOD = D3D11_FLOAT32_MAX;
    ok = ok && SUCCEEDED(dev->CreateSamplerState(&sd, &g.sampler));

    D3D11_RASTERIZER_DESC rd{};
    rd.FillMode = D3D11_FILL_SOLID;
    rd.CullMode = D3D11_CULL_NONE;
    rd.DepthClipEnable = TRUE;
    ok = ok && SUCCEEDED(dev->CreateRasterizerState(&rd, &g.rs));

    D3D11_BLEND_DESC bld{};
    bld.RenderTarget[0].BlendEnable = FALSE;
    bld.RenderTarget[0].RenderTargetWriteMask = D3D11_COLOR_WRITE_ENABLE_RED | D3D11_COLOR_WRITE_ENABLE_GREEN |
                                                D3D11_COLOR_WRITE_ENABLE_BLUE;
    ok = ok && SUCCEEDED(dev->CreateBlendState(&bld, &g.blend));

    D3D11_DEPTH_STENCIL_DESC dsd{};
    dsd.DepthEnable = FALSE;
    dsd.StencilEnable = FALSE;
    ok = ok && SUCCEEDED(dev->CreateDepthStencilState(&dsd, &g.ds));
    return ok;
}

bool EnsureCopy(ID3D11Device* dev, const D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC& bb) {
    if (g.copy && g.copyDesc.Width == bb.Width && g.copyDesc.Height == bb.Height && g.copyDesc.Format == bb.Format) return true;
    SafeRelease(g.copySrv);
    SafeRelease(g.copy);
    D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC td{};
    td.Width = bb.Width;
    td.Height = bb.Height;
    td.MipLevels = 1;
    td.ArraySize = 1;
    td.Format = bb.Format;
    td.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
    td.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_DEFAULT;
    td.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_SHADER_RESOURCE;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateTexture2D(&td, nullptr, &g.copy))) return false;
    D3D11_SHADER_RESOURCE_VIEW_DESC sv{};
    sv.Format = TypedFormat(bb.Format);
    sv.ViewDimension = D3D11_SRV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
    sv.Texture2D.MipLevels = 1;
    if (FAILED(dev->CreateShaderResourceView(g.copy, &sv, &g.copySrv))) {
        SafeRelease(g.copy);
        return false;
    }
    g.copyDesc = td;
    return true;
}

// Backup of the pipeline state that the pass changes.
struct StateBackup {
    UINT viewportCount = D3D11_VIEWPORT_AND_SCISSORRECT_OBJECT_COUNT_PER_PIPELINE;
    D3D11_VIEWPORT viewports[D3D11_VIEWPORT_AND_SCISSORRECT_OBJECT_COUNT_PER_PIPELINE]{};
    ID3D11RasterizerState* rs = nullptr;
    ID3D11BlendState* blend = nullptr;
    FLOAT blendFactor[4]{};
    UINT sampleMask = 0;
    ID3D11DepthStencilState* ds = nullptr;
    UINT stencilRef = 0;
    ID3D11ShaderResourceView* psSrv = nullptr;
    ID3D11SamplerState* psSampler = nullptr;
    ID3D11Buffer* psCb = nullptr;
    ID3D11PixelShader* ps = nullptr;
    ID3D11ClassInstance* psInst[256]{};
    UINT psInstCount = 256;
    ID3D11VertexShader* vs = nullptr;
    ID3D11ClassInstance* vsInst[256]{};
    UINT vsInstCount = 256;
    ID3D11GeometryShader* gs = nullptr;
    ID3D11HullShader* hs = nullptr;
    ID3D11DomainShader* dsh = nullptr;
    D3D11_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY topology{};
    ID3D11InputLayout* layout = nullptr;
    ID3D11Buffer* vb = nullptr;
    UINT vbStride = 0, vbOffset = 0;
    ID3D11Buffer* ib = nullptr;
    DXGI_FORMAT ibFormat{};
    UINT ibOffset = 0;
    ID3D11RenderTargetView* rtvs[D3D11_SIMULTANEOUS_RENDER_TARGET_COUNT]{};
    ID3D11DepthStencilView* dsv = nullptr;

    void Save(ID3D11DeviceContext* c) {
        c->RSGetViewports(&viewportCount, viewports);
        c->RSGetState(&rs);
        c->OMGetBlendState(&blend, blendFactor, &sampleMask);
        c->OMGetDepthStencilState(&ds, &stencilRef);
        c->PSGetShaderResources(0, 1, &psSrv);
        c->PSGetSamplers(0, 1, &psSampler);
        c->PSGetConstantBuffers(0, 1, &psCb);
        c->PSGetShader(&ps, psInst, &psInstCount);
        c->VSGetShader(&vs, vsInst, &vsInstCount);
        c->GSGetShader(&gs, nullptr, nullptr);
        c->HSGetShader(&hs, nullptr, nullptr);
        c->DSGetShader(&dsh, nullptr, nullptr);
        c->IAGetPrimitiveTopology(&topology);
        c->IAGetInputLayout(&layout);
        c->IAGetVertexBuffers(0, 1, &vb, &vbStride, &vbOffset);
        c->IAGetIndexBuffer(&ib, &ibFormat, &ibOffset);
        c->OMGetRenderTargets(D3D11_SIMULTANEOUS_RENDER_TARGET_COUNT, rtvs, &dsv);
    }

    void Restore(ID3D11DeviceContext* c) {
        c->RSSetViewports(viewportCount, viewports);
        c->RSSetState(rs);
        c->OMSetBlendState(blend, blendFactor, sampleMask);
        c->OMSetDepthStencilState(ds, stencilRef);
        c->PSSetShaderResources(0, 1, &psSrv);
        c->PSSetSamplers(0, 1, &psSampler);
        c->PSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, &psCb);
        c->PSSetShader(ps, psInst, psInstCount);
        c->VSSetShader(vs, vsInst, vsInstCount);
        c->GSSetShader(gs, nullptr, 0);
        c->HSSetShader(hs, nullptr, 0);
        c->DSSetShader(dsh, nullptr, 0);
        c->IASetPrimitiveTopology(topology);
        c->IASetInputLayout(layout);
        c->IASetVertexBuffers(0, 1, &vb, &vbStride, &vbOffset);
        c->IASetIndexBuffer(ib, ibFormat, ibOffset);
        c->OMSetRenderTargets(D3D11_SIMULTANEOUS_RENDER_TARGET_COUNT, rtvs, dsv);
        SafeRelease(rs);
        SafeRelease(blend);
        SafeRelease(ds);
        SafeRelease(psSrv);
        SafeRelease(psSampler);
        SafeRelease(psCb);
        SafeRelease(ps);
        for (UINT k = 0; k < psInstCount; ++k) SafeRelease(psInst[k]);
        SafeRelease(vs);
        for (UINT k = 0; k < vsInstCount; ++k) SafeRelease(vsInst[k]);
        SafeRelease(gs);
        SafeRelease(hs);
        SafeRelease(dsh);
        SafeRelease(layout);
        SafeRelease(vb);
        SafeRelease(ib);
        for (auto*& r : rtvs) SafeRelease(r);
        SafeRelease(dsv);
    }
};

}  // namespace

bool ApplyPostFx(IDXGISwapChain* sc, ID3D11Device* dev, ID3D11DeviceContext* ctx, const PostFxParams& p) {
    if (g.failed) return false;
    if (g.device != dev) {
        ReleasePostFx();
        g.device = dev;
        if (!CreateStatic(dev)) {
            ReleasePostFx();
            g.failed = true;  // e.g. shader compiler unavailable: filter permanently off
            return false;
        }
    }
    ID3D11Texture2D* back = nullptr;
    if (FAILED(sc->GetBuffer(0, __uuidof(ID3D11Texture2D), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&back))) || !back) return false;
    D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC bd{};
    back->GetDesc(&bd);
    bool ok = bd.SampleDesc.Count == 1 && EnsureCopy(dev, bd);
    ID3D11RenderTargetView* rtv = nullptr;
    if (ok) {
        D3D11_RENDER_TARGET_VIEW_DESC rd{};
        rd.Format = TypedFormat(bd.Format);
        rd.ViewDimension = D3D11_RTV_DIMENSION_TEXTURE2D;
        ok = SUCCEEDED(dev->CreateRenderTargetView(back, &rd, &rtv));
    }
    if (ok) {
        D3D11_MAPPED_SUBRESOURCE m{};
        if (SUCCEEDED(ctx->Map(g.cb, 0, D3D11_MAP_WRITE_DISCARD, 0, &m))) {
            const float data[8] = {p.grain, p.vignette, p.brightness, p.temperature, p.tint, p.saturation,
                                   static_cast<float>(GetTickCount64() % 100000) * 0.001f, p.sharpen};
            std::memcpy(m.pData, data, sizeof(data));
            ctx->Unmap(g.cb, 0);
        }
        StateBackup st;
        st.Save(ctx);

        ctx->CopyResource(g.copy, back);
        D3D11_VIEWPORT vp{0, 0, static_cast<float>(bd.Width), static_cast<float>(bd.Height), 0, 1};
        ctx->RSSetViewports(1, &vp);
        ctx->RSSetState(g.rs);
        const float bf[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};
        ctx->OMSetBlendState(g.blend, bf, 0xffffffff);
        ctx->OMSetDepthStencilState(g.ds, 0);
        ctx->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &rtv, nullptr);
        ctx->IASetPrimitiveTopology(D3D11_PRIMITIVE_TOPOLOGY_TRIANGLELIST);
        ctx->IASetInputLayout(nullptr);
        ID3D11Buffer* noVb = nullptr;
        const UINT zero = 0;
        ctx->IASetVertexBuffers(0, 1, &noVb, &zero, &zero);
        ctx->VSSetShader(g.vs, nullptr, 0);
        ctx->GSSetShader(nullptr, nullptr, 0);
        ctx->HSSetShader(nullptr, nullptr, 0);
        ctx->DSSetShader(nullptr, nullptr, 0);
        ctx->PSSetShader(g.ps, nullptr, 0);
        ctx->PSSetConstantBuffers(0, 1, &g.cb);
        ctx->PSSetShaderResources(0, 1, &g.copySrv);
        ctx->PSSetSamplers(0, 1, &g.sampler);
        ctx->Draw(3, 0);
        ID3D11ShaderResourceView* noSrv = nullptr;
        ctx->PSSetShaderResources(0, 1, &noSrv);

        st.Restore(ctx);
    }
    if (rtv) rtv->Release();
    back->Release();  // no back buffer reference beyond the frame
    return ok;
}

void ReleasePostFx() {
    SafeRelease(g.copySrv);
    SafeRelease(g.copy);
    SafeRelease(g.vs);
    SafeRelease(g.ps);
    SafeRelease(g.cb);
    SafeRelease(g.sampler);
    SafeRelease(g.rs);
    SafeRelease(g.blend);
    SafeRelease(g.ds);
    g.device = nullptr;
    g.copyDesc = {};
}

}  // namespace acp::hook

```

`src/hook/postfx.h`:

```h
#pragma once

// Own image filter (replaces the PostProcessCustom* values, which have no effect in the game).
// Use only on the render thread under renderMutex.

struct IDXGISwapChain;
struct ID3D11Device;
struct ID3D11DeviceContext;

namespace acp::hook {

struct PostFxParams {
    float grain = 0;        // 0..1
    float vignette = 0;     // 0..1
    float brightness = 1;   // 0.25..4
    float temperature = 0;  // -100..100
    float tint = 0;         // -100..100
    float saturation = 0;   // -100..100
    float sharpen = 0;      // 0..1 (contrast-adaptive sharpening)
    bool IsNeutral() const {
        return grain == 0 && vignette == 0 && brightness == 1 && temperature == 0 && tint == 0 && saturation == 0 && sharpen == 0;
    }
};

// Applies the filter to the current back buffer. false on error (the image then stays unchanged).
bool ApplyPostFx(IDXGISwapChain* sc, ID3D11Device* dev, ID3D11DeviceContext* ctx, const PostFxParams& p);
// Releases all of our D3D objects (teardown/device change).
void ReleasePostFx();

}  // namespace acp::hook

```

`src/hook/uplay_hook.cpp`:

```cpp
// Uplay options channel (analysis round 3): hooks on exports of uplay_r1_loader64.dll.
//  - UPLAY_OPTIONS_SetInGameState: pass-through; remembers whether the game accepts ACP (1 = Hacked Animus + in game)
//  - UPLAY_OPTIONS_Enumerate:      pass-through; capture only
//  - UPLAY_GetNextEvent:           pass-through; when ACP is accepted, delivers pending values as event 80000
// Detours: no allocation, no file I/O, no logger (ring buffer only), never blocking.
#include <windows.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <atomic>
#include <charconv>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <mutex>
#include <cmath>
#include <optional>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "MinHook.h"
#include "ini_document.h"
#include "hooks.h"
#include "game_bridge.h"
#include "platform.h"

namespace acp::hook {
namespace {

// --- Structures from static analysis (private notes, not included) ---
struct UplayEvent {
    std::uint32_t type;
    std::uint32_t pad;
    void* data;
};
struct KeyValue {
    const char* key;
    const char* value;
};
struct KeyValueList {
    std::uint32_t count;
    std::uint32_t pad;
    KeyValue** items;
};
struct OptionsEvent {
    std::uint64_t unknown0;  // not read by the game
    KeyValueList* list;
};
constexpr std::uint32_t kEventOptions = 80000;

using GetNextEvent_t = bool (*)(UplayEvent*);
using SetInGameState_t = int (*)(std::uint32_t);
using Enumerate_t = int (*)(std::uint32_t, void**, void*);
using Set_t = int (*)(KeyValueList*, const char*, const char*);
using Apply_t = int (*)(std::uint32_t, KeyValueList*, void*);
using Release_t = int (*)(KeyValueList*);
using HasOverlapped_t = bool (*)(void*);

GetNextEvent_t g_oGetNextEvent = nullptr;
SetInGameState_t g_oSetInGameState = nullptr;
Enumerate_t g_oEnumerate = nullptr;
Set_t g_oSet = nullptr;
Apply_t g_oApply = nullptr;
Release_t g_oRelease = nullptr;
HasOverlapped_t g_hasOverlapped = nullptr;  // not hooked, only for the self-test

// --- Reverse direction (stage c): the game reports its save-game values via Enumerate/Set/Apply ---
std::atomic<bool> g_emulate{false};     // Set/Apply/Release hooks active
std::atomic<bool> g_ourListOut{false};  // our list is currently with the game
KeyValueList g_ourList{};               // placeholder list (the game does not read it)
std::mutex g_captureMutex;              // leaf mutex: work and result buffers
std::array<std::optional<ParamValue>, kParamCount> g_working{};
std::array<std::optional<ParamValue>, kParamCount> g_captured{};
std::atomic<bool> g_captureReady{false};

std::atomic<UplayMode> g_mode{UplayMode::Off};
std::atomic<bool> g_hooked{false};
std::atomic<int> g_gameState{-1};  // -1 unknown, 0 not accepting, 1 ACP active

// --- Pending values (leaf mutex; detour uses try_lock only) ---
std::mutex g_pendingMutex;
std::array<std::optional<ParamValue>, kParamCount> g_pending{};
std::atomic<bool> g_hasPending{false};

// Under g_pendingMutex: last seen values (basis for "send only changes") and values reported by the
// game that have not yet arrived in the snapshot (do not send old values back).
std::array<ParamValue, kParamCount> g_lastValues{};
bool g_baselineSet = false;
std::array<std::optional<ParamValue>, kParamCount> g_fromGame{};
std::uint64_t g_fromGameUntil = 0;

// --- Event packets: fixed, rotated, never freed (the game copies them immediately) ---
struct Packet {
    OptionsEvent ev;
    KeyValueList list;
    std::array<KeyValue, kParamCount> kv;
    std::array<KeyValue*, kParamCount> ptrs;
    std::array<std::array<char, 32>, kParamCount> values;
};
std::array<Packet, 4> g_packets{};
std::atomic<unsigned> g_packetIdx{0};

// --- Capture ring (detours write, lifecycle thread reads) ---
enum class Rec : std::uint32_t { EventHit = 1, SetInGameState, Enumerate, Injected, EnumEmulated, ApplyCaptured };
struct LogRec {
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> seq{0};
    Rec kind{};
    std::uint32_t tid = 0;
    std::uint64_t a = 0, b = 0, tick = 0;
};
constexpr std::size_t kRing = 512;
std::array<LogRec, kRing> g_ring;
std::atomic<std::uint64_t> g_head{0};
std::uint64_t g_tail = 0;  // lifecycle thread only

std::atomic<std::uint64_t> g_calls{0}, g_hits{0}, g_injected{0};

void Record(Rec kind, std::uint64_t a, std::uint64_t b) noexcept {
    const std::uint64_t idx = g_head.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    LogRec& r = g_ring[idx % kRing];
    r.kind = kind;
    r.tid = GetCurrentThreadId();
    r.a = a;
    r.b = b;
    r.tick = GetTickCount64();
    r.seq.store(idx + 1, std::memory_order_release);
}

// Value in the game's format without allocation (strings, "true"/"false", dot as decimal separator).
void FormatInto(const ParamValue& v, std::array<char, 32>& out) noexcept {
    char* b = out.data();
    char* e = b + out.size() - 1;
    char* p = b;
    switch (v.type) {
        case ParamType::Bool: {
            const char* s = v.b ? "true" : "false";
            const std::size_t n = std::strlen(s);
            std::memcpy(b, s, n);
            p = b + n;
            break;
        }
        case ParamType::Int: p = std::to_chars(b, e, v.i).ptr; break;
        case ParamType::Float: {
            p = std::to_chars(b, e, v.f == 0.0f ? 0.0f : v.f, std::chars_format::fixed, 4).ptr;
            char* dot = std::find(b, p, '.');
            if (dot != p) {
                while (p > dot && p[-1] == '0') --p;
                if (p == dot + 1) p = dot;
            }
            break;
        }
    }
    *p = '\0';
}

bool HkGetNextEvent(UplayEvent* ev) {
    InFlight guard(G().inFlightUplay);
    const bool r = g_oGetNextEvent(ev);
    if (!ev || G().phase.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != Phase::Running) return r;
    g_calls.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (r) {
        g_hits.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        Record(Rec::EventHit, ev->type, reinterpret_cast<std::uint64_t>(ev->data));
        return r;
    }
    game::OnPreStep(g_gameState.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 1);  // game task PreStep: the only place for game access
    if (g_mode.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) != UplayMode::Inject || g_gameState.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != 1 ||
        !g_hasPending.load(std::memory_order_acquire))
        return r;

    std::unique_lock lock(g_pendingMutex, std::try_to_lock);
    if (!lock.owns_lock()) return r;
    Packet& pk = g_packets[g_packetIdx.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed) % g_packets.size()];
    std::uint32_t n = 0;
    std::uint64_t firstKey = 0;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < kParamCount; ++i) {
        if (!g_pending[i]) continue;
        const ParamDesc& d = ParamTable()[i];
        if (!d.readOnly && !d.local) {
            if (n == 0) firstKey = i;
            FormatInto(*g_pending[i], pk.values[n]);
            pk.kv[n].key = d.key.data();  // literal from the table, null-terminated
            pk.kv[n].value = pk.values[n].data();
            pk.ptrs[n] = &pk.kv[n];
            ++n;
        }
        g_pending[i].reset();
    }
    g_hasPending.store(false, std::memory_order_release);
    lock.unlock();
    if (n == 0) return r;

    pk.list.count = n;
    pk.list.pad = 0;
    pk.list.items = pk.ptrs.data();
    pk.ev.unknown0 = 0;
    pk.ev.list = &pk.list;
    ev->type = kEventOptions;
    ev->pad = 0;
    ev->data = &pk.ev;
    g_injected.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    Record(Rec::Injected, n, firstKey);
    return true;
}

int HkSetInGameState(std::uint32_t flags) {
    InFlight guard(G().inFlightUplay);
    g_gameState.store(flags ? 1 : 0, std::memory_order_release);
    Record(Rec::SetInGameState, flags, 0);
    return g_oSetInGameState(flags);
}

// Mark the overlapped as completed (layout: +4 bytes "done", +8 result) and cross-check it with the real
// client function so that the game's state machine never gets stuck.
bool CompleteOverlapped(void* ovl) noexcept {
    auto* b = static_cast<unsigned char*>(ovl);
    b[4] = 1;
    *reinterpret_cast<std::uint32_t*>(b + 8) = 0;
    return g_hasOverlapped ? g_hasOverlapped(ovl) : false;
}

int HkEnumerate(std::uint32_t handle, void** outList, void* overlapped) {
    InFlight guard(G().inFlightUplay);
    const int r = g_oEnumerate(handle, outList, overlapped);
    Record(Rec::Enumerate, handle, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(static_cast<std::uint32_t>(r)));
    // Only if the client does not deliver (anymore): emulate the reverse direction.
    if (r != 0 || !outList || !overlapped || !g_emulate.load() || G().phase.load() != Phase::Running ||
        g_mode.load() != UplayMode::Inject)
        return r;
    if (g_ourListOut.exchange(true)) return r;  // a list is already out
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(g_captureMutex);
        g_working = {};
    }
    g_ourList = {};
    if (!CompleteOverlapped(overlapped)) {
        g_ourListOut = false;
        return r;  // self-test failed: behave as without the hook
    }
    *outList = &g_ourList;
    Record(Rec::EnumEmulated, handle, 0);
    return 1;
}

int HkSet(KeyValueList* list, const char* key, const char* value) {
    InFlight guard(G().inFlightUplay);
    if (list != &g_ourList || !g_ourListOut.load()) return g_oSet(list, key, value);
    if (key && value) {
        if (const auto id = FindParam(key)) {
            if (const auto v = IniDocument::ParseValue(Desc(*id).type, value)) {
                std::lock_guard lock(g_captureMutex);
                g_working[static_cast<std::size_t>(*id)] = *v;
            }
        }
    }
    return 1;
}

int HkApply(std::uint32_t handle, KeyValueList* list, void* overlapped) {
    InFlight guard(G().inFlightUplay);
    if (list != &g_ourList || !g_ourListOut.load()) return g_oApply(handle, list, overlapped);
    std::uint64_t n = 0;
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(g_captureMutex);
        g_captured = g_working;
        for (const auto& v : g_captured) n += v.has_value();
    }
    g_captureReady.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    if (overlapped) CompleteOverlapped(overlapped);
    Record(Rec::ApplyCaptured, n, 0);
    return 1;
}

int HkRelease(KeyValueList* list) {
    InFlight guard(G().inFlightUplay);
    if (list == &g_ourList) {
        g_ourListOut = false;
        return 1;
    }
    return g_oRelease(list);
}

template <std::size_t N>
bool PrologueIs(void* fn, const std::array<unsigned char, N>& bytes) {
    return std::memcmp(fn, bytes.data(), N) == 0;
}

template <std::size_t N>
bool HookExport(HMODULE m, const char* name, void* detour, void** original, const std::array<unsigned char, N>& prologue,
                std::string& info) {
    void* fn = reinterpret_cast<void*>(GetProcAddress(m, name));
    if (!fn) {
        info += std::string(name) + " missing; ";
        return false;
    }
    if (!PrologueIs(fn, prologue)) {
        info += std::string(name) + ": unexpected prologue (already hooked or different build); ";
        return false;
    }
    if (MH_CreateHook(fn, detour, original) != MH_OK) {
        info += std::string(name) + ": MH_CreateHook failed; ";
        return false;
    }
    MH_QueueEnableHook(fn);
    RegisterHookTarget(fn);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace

bool CreateUplayHooks(UplayMode mode, bool anyHost, std::string& info) {
    g_mode = mode;
    if (mode == UplayMode::Off) {
        info = "Uplay channel: off";
        return false;
    }
    HMODULE m = nullptr;
    if (!GetModuleHandleExW(0, L"uplay_r1_loader64.dll", &m) || !m) {
        info = "Uplay channel: uplay_r1_loader64.dll not loaded";
        return false;
    }
    wchar_t path[MAX_PATH]{};
    GetModuleFileNameW(m, path, MAX_PATH);
    const auto st = platform::StatFile(path);
    if (!anyHost && (!st || st->size != 416768ull)) {
        info = "Uplay channel: unknown uplay_r1_loader64.dll (size " + std::to_string(st ? st->size : 0) + ") - no hook";
        return false;
    }
    std::string err;
    static constexpr std::array<unsigned char, 6> kPrologA{0x40, 0x53, 0x48, 0x83, 0xEC, 0x20};
    static constexpr std::array<unsigned char, 5> kPrologB{0x48, 0x89, 0x5C, 0x24, 0x08};
    const bool a = HookExport(m, "UPLAY_OPTIONS_SetInGameState", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&HkSetInGameState),
                              reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_oSetInGameState), kPrologA, err);
    const bool b = HookExport(m, "UPLAY_GetNextEvent", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&HkGetNextEvent),
                              reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_oGetNextEvent), kPrologA, err);
    HookExport(m, "UPLAY_OPTIONS_Enumerate", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&HkEnumerate), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_oEnumerate),
               kPrologB, err);
    g_hasOverlapped = reinterpret_cast<HasOverlapped_t>(GetProcAddress(m, "UPLAY_HasOverlappedOperationCompleted"));
    const bool c = HookExport(m, "UPLAY_OPTIONS_Set", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&HkSet), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_oSet),
                              kPrologB, err);
    const bool d = HookExport(m, "UPLAY_OPTIONS_Apply", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&HkApply),
                              reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_oApply), kPrologB, err);
    const bool e = HookExport(m, "UPLAY_OPTIONS_ReleaseKeyValueList", reinterpret_cast<void*>(&HkRelease),
                              reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_oRelease), kPrologA, err);
    g_emulate = c && d && e && g_oEnumerate && g_hasOverlapped;
    g_hooked = a && b;
    info = std::string("Uplay channel: ") + (g_hooked ? (mode == UplayMode::Inject ? (g_emulate ? "hooks active (injection + save-game values)" : "hooks active (injection on)") : "hooks active (capture only)")
                                                    : "not available") +
           (err.empty() ? "" : " - " + err);
    return g_hooked;
}

void UplayOnValues(const std::array<ParamValue, kParamCount>& values) {
    std::lock_guard lock(g_pendingMutex);
    if (!g_baselineSet) {
        g_lastValues = values;
        g_baselineSet = true;
        return;
    }
    const bool fromGameActive = GetTickCount64() < g_fromGameUntil;
    bool any = false;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < kParamCount; ++i) {
        if (g_fromGame[i]) {
            // Value reported by the game: wait until it arrives in the snapshot; do not send old values back.
            if (values[i] == *g_fromGame[i] || !fromGameActive) g_fromGame[i].reset();
            else continue;
        }
        if (!(values[i] == g_lastValues[i])) {
            g_pending[i] = values[i];
            any = true;
        }
        g_lastValues[i] = values[i];
    }
    if (any) g_hasPending.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
}

std::vector<std::pair<ParamId, ParamValue>> UplayTakeGameValues() {
    std::vector<std::pair<ParamId, ParamValue>> out;
    if (!g_captureReady.exchange(false, std::memory_order_acq_rel)) return out;
    std::array<std::optional<ParamValue>, kParamCount> cap;
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(g_captureMutex);
        cap = g_captured;
    }
    std::lock_guard lock(g_pendingMutex);
    g_fromGameUntil = GetTickCount64() + 3000;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < kParamCount; ++i) {
        if (!cap[i] || ParamTable()[i].readOnly) continue;
        ParamValue v = *cap[i];
        // The game reports decimals truncated ("%d"): keep our own value if it matches.
        if (v.type == ParamType::Float && g_baselineSet && std::trunc(g_lastValues[i].f) == v.f) v = g_lastValues[i];
        g_pending[i].reset();
        g_fromGame[i] = v;
        g_lastValues[i] = v;
        out.emplace_back(static_cast<ParamId>(i), v);
    }
    return out;
}

void UplayPushAll(const std::array<ParamValue, kParamCount>& values) {
    std::lock_guard lock(g_pendingMutex);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < kParamCount; ++i) g_pending[i] = values[i];
    g_hasPending.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
}

bool UplayHooked() { return g_hooked.load(); }
int UplayGameState() { return g_gameState.load(); }

void UplayDrainLog() {
    static std::uint64_t nextStats = GetTickCount64() + 10000;
    for (;;) {
        const std::uint64_t head = g_head.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
        if (g_tail >= head) break;
        if (head - g_tail > kRing) g_tail = head - kRing;  // overrun: discard older entries
        LogRec& r = g_ring[g_tail % kRing];
        if (r.seq.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != g_tail + 1) break;  // not completely written yet
        char line[160];
        switch (r.kind) {
            case Rec::EventHit:
                std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "UPLAY tid=%u GetNextEvent -> type=%llu data=%p", r.tid,
                              static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.a), reinterpret_cast<void*>(r.b));
                break;
            case Rec::SetInGameState:
                std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "UPLAY tid=%u SetInGameState(%llu)%s", r.tid,
                              static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.a), r.a ? "  -> ACP active (Hacked Animus, in game)" : "");
                break;
            case Rec::Enumerate:
                std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "UPLAY tid=%u Enumerate(handle=%llu) ret=%llu", r.tid,
                              static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.a), static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.b));
                break;
            case Rec::Injected:
                std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "UPLAY tid=%u event 80000 injected: %llu value(s), first %.*s", r.tid,
                              static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.a), static_cast<int>(ParamTable()[r.b].key.size()),
                              ParamTable()[r.b].key.data());
                break;
            case Rec::EnumEmulated:
                std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "UPLAY tid=%u Enumerate emulated (game reports its save-game values)", r.tid);
                break;
            case Rec::ApplyCaptured:
                std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "UPLAY tid=%u Apply: %llu values received from the game", r.tid,
                              static_cast<unsigned long long>(r.a));
                break;
            default: std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "UPLAY ?"); break;
        }
        platform::log::Write(line);
        ++g_tail;
    }
    const std::uint64_t now = GetTickCount64();
    if (g_hooked && now >= nextStats) {
        nextStats = now + 60000;
        char line[160];
        std::snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "UPLAY stats: GetNextEvent %llu calls, %llu events, %llu injected, state %d",
                      static_cast<unsigned long long>(g_calls.load()), static_cast<unsigned long long>(g_hits.load()),
                      static_cast<unsigned long long>(g_injected.load()), g_gameState.load());
        platform::log::Write(line);
    }
}

}  // namespace acp::hook

```

`src/hook/vtable_probe.cpp`:

```cpp
// Determines the addresses of IDXGISwapChain::Present/ResizeBuffers/Present1 via a
// dummy swap chain (replaces Kiero). Runs on the lifecycle thread.
#include <d3d11.h>
#include <dxgi1_2.h>

#include "hooks.h"

namespace acp::hook {

bool ProbeSwapChainVtable(SwapChainVtbl& out, std::string& error) {
    const HINSTANCE inst = G().module;
    WNDCLASSEXW wc{};
    wc.cbSize = sizeof(wc);
    wc.lpfnWndProc = DefWindowProcW;
    wc.hInstance = inst;
    wc.lpszClassName = L"ACPReloadedProbeWindow";
    RegisterClassExW(&wc);
    HWND hwnd = CreateWindowExW(0, wc.lpszClassName, L"", WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW, 0, 0, 16, 16, nullptr, nullptr, inst, nullptr);
    if (!hwnd) {
        error = "Probe window: " + std::to_string(GetLastError());
        UnregisterClassW(wc.lpszClassName, inst);
        return false;
    }

    DXGI_SWAP_CHAIN_DESC sd{};
    sd.BufferCount = 1;
    sd.BufferDesc.Width = 16;
    sd.BufferDesc.Height = 16;
    sd.BufferDesc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM;
    sd.BufferUsage = DXGI_USAGE_RENDER_TARGET_OUTPUT;
    sd.OutputWindow = hwnd;
    sd.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
    sd.Windowed = TRUE;
    sd.SwapEffect = DXGI_SWAP_EFFECT_DISCARD;

    IDXGISwapChain* sc = nullptr;
    ID3D11Device* dev = nullptr;
    ID3D11DeviceContext* ctx = nullptr;
    const D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL levels[] = {D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL_11_0, D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL_10_0};
    HRESULT hr = D3D11CreateDeviceAndSwapChain(nullptr, D3D_DRIVER_TYPE_HARDWARE, nullptr, 0, levels, 2, D3D11_SDK_VERSION,
                                               &sd, &sc, &dev, nullptr, &ctx);
    if (FAILED(hr)) {
        hr = D3D11CreateDeviceAndSwapChain(nullptr, D3D_DRIVER_TYPE_WARP, nullptr, 0, levels, 2, D3D11_SDK_VERSION, &sd,
                                           &sc, &dev, nullptr, &ctx);
    }
    if (SUCCEEDED(hr) && sc) {
        void** vtbl = *reinterpret_cast<void***>(sc);
        out.present = vtbl[8];         // IDXGISwapChain::Present
        out.resizeBuffers = vtbl[13];  // IDXGISwapChain::ResizeBuffers
        IDXGISwapChain1* sc1 = nullptr;
        if (SUCCEEDED(sc->QueryInterface(__uuidof(IDXGISwapChain1), reinterpret_cast<void**>(&sc1))) && sc1) {
            out.present1 = (*reinterpret_cast<void***>(sc1))[22];  // IDXGISwapChain1::Present1
            sc1->Release();
        }
    } else {
        error = "D3D11CreateDeviceAndSwapChain: 0x" + std::to_string(static_cast<unsigned long>(hr));
    }
    if (sc) sc->Release();
    if (ctx) ctx->Release();
    if (dev) dev->Release();
    DestroyWindow(hwnd);
    UnregisterClassW(wc.lpszClassName, inst);
    return out.present && out.resizeBuffers;
}

}  // namespace acp::hook

```

`src/hook/wndproc_hook.cpp`:

```cpp
// Subclassing of the game window: hotkeys (configurable, default Shift+F2; End = unload) and input blocking while the overlay is open.
#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <mutex>

#include "hooks.h"
#include "imgui.h"
#include "imgui_impl_win32.h"

extern IMGUI_IMPL_API LRESULT ImGui_ImplWin32_WndProcHandler(HWND hWnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wParam, LPARAM lParam);

namespace acp::hook {
namespace {

std::atomic<WNDPROC> g_orig{nullptr};
std::atomic<HWND> g_hwnd{nullptr};
std::atomic<bool> g_unicode{false};

LRESULT CallOrig(HWND h, UINT msg, WPARAM w, LPARAM l) {
    WNDPROC orig = g_orig.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
    if (!orig) return g_unicode ? DefWindowProcW(h, msg, w, l) : DefWindowProcA(h, msg, w, l);
    return g_unicode ? CallWindowProcW(orig, h, msg, w, l) : CallWindowProcA(orig, h, msg, w, l);
}

LRESULT CallDef(HWND h, UINT msg, WPARAM w, LPARAM l) {
    return g_unicode ? DefWindowProcW(h, msg, w, l) : DefWindowProcA(h, msg, w, l);
}

std::uint32_t CurrentMods() {
    std::uint32_t m = 0;
    if (GetKeyState(VK_SHIFT) & 0x8000) m |= 1u << 16;
    if (GetKeyState(VK_CONTROL) & 0x8000) m |= 1u << 17;
    if (GetKeyState(VK_MENU) & 0x8000) m |= 1u << 18;
    return m;
}

// Window thread: last swallowed hotkey (its release is swallowed as well).
WPARAM g_swallowedVk = 0;

bool IsKeyboardOrMouse(UINT msg) {
    return (msg >= WM_KEYFIRST && msg <= WM_KEYLAST) || (msg >= WM_MOUSEFIRST && msg <= WM_MOUSELAST);
}

// Releases always go to the game so that no key gets stuck there.
bool IsRelease(UINT msg) {
    switch (msg) {
        case WM_KEYUP: case WM_SYSKEYUP: case WM_LBUTTONUP: case WM_RBUTTONUP: case WM_MBUTTONUP: case WM_XBUTTONUP:
            return true;
        default:
            return false;
    }
}

// true if a raw input packet reports only a release (may go to the game).
bool RawInputIsReleaseOnly(LPARAM l) {
    alignas(8) BYTE buf[sizeof(RAWINPUT) + 64];
    UINT size = sizeof(buf);
    if (GetRawInputData(reinterpret_cast<HRAWINPUT>(l), RID_INPUT, buf, &size, sizeof(RAWINPUTHEADER)) == static_cast<UINT>(-1))
        return false;
    const auto* ri = reinterpret_cast<const RAWINPUT*>(buf);
    if (ri->header.dwType == RIM_TYPEKEYBOARD) return (ri->data.keyboard.Flags & RI_KEY_BREAK) != 0;
    if (ri->header.dwType == RIM_TYPEMOUSE) {
        const USHORT f = ri->data.mouse.usButtonFlags;
        constexpr USHORT downs = RI_MOUSE_BUTTON_1_DOWN | RI_MOUSE_BUTTON_2_DOWN | RI_MOUSE_BUTTON_3_DOWN |
                                 RI_MOUSE_BUTTON_4_DOWN | RI_MOUSE_BUTTON_5_DOWN | RI_MOUSE_WHEEL | RI_MOUSE_HWHEEL;
        constexpr USHORT ups = RI_MOUSE_BUTTON_1_UP | RI_MOUSE_BUTTON_2_UP | RI_MOUSE_BUTTON_3_UP | RI_MOUSE_BUTTON_4_UP |
                               RI_MOUSE_BUTTON_5_UP;
        return (f & ups) && !(f & downs) && ri->data.mouse.lLastX == 0 && ri->data.mouse.lLastY == 0;
    }
    return false;
}

LRESULT CALLBACK HkWndProc(HWND h, UINT msg, WPARAM w, LPARAM l) {
    InFlight guard(G().inFlightWndProc);
    if (msg == WM_NCDESTROY) {
        RestoreWndProc();
        return CallOrig(h, msg, w, l);
    }
    if (G().phase.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != Phase::Running) return CallOrig(h, msg, w, l);

    // Hotkeys: edge-triggered (bit 30 = autorepeat); the press and its matching release are swallowed.
    // Open/close: configurable combination (default Shift+F2). Unload: End.
    if ((msg == WM_KEYDOWN || msg == WM_SYSKEYDOWN) && !G().keyCapture.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        const std::uint32_t hk = G().toggleKey.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        const bool isToggle = hk != 0 && w == (hk & 0xFFFF) && CurrentMods() == (hk & 0x70000);
        const bool isUnload = w == VK_END && CurrentMods() == 0 && hk != VK_END;
        const std::uint32_t tk = G().tpKey.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
        const bool isTp = !isToggle && tk != 0 && w == (tk & 0xFFFF) && CurrentMods() == (tk & 0x70000);
        if (isToggle || isUnload || isTp) {
            if (!(l & (1 << 30))) {
                if (isToggle) G().keyToggles.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
                else if (isTp) G().keyTeleports.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
                else RequestUnload();
            }
            g_swallowedVk = w;
            return 0;
        }
    }
    if ((msg == WM_KEYUP || msg == WM_SYSKEYUP) && g_swallowedVk != 0 && w == g_swallowedVk) {
        g_swallowedVk = 0;
        return 0;
    }

    if (!G().overlayOpen.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return CallOrig(h, msg, w, l);

    // Overlay open: input goes to ImGui.
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(G().ctxMutex);
        if (G().imguiReady.load() && ImGui::GetCurrentContext()) ImGui_ImplWin32_WndProcHandler(h, msg, w, l);
    }

    if (msg == WM_INPUT) {
        if (RawInputIsReleaseOnly(l)) return CallOrig(h, msg, w, l);
        return CallDef(h, msg, w, l);  // mandatory cleanup, the game does not see the packet
    }
    if (IsKeyboardOrMouse(msg)) {
        if (IsRelease(msg)) return CallOrig(h, msg, w, l);
        // Alt combinations (Alt+Tab, Alt+F4, Alt+Enter) stay with the game.
        if ((msg == WM_SYSKEYDOWN || msg == WM_SYSCHAR) && (l & (1 << 29))) return CallOrig(h, msg, w, l);
        return 0;
    }
    if (msg == WM_SETCURSOR && LOWORD(l) == HTCLIENT) {
        SetCursor(nullptr);  // ImGui draws the cursor itself
        return TRUE;
    }
    return CallOrig(h, msg, w, l);
}

}  // namespace

bool InstallWndProc(HWND hwnd) {
    const bool unicode = IsWindowUnicode(hwnd) != FALSE;
    g_unicode = unicode;
    g_hwnd = hwnd;
    const LONG_PTR cur = unicode ? GetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWLP_WNDPROC) : GetWindowLongPtrA(hwnd, GWLP_WNDPROC);
    g_orig.store(reinterpret_cast<WNDPROC>(cur), std::memory_order_release);
    const LONG_PTR hook = reinterpret_cast<LONG_PTR>(&HkWndProc);
    SetLastError(0);
    const LONG_PTR prev = unicode ? SetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWLP_WNDPROC, hook) : SetWindowLongPtrA(hwnd, GWLP_WNDPROC, hook);
    if (prev == 0 && GetLastError() != 0) {
        g_orig = nullptr;
        return false;
    }
    if (prev != cur) g_orig.store(reinterpret_cast<WNDPROC>(prev), std::memory_order_release);
    return true;
}

bool RestoreWndProc() {
    const HWND hwnd = g_hwnd.exchange(nullptr);
    if (!hwnd) return true;
    const bool unicode = g_unicode.load();
    const LONG_PTR cur = unicode ? GetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWLP_WNDPROC) : GetWindowLongPtrA(hwnd, GWLP_WNDPROC);
    if (cur != reinterpret_cast<LONG_PTR>(&HkWndProc)) {
        g_hwnd = hwnd;  // foreign subclass on top of us: leave ours in place as a pass-through
        return false;
    }
    const LONG_PTR orig = reinterpret_cast<LONG_PTR>(g_orig.load());
    if (unicode) SetWindowLongPtrW(hwnd, GWLP_WNDPROC, orig);
    else SetWindowLongPtrA(hwnd, GWLP_WNDPROC, orig);
    return true;
}

}  // namespace acp::hook

```

`src/hook/xinput_hook.cpp`:

```cpp
// Hook on XInputGetState (xinput9_1_0.dll, static import of the game):
// combo detection + masking + blocking while the overlay is open. Detour without locks/allocation/logging.
#include <windows.h>
#include <xinput.h>

#include <array>
#include <atomic>

#include "MinHook.h"
#include "hooks.h"

namespace acp::hook {
namespace {

using XInputGetState_t = DWORD(WINAPI*)(DWORD, XINPUT_STATE*);

XInputGetState_t g_original = nullptr;  // trampoline (when hooked) or the function itself (fallback)
void* g_target = nullptr;
bool g_hooked = false;

std::atomic<input::PadGate> g_gate{input::PadGate::Pass};
std::atomic<std::uint32_t> g_toggles{0};
std::atomic<std::uint64_t> g_lastSampleMs{0};
input::PadFilterConfig g_cfg;  // immutable after init

struct Slot {
    std::atomic_flag busy;  // try-lock for PadFilter::Step only
    input::PadFilter filter;
    std::atomic<std::uint32_t> seq{0};  // seqlock for the raw state
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> w0{0}, w1{0};
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> lastMs{0};
    std::atomic<bool> connected{false};
};
std::array<Slot, XUSER_MAX_COUNT> g_slots;

// Fallback polling in Present (render thread only)
DWORD g_fallbackIndex = 0;
std::uint64_t g_fallbackNextScan = 0;

input::PadRaw FromX(const XINPUT_GAMEPAD& g) {
    return {g.wButtons, g.bLeftTrigger, g.bRightTrigger, g.sThumbLX, g.sThumbLY, g.sThumbRX, g.sThumbRY};
}
void ToX(const input::PadRaw& p, XINPUT_GAMEPAD& g) {
    g.wButtons = p.buttons;
    g.bLeftTrigger = p.lt;
    g.bRightTrigger = p.rt;
    g.sThumbLX = p.lx;
    g.sThumbLY = p.ly;
    g.sThumbRX = p.rx;
    g.sThumbRY = p.ry;
}

std::uint64_t Pack0(const input::PadRaw& p) {
    return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(p.buttons) | (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(p.lt) << 16) |
           (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(p.rt) << 24) | (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(p.lx)) << 32) |
           (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(p.ly)) << 48);
}
std::uint64_t Pack1(const input::PadRaw& p) {
    return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(p.rx)) |
           (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(p.ry)) << 16);
}
input::PadRaw Unpack(std::uint64_t a, std::uint64_t b) {
    input::PadRaw p;
    p.buttons = static_cast<std::uint16_t>(a);
    p.lt = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(a >> 16);
    p.rt = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(a >> 24);
    p.lx = static_cast<std::int16_t>(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(a >> 32));
    p.ly = static_cast<std::int16_t>(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(a >> 48));
    p.rx = static_cast<std::int16_t>(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(b));
    p.ry = static_cast<std::int16_t>(static_cast<std::uint16_t>(b >> 16));
    return p;
}

DWORD WINAPI HkXInputGetState(DWORD index, XINPUT_STATE* state) {
    InFlight guard(G().inFlightXInput);
    const DWORD r = g_original(index, state);
    if (index >= XUSER_MAX_COUNT || !state || G().phase.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != Phase::Running) return r;

    Slot& s = g_slots[index];
    const bool connected = r == ERROR_SUCCESS;
    const std::uint64_t now = GetTickCount64();
    const input::PadGate gate = g_gate.load(std::memory_order_acquire);

    if (s.busy.test_and_set(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        // Second concurrent caller: do not wait, only apply the block.
        if (connected && gate == input::PadGate::Block) ToX(input::PadRaw{}, state->Gamepad);
        return r;
    }
    const input::PadRaw raw = connected ? FromX(state->Gamepad) : input::PadRaw{};
    const input::PadFilterOut out = s.filter.Step(now, connected, raw, gate, g_cfg);

    // Publish the raw state for the render thread (seqlock).
    s.seq.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    s.w0.store(Pack0(raw), std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.w1.store(Pack1(raw), std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.connected.store(connected, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.lastMs.store(now, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    s.seq.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_release);
    s.busy.clear(std::memory_order_release);

    g_lastSampleMs.store(now, std::memory_order_relaxed);
    if (out.toggleFired) g_toggles.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    if (connected) {
        ToX(out.visible, state->Gamepad);
        state->dwPacketNumber = out.packet;
    }
    return r;
}

}  // namespace

bool CreateXInputHook(bool anyHost, std::string& info) {
    const wchar_t* modules[] = {L"xinput9_1_0.dll", anyHost ? L"xinput1_4.dll" : nullptr};
    for (const wchar_t* name : modules) {
        if (!name) continue;
        HMODULE m = nullptr;
        // Hold our own reference so the module is not unloaded under the hook.
        if (!GetModuleHandleExW(0, name, &m) || !m) continue;
        auto fn = reinterpret_cast<XInputGetState_t>(GetProcAddress(m, "XInputGetState"));
        if (!fn) continue;
        g_target = reinterpret_cast<void*>(fn);
        const MH_STATUS st = MH_CreateHook(g_target, reinterpret_cast<void*>(&HkXInputGetState),
                                           reinterpret_cast<void**>(&g_original));
        if (st != MH_OK) {
            g_original = fn;  // fallback: navigation only, no blocking
            info = "XInput hook failed: " + std::string(MH_StatusToString(st));
            return false;
        }
        MH_QueueEnableHook(g_target);
        RegisterHookTarget(g_target);
        g_hooked = true;
        info = "XInput hook on " + std::string(name == modules[0] ? "xinput9_1_0.dll" : "xinput1_4.dll");
        return true;
    }
    // No XInput loaded by the game: fall back to direct polling.
    if (HMODULE m = LoadLibraryW(L"xinput1_4.dll")) g_original = reinterpret_cast<XInputGetState_t>(GetProcAddress(m, "XInputGetState"));
    info = "No XInput module in the process; falling back to direct polling";
    return false;
}

bool XInputHookActive() {
    if (!g_hooked) return false;
    const std::uint64_t last = g_lastSampleMs.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    return last != 0 && GetTickCount64() - last < 2000;
}

std::uint32_t ConsumePadToggles() {
    const std::uint32_t t = g_toggles.exchange(0, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    // Without an active hook the pad toggle is off (otherwise the combo buttons would be visible to the game).
    return XInputHookActive() ? t : 0;
}

void SetPadGate(input::PadGate gate) { g_gate.store(gate, std::memory_order_release); }

bool ReadPad(input::PadRaw& raw) {
    raw = {};
    if (XInputHookActive()) {
        // Most recently active connected index
        int best = -1;
        std::uint64_t bestMs = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < XUSER_MAX_COUNT; ++i) {
            const Slot& s = g_slots[i];
            if (s.connected.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) && s.lastMs.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) >= bestMs) {
                bestMs = s.lastMs.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
                best = i;
            }
        }
        if (best < 0) return false;
        const Slot& s = g_slots[best];
        for (int tries = 0; tries < 8; ++tries) {
            const std::uint32_t a = s.seq.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
            if (a & 1u) continue;
            const std::uint64_t w0 = s.w0.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
            const std::uint64_t w1 = s.w1.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
            std::atomic_thread_fence(std::memory_order_acquire);
            if (s.seq.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) == a) {
                raw = Unpack(w0, w1);
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    // Fallback: direct polling (trampoline or original function, not the detour).
    if (!g_original) return false;
    const std::uint64_t now = GetTickCount64();
    XINPUT_STATE st{};
    if (g_original(g_fallbackIndex, &st) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        raw = FromX(st.Gamepad);
        return true;
    }
    if (now >= g_fallbackNextScan) {
        g_fallbackNextScan = now + 2000;
        for (DWORD i = 0; i < XUSER_MAX_COUNT; ++i) {
            if (g_original(i, &st) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
                g_fallbackIndex = i;
                raw = FromX(st.Gamepad);
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

}  // namespace acp::hook
```

`src/input/pad_filter.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "pad_filter.h"

namespace acp::input {

PadFilterOut PadFilter::Step(std::uint64_t now, bool connected, const PadRaw& raw, PadGate gate,
                             const PadFilterConfig& cfg) noexcept {
    PadFilterOut out;

    if (!connected) {
        chord_ = Chord::Idle;
        latchButtons_ = 0;
        latchLt_ = latchRt_ = false;
        prevGate_ = gate;
        if (lastConnected_) ++packet_;
        lastConnected_ = false;
        lastVisible_ = {};
        out.packet = packet_;
        return out;
    }

    // --- Chord detection and masking ---
    const std::uint16_t mask = cfg.chordEnabled ? cfg.chordMask : 0;
    const std::uint16_t held = raw.buttons & mask;
    std::uint16_t hide = 0;
    std::uint16_t add = 0;

    switch (chord_) {
        case Chord::Idle:
            if (mask && held == mask) {
                chord_ = Chord::Held;
                t0_ = now;
                fired_ = false;
                hide = mask;
            } else if (held) {
                chord_ = Chord::Deferring;
                deferredBits_ = held;
                t0_ = now;
                hide = held;
            }
            break;
        case Chord::Deferring:
            if (held == mask) {
                chord_ = Chord::Held;
                t0_ = now;
                fired_ = false;
                hide = mask;
            } else if (held == 0) {
                // Short tap: replay it so the game sees it.
                // This step counts as the first of at least two visible polls.
                chord_ = Chord::Replay;
                replayUntil_ = now + cfg.replayMs;
                replaySteps_ = 1;
                add = deferredBits_;
            } else if (now - t0_ >= cfg.deferMs) {
                chord_ = Chord::PassThrough;  // visible normally from now on
            } else {
                deferredBits_ |= held;
                hide = held;
            }
            break;
        case Chord::Replay:
            if (now >= replayUntil_ && replaySteps_ <= 0) {
                chord_ = Chord::Idle;  // tap shown long enough; normal from the next step
            } else {
                add = deferredBits_;
                if (replaySteps_ > 0) --replaySteps_;
            }
            break;
        case Chord::Held:
            hide = mask;
            if (!fired_ && now - t0_ >= cfg.holdMs) {
                fired_ = true;
                out.toggleFired = true;
            }
            if (held == 0) chord_ = Chord::Idle;
            break;
        case Chord::PassThrough:
            if (held == 0) chord_ = Chord::Idle;
            break;
    }

    PadRaw visible = raw;
    visible.buttons = static_cast<std::uint16_t>((raw.buttons & ~hide) | add);

    // --- Gate ---
    if (gate == PadGate::Block) {
        visible = PadRaw{};
    } else {
        if (prevGate_ == PadGate::Block) {
            // Buttons/triggers held while closing stay hidden until released.
            latchButtons_ = raw.buttons;
            latchLt_ = raw.lt > pad::TriggerThreshold;
            latchRt_ = raw.rt > pad::TriggerThreshold;
        }
        latchButtons_ &= raw.buttons;
        visible.buttons = static_cast<std::uint16_t>(visible.buttons & ~latchButtons_);
        if (latchLt_) {
            if (raw.lt <= pad::TriggerThreshold) latchLt_ = false;
            else visible.lt = 0;
        }
        if (latchRt_) {
            if (raw.rt <= pad::TriggerThreshold) latchRt_ = false;
            else visible.rt = 0;
        }
    }
    prevGate_ = gate;

    if (!lastConnected_ || !(visible == lastVisible_)) ++packet_;
    lastConnected_ = true;
    lastVisible_ = visible;
    out.visible = visible;
    out.packet = packet_;
    return out;
}

}  // namespace acp::input

```

`src/input/pad_filter.h`:

```h
#pragma once

// PadFilter: decides, per XInput poll, what the game sees from the controller.
// Pure (no Win32), unit-tested. One instance per pad index; Step() is not reentrant.

#include <cstdint>

namespace acp::input {

// Bit masks matching XINPUT_GAMEPAD_* (without depending on xinput.h).
namespace pad {
inline constexpr std::uint16_t DpadUp = 0x0001, DpadDown = 0x0002, DpadLeft = 0x0004, DpadRight = 0x0008;
inline constexpr std::uint16_t Start = 0x0010, Back = 0x0020, LeftThumb = 0x0040, RightThumb = 0x0080;
inline constexpr std::uint16_t LeftShoulder = 0x0100, RightShoulder = 0x0200;
inline constexpr std::uint16_t A = 0x1000, B = 0x2000, X = 0x4000, Y = 0x8000;
inline constexpr std::uint8_t TriggerThreshold = 30;  // XINPUT_GAMEPAD_TRIGGER_THRESHOLD
}  // namespace pad

struct PadRaw {
    std::uint16_t buttons = 0;
    std::uint8_t lt = 0, rt = 0;
    std::int16_t lx = 0, ly = 0, rx = 0, ry = 0;
    friend bool operator==(const PadRaw&, const PadRaw&) = default;
};

enum class PadGate : std::uint8_t {
    Pass,   // game sees everything (except chord masking and the release latch)
    Block,  // overlay open: game sees a neutral controller
};

struct PadFilterConfig {
    std::uint16_t chordMask = pad::Back | pad::Start;  // View + Menu
    std::uint16_t holdMs = 500;    // hold time until toggling
    std::uint16_t deferMs = 120;   // how long a single chord button is held back
    std::uint16_t replayMs = 60;   // minimum replay time for a short tap
    bool chordEnabled = true;
};

struct PadFilterOut {
    PadRaw visible;           // what the game should see
    std::uint32_t packet = 0; // own packet number: counts changes of `visible`
    bool toggleFired = false; // chord held long enough (once per press)
};

class PadFilter {
public:
    PadFilterOut Step(std::uint64_t nowMs, bool connected, const PadRaw& raw, PadGate gate,
                      const PadFilterConfig& cfg) noexcept;

private:
    enum class Chord : std::uint8_t { Idle, Deferring, Replay, Held, PassThrough };

    Chord chord_ = Chord::Idle;
    std::uint16_t deferredBits_ = 0;
    std::uint64_t t0_ = 0;
    std::uint64_t replayUntil_ = 0;
    int replaySteps_ = 0;
    bool fired_ = false;

    PadGate prevGate_ = PadGate::Pass;
    std::uint16_t latchButtons_ = 0;
    bool latchLt_ = false, latchRt_ = false;

    PadRaw lastVisible_{};
    bool lastConnected_ = false;
    std::uint32_t packet_ = 0;
};

}  // namespace acp::input

```

`src/platform/platform.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "platform.h"

#include <windows.h>
#include <knownfolders.h>
#include <shlobj.h>

#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstdio>
#include <mutex>
#include <thread>

namespace acp::platform {

std::optional<fs::path> DocumentsDir() {
    PWSTR raw = nullptr;
    if (FAILED(SHGetKnownFolderPath(FOLDERID_Documents, KF_FLAG_DEFAULT, nullptr, &raw)) || !raw) {
        if (raw) CoTaskMemFree(raw);
        return std::nullopt;
    }
    fs::path p(raw);
    CoTaskMemFree(raw);
    return p;
}

namespace {
std::optional<std::wstring> Env(const wchar_t* name) {
    wchar_t buf[2048];
    const DWORD n = GetEnvironmentVariableW(name, buf, static_cast<DWORD>(std::size(buf)));
    if (n == 0 || n >= std::size(buf)) return std::nullopt;
    return std::wstring(buf, n);
}
}  // namespace

std::vector<fs::path> DefaultIniCandidates() {
    if (auto e = Env(L"ACP_RELOADED_INI")) return {fs::path(*e)};
    std::vector<fs::path> out;
    if (auto docs = DocumentsDir()) {
        out.push_back(*docs / L"ACP.ini");
        out.push_back(*docs / L"Assassin's Creed Origins" / L"ACP.ini");
    }
    return out;
}

std::vector<fs::path> GameSaveDirs() {
    std::vector<fs::path> out;
    // Launcher install location from the registry, else the default path
    std::wstring launcher;
    wchar_t buf[MAX_PATH]{};
    DWORD size = sizeof(buf);
    if (RegGetValueW(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, L"SOFTWARE\\WOW6432Node\\Ubisoft\\Launcher", L"InstallDir", RRF_RT_REG_SZ, nullptr, buf,
                     &size) == ERROR_SUCCESS)
        launcher = buf;
    if (launcher.empty()) launcher = L"C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Ubisoft\\Ubisoft Game Launcher\\";
    const fs::path root = fs::path(launcher) / L"savegames";
    std::error_code ec;
    for (const auto& account : fs::directory_iterator(root, ec)) {
        if (!account.is_directory(ec)) continue;
        for (const wchar_t* product : {L"4923", L"3539"}) {
            const fs::path dir = account.path() / product;
            if (fs::is_directory(dir, ec)) out.push_back(dir);
        }
    }
    return out;
}
std::optional<fs::path> UserDataDir() {
    if (auto e = Env(L"ACP_RELOADED_DATA_DIR")) return fs::path(*e);
    auto docs = DocumentsDir();
    if (!docs) return std::nullopt;
    return *docs / L"Assassin's Creed Origins" / L"ACP_Reloaded";
}

fs::path ThisModuleDir() {
    HMODULE self = nullptr;
    GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_FROM_ADDRESS | GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT,
                       reinterpret_cast<LPCWSTR>(&ThisModuleDir), &self);
    std::wstring buf(MAX_PATH, L'\0');
    for (;;) {
        const DWORD n = GetModuleFileNameW(self, buf.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(buf.size()));
        if (n < buf.size()) {
            buf.resize(n);
            break;
        }
        buf.resize(buf.size() * 2);
    }
    return fs::path(buf).parent_path();
}

std::optional<FileStamp> StatFile(const fs::path& p) {
    WIN32_FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DATA d{};
    if (!GetFileAttributesExW(p.c_str(), GetFileExInfoStandard, &d)) return std::nullopt;
    if (d.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) return std::nullopt;
    FileStamp s;
    s.size = (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(d.nFileSizeHigh) << 32) | d.nFileSizeLow;
    s.writeTime = (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(d.ftLastWriteTime.dwHighDateTime) << 32) | d.ftLastWriteTime.dwLowDateTime;
    s.readOnly = (d.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_READONLY) != 0;
    return s;
}

std::optional<std::string> ReadFileBytes(const fs::path& p, std::uint32_t* lastError) {
    HANDLE h = CreateFileW(p.c_str(), GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE | FILE_SHARE_DELETE, nullptr,
                           OPEN_EXISTING, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        if (lastError) *lastError = GetLastError();
        return std::nullopt;
    }
    std::string out;
    LARGE_INTEGER size{};
    if (GetFileSizeEx(h, &size) && size.QuadPart > 0 && size.QuadPart < (16LL << 20)) {
        out.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(size.QuadPart));
        DWORD read = 0;
        if (!ReadFile(h, out.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(out.size()), &read, nullptr)) {
            if (lastError) *lastError = GetLastError();
            CloseHandle(h);
            return std::nullopt;
        }
        out.resize(read);
    }
    CloseHandle(h);
    if (lastError) *lastError = 0;
    return out;
}

namespace {

bool IsRetryable(DWORD e) {
    return e == ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION || e == ERROR_LOCK_VIOLATION || e == ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED ||
           e == ERROR_UNABLE_TO_REMOVE_REPLACED || e == ERROR_UNABLE_TO_MOVE_REPLACEMENT ||
           e == ERROR_UNABLE_TO_MOVE_REPLACEMENT_2;
}

DWORD WriteWholeHandle(HANDLE h, std::string_view bytes) {
    std::size_t done = 0;
    while (done < bytes.size()) {
        DWORD written = 0;
        const DWORD chunk = static_cast<DWORD>(std::min<std::size_t>(bytes.size() - done, 1u << 20));
        if (!WriteFile(h, bytes.data() + done, chunk, &written, nullptr)) return GetLastError();
        done += written;
    }
    if (!SetEndOfFile(h)) return GetLastError();
    if (!FlushFileBuffers(h)) return GetLastError();
    return ERROR_SUCCESS;
}

DWORD TryReplace(const fs::path& target, std::string_view bytes, bool allowCreate) {
    const bool exists = GetFileAttributesW(target.c_str()) != INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES;
    if (!exists && !allowCreate) return ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND;

    fs::path tmp = target;
    tmp += L".acpr.tmp";
    HANDLE h = CreateFileW(tmp.c_str(), GENERIC_WRITE, 0, nullptr, CREATE_ALWAYS, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return GetLastError();
    DWORD err = WriteWholeHandle(h, bytes);
    CloseHandle(h);
    if (err != ERROR_SUCCESS) {
        DeleteFileW(tmp.c_str());
        return err;
    }
    BOOL ok;
    if (exists) {
        ok = ReplaceFileW(target.c_str(), tmp.c_str(), nullptr,
                          REPLACEFILE_IGNORE_MERGE_ERRORS | REPLACEFILE_IGNORE_ACL_ERRORS, nullptr, nullptr);
    } else {
        ok = MoveFileExW(tmp.c_str(), target.c_str(), MOVEFILE_WRITE_THROUGH);
    }
    if (!ok) {
        err = GetLastError();
        DeleteFileW(tmp.c_str());
        return err;
    }
    return ERROR_SUCCESS;
}

DWORD TryInPlace(const fs::path& target, std::string_view bytes) {
    HANDLE h = CreateFileW(target.c_str(), GENERIC_WRITE, 0, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return GetLastError();
    const DWORD err = WriteWholeHandle(h, bytes);
    CloseHandle(h);
    return err;
}

}  // namespace

WriteResult WriteFileAtomic(const fs::path& target, std::string_view bytes, WriteStrategy strategy, bool allowCreate) {
    static constexpr std::array<int, 6> kDelaysMs{15, 30, 60, 120, 250, 500};
    WriteResult r;
    for (std::size_t attempt = 0;; ++attempt) {
        ++r.attempts;
        const DWORD err = strategy == WriteStrategy::AtomicReplace ? TryReplace(target, bytes, allowCreate)
                                                                  : TryInPlace(target, bytes);
        if (err == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
            r.ok = true;
            r.lastError = 0;
            return r;
        }
        r.lastError = err;
        r.retryable = IsRetryable(err);
        if (!r.retryable || attempt >= kDelaysMs.size()) return r;
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(kDelaysMs[attempt]));
    }
}

std::uint64_t Fnv1a64(std::string_view data) noexcept {
    std::uint64_t h = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
    for (unsigned char c : data) {
        h ^= c;
        h *= 0x100000001b3ull;
    }
    return h;
}

std::wstring FormatWin32Error(std::uint32_t code) {
    wchar_t* buf = nullptr;
    const DWORD n = FormatMessageW(FORMAT_MESSAGE_ALLOCATE_BUFFER | FORMAT_MESSAGE_FROM_SYSTEM | FORMAT_MESSAGE_IGNORE_INSERTS,
                                   nullptr, code, 0, reinterpret_cast<LPWSTR>(&buf), 0, nullptr);
    std::wstring s = n && buf ? std::wstring(buf, n) : L"Error " + std::to_wstring(code);
    if (buf) LocalFree(buf);
    while (!s.empty() && (s.back() == L'\n' || s.back() == L'\r' || s.back() == L' ')) s.pop_back();
    return s;
}

std::string Narrow(std::wstring_view w) {
    if (w.empty()) return {};
    const int n = WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, w.data(), static_cast<int>(w.size()), nullptr, 0, nullptr, nullptr);
    std::string s(static_cast<std::size_t>(n), '\0');
    WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, w.data(), static_cast<int>(w.size()), s.data(), n, nullptr, nullptr);
    return s;
}

std::wstring Widen(std::string_view s) {
    if (s.empty()) return {};
    const int n = MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, s.data(), static_cast<int>(s.size()), nullptr, 0);
    std::wstring w(static_cast<std::size_t>(n), L'\0');
    MultiByteToWideChar(CP_UTF8, 0, s.data(), static_cast<int>(s.size()), w.data(), n);
    return w;
}

namespace log {
namespace {
std::mutex g_mutex;
std::string g_buffer;
fs::path g_file;
}  // namespace

void SetFile(const fs::path& path) {
    std::lock_guard lock(g_mutex);
    g_file = path;
}

void Write(std::string_view line) {
    SYSTEMTIME st;
    GetLocalTime(&st);
    char stamp[32];
    std::snprintf(stamp, sizeof(stamp), "%02u:%02u:%02u.%03u  ", st.wHour, st.wMinute, st.wSecond, st.wMilliseconds);
    std::lock_guard lock(g_mutex);
    if (g_buffer.size() > (1u << 20)) return;  // guard against unbounded growth
    g_buffer += stamp;
    g_buffer += line;
    g_buffer += "\r\n";
}

void Flush() {
    std::string data;
    fs::path file;
    {
        std::lock_guard lock(g_mutex);
        if (g_buffer.empty() || g_file.empty()) return;
        data.swap(g_buffer);
        file = g_file;
    }
    std::error_code ec;
    fs::create_directories(file.parent_path(), ec);
    HANDLE h = CreateFileW(file.c_str(), FILE_APPEND_DATA, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE, nullptr, OPEN_ALWAYS,
                           FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
    if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;
    DWORD written = 0;
    WriteFile(h, data.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(data.size()), &written, nullptr);
    CloseHandle(h);
}
}  // namespace log

}  // namespace acp::platform
```

```h
#pragma once

// Win32 helpers for paths, file I/O and logging. Every function here does file I/O
// and may only be called on the I/O or lifecycle thread.

#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

namespace acp::platform {

namespace fs = std::filesystem;

// <Documents> via SHGetKnownFolderPath(FOLDERID_Documents).
std::optional<fs::path> DocumentsDir();

// Default candidates: <Documents>\ACP.ini, <Documents>\Assassin's Creed Origins\ACP.ini.
// The environment variable ACP_RELOADED_INI replaces the list (tests, unusual setups).
std::vector<fs::path> DefaultIniCandidates();

// <Documents>\Assassin's Creed Origins\ACP_Reloaded (or ACP_RELOADED_DATA_DIR). Not created here.
std::optional<fs::path> UserDataDir();

// Assassin's Creed Origins save folders in the Ubisoft Connect directory
// (<Launcher>\savegames\<account>\<product>, product 4923 = Steam edition, 3539 = Ubisoft Connect edition).
std::vector<fs::path> GameSaveDirs();

// Directory of the module containing this code (DLL or EXE).
fs::path ThisModuleDir();

struct FileStamp {
    std::uint64_t size = 0;
    std::uint64_t writeTime = 0;
    bool readOnly = false;
    friend bool operator==(const FileStamp&, const FileStamp&) = default;
};
std::optional<FileStamp> StatFile(const fs::path& p);

// Reads the whole file; shares read, write and delete access so nobody is blocked.
std::optional<std::string> ReadFileBytes(const fs::path& p, std::uint32_t* lastError = nullptr);

enum class WriteStrategy { AtomicReplace, InPlace };

struct WriteResult {
    bool ok = false;
    std::uint32_t lastError = 0;
    int attempts = 0;
    bool retryable = false;  // lock or similar; retry later
};

// Writes bytes atomically (ReplaceFile) or exclusively in place; retries on locks
// (15/30/60/120/250/500 ms). With ReplaceFile, creates the target only if allowCreate.
WriteResult WriteFileAtomic(const fs::path& target, std::string_view bytes, WriteStrategy strategy,
                            bool allowCreate = false);

std::uint64_t Fnv1a64(std::string_view data) noexcept;

std::wstring FormatWin32Error(std::uint32_t code);

// Simple logger: Write() only buffers in memory (callable from any thread, never from
// hook detours); Flush() writes to disk (I/O or lifecycle thread only).
namespace log {
void SetFile(const fs::path& path);
void Write(std::string_view line);
void Flush();
}  // namespace log
```

`src/proxy/dinput8_proxy.cpp`:

```cpp
// dinput8.dll proxy: forwards DirectInput8Create to System32\dinput8.dll and loads
// ACPReloaded.dll from its own folder (if missing, the proxy just forwards).
// Chaining for other mods that also ship a dinput8.dll: rename their DLL to dinput8_chain.dll
// and place it alongside - it is loaded before the overlay.
#include <windows.h>

#include <string>

namespace {

HMODULE g_self = nullptr;
HMODULE g_real = nullptr;
INIT_ONCE g_once = INIT_ONCE_STATIC_INIT;

BOOL CALLBACK LoadReal(PINIT_ONCE, PVOID, PVOID*) {
    wchar_t sys[MAX_PATH]{};
    const UINT n = GetSystemDirectoryW(sys, MAX_PATH);
    if (n == 0 || n >= MAX_PATH) return TRUE;
    std::wstring path(sys, n);
    path += L"\\dinput8.dll";
    g_real = LoadLibraryExW(path.c_str(), nullptr, 0);
    return TRUE;
}

FARPROC Real(const char* name) {
    InitOnceExecuteOnce(&g_once, &LoadReal, nullptr, nullptr);
    return g_real ? GetProcAddress(g_real, name) : nullptr;
}

DWORD WINAPI LoaderThread(LPVOID) {
    std::wstring buf(MAX_PATH, L'\0');
    const DWORD n = GetModuleFileNameW(g_self, buf.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(buf.size()));
    if (n == 0 || n >= buf.size()) return 0;
    buf.resize(n);
    const auto slash = buf.find_last_of(L"\\/");
    if (slash == std::wstring::npos) return 0;
    const std::wstring dir = buf.substr(0, slash + 1);
    const std::wstring chain = dir + L"dinput8_chain.dll";
    if (GetFileAttributesW(chain.c_str()) != INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES) LoadLibraryW(chain.c_str());
    const std::wstring overlay = dir + L"ACPReloaded.dll";
    if (GetFileAttributesW(overlay.c_str()) != INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES) LoadLibraryW(overlay.c_str());
    return 0;
}

}  // namespace

// Exports (names via dinput8.def). Normal functions with their own prologue: the Steam and Ubisoft
// overlays place their own inline hooks here.
extern "C" HRESULT WINAPI ACP_DirectInput8Create(HINSTANCE inst, DWORD version, REFIID riid, LPVOID* out, void* outer) {
    using Fn = HRESULT(WINAPI*)(HINSTANCE, DWORD, REFIID, LPVOID*, void*);
    if (auto fn = reinterpret_cast<Fn>(Real("DirectInput8Create"))) return fn(inst, version, riid, out, outer);
    if (out) *out = nullptr;
    return E_FAIL;
}

extern "C" HRESULT WINAPI ACP_DllCanUnloadNow() {
    using Fn = HRESULT(WINAPI*)();
    if (auto fn = reinterpret_cast<Fn>(Real("DllCanUnloadNow"))) return fn();
    return S_FALSE;
}

extern "C" HRESULT WINAPI ACP_DllGetClassObject(REFCLSID clsid, REFIID riid, LPVOID* out) {
    using Fn = HRESULT(WINAPI*)(REFCLSID, REFIID, LPVOID*);
    if (auto fn = reinterpret_cast<Fn>(Real("DllGetClassObject"))) return fn(clsid, riid, out);
    if (out) *out = nullptr;
    return CLASS_E_CLASSNOTAVAILABLE;
}

extern "C" HRESULT WINAPI ACP_DllRegisterServer() {
    using Fn = HRESULT(WINAPI*)();
    if (auto fn = reinterpret_cast<Fn>(Real("DllRegisterServer"))) return fn();
    return E_FAIL;
}

extern "C" HRESULT WINAPI ACP_DllUnregisterServer() {
    using Fn = HRESULT(WINAPI*)();
    if (auto fn = reinterpret_cast<Fn>(Real("DllUnregisterServer"))) return fn();
    return E_FAIL;
}

extern "C" const void* WINAPI ACP_GetdfDIJoystick() {
    using Fn = const void*(WINAPI*)();
    if (auto fn = reinterpret_cast<Fn>(Real("GetdfDIJoystick"))) return fn();
    return nullptr;
}

BOOL APIENTRY DllMain(HMODULE module, DWORD reason, LPVOID) {
    if (reason == DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH) {
        g_self = module;
        DisableThreadLibraryCalls(module);
        if (HANDLE t = CreateThread(nullptr, 0, &LoaderThread, nullptr, 0, nullptr)) CloseHandle(t);
    }
    return TRUE;
}
```
