```cpp
void Panel::DrawCustomFilterRow(const PanelEnv& env) {
    ImGui::PushID("customfx");
    LabelCell("Enable custom filter",
              "These six values are ignored by the current game version.\n"
              "ACP Reloaded renders them itself on top of the game image (in-game overlay only).");
    bool on = settings_.customFilter;
    if (ToggleSwitch("##cf", &on)) {
        settings_.customFilter = on;
        SaveSettings();
    }
    if (!env.customFilterAvailable) {
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("(in-game only)");
    }
    ImGui::PopID();
}

// Settings: frame rate limit, FPS counter and frame time graph (in-game overlay only).
void Panel::DrawPerformanceSection(const PanelEnv& env) {
    const Palette& p = CurrentPalette();
    const float em = Em();
    SectionTitle("Performance & image");
    if (!env.overlay) ImGui::TextDisabled("These options take effect in the in-game overlay.");

    // Sharpening (applies to the game image, not the panel)
    ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
    ImGui::TextUnformatted("Sharpening");
    if (ImGui::IsItemHovered())
        ImGui::SetTooltip("Contrast-adaptive sharpening against the soft look of the game's anti-aliasing.\n"
                          "Strong on soft edges, gentle on hard ones (no halos). 0 = off.");
    ImGui::SameLine(em * 11.0f);
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(em * 11.0f);
    {
        const int lo = 0, hi = 100;
        ValueSlider("##sharpen", ImGuiDataType_S32, &settings_.sharpen, &lo, &hi, "%d", ImGuiSliderFlags_AlwaysClamp);
        if (ImGui::IsItemDeactivatedAfterEdit() | PadAdjustInt(&settings_.sharpen, lo, hi)) SaveSettings();
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
        if (settings_.sharpen == 0) ImGui::TextDisabled("Off");
        else ImGui::Text("%d %%", settings_.sharpen);
    }

    static constexpr int kSteps[] = {0, 30, 40, 45, 50, 60, 72, 75, 90, 100, 120, 144, 165, 180, 240};
    const bool isStep = !settings_.fpsCustom && std::find(std::begin(kSteps), std::end(kSteps), settings_.fpsLimit) != std::end(kSteps);
    char preview[32];
    if (settings_.fpsLimit == 0) std::snprintf(preview, sizeof(preview), "Off");
    else if (isStep) std::snprintf(preview, sizeof(preview), "%d FPS", settings_.fpsLimit);
    else std::snprintf(preview, sizeof(preview), "Custom (%d FPS)", settings_.fpsLimit);
    ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
    ImGui::TextUnformatted("Frame rate limit");
    ImGui::SameLine(em * 11.0f);
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(em * 11.0f);
    if (ComboBox("##fps", preview)) {
        for (int v : kSteps) {
            char label[24];
            if (v == 0) std::snprintf(label, sizeof(label), "Off");
            else std::snprintf(label, sizeof(label), "%d FPS", v);
            if (ImGui::Selectable(label, isStep && v == settings_.fpsLimit)) {
                settings_.fpsLimit = v;
                settings_.fpsCustom = false;
                SaveSettings();
            }
            if (isStep && v == settings_.fpsLimit) ImGui::SetItemDefaultFocus();
        }
        if (ImGui::Selectable("Custom...", !isStep)) {
            if (settings_.fpsLimit == 0) settings_.fpsLimit = 110;
            settings_.fpsCustom = true;
            SaveSettings();
        }
        ImGui::EndCombo();
    }
    if (!isStep) {
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(em * 10.0f);
        const int lo = 20, hi = 360;
        ValueSlider("##fpscustom", ImGuiDataType_S32, &settings_.fpsLimit, &lo, &hi, "%d", ImGuiSliderFlags_AlwaysClamp);
        if (ImGui::IsItemDeactivatedAfterEdit() | PadAdjustInt(&settings_.fpsLimit, lo, hi)) SaveSettings();
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
        ImGui::Text("%d FPS", settings_.fpsLimit);
    }
    ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Text, p.textDim);
    ImGui::TextWrapped("Higher than the game's own limit only works if the in-game frame rate limit is off and VSync is "
                       "disabled (game options, Display).");
    ImGui::PopStyleColor();
    if (LabeledToggle("Limit to 30 FPS while the game is in the background", &settings_.backgroundLimit)) SaveSettings();
    if (LabeledToggle("Show FPS", &settings_.showFps)) SaveSettings();
    if (LabeledToggle("Show frame time graph", &settings_.showFrameGraph)) SaveSettings();
    if (settings_.showFps || settings_.showFrameGraph) {
        static constexpr const char* kCorners[] = {"Top left", "Top right", "Bottom left", "Bottom right"};
        ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
        ImGui::TextUnformatted("Position");
        ImGui::SameLine(em * 11.0f);
        ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(em * 11.0f);
        if (ComboBox("##corner", kCorners[settings_.statsCorner])) {
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
                if (ImGui::Selectable(kCorners[i], i == settings_.statsCorner)) {
                    settings_.statsCorner = i;
                    SaveSettings();
                }
            ImGui::EndCombo();
        }
    }
}

// Settings: send values to the game, restore original values.
void Panel::DrawDataSection(const PanelEnv& env) {
    SectionTitle("Game saves");
    if (ImGui::Button("Back up game saves now")) sync_.BackupGameSaves();
    if (ImGui::IsItemHovered())
        ImGui::SetTooltip("Copies your Assassin's Creed Origins saves (Ubisoft Connect folder) to\n"
                          "Documents\\Assassin's Creed Origins\\ACP_Reloaded\\SaveBackups (last 10 are kept).\n"
                          "Recommended before granting XP, levels or ability points.");
    ImGui::SameLine();
    if (ImGui::Button("Open backup folder")) sync_.OpenSaveBackupFolder();
    if (haveSnap_ && snap_.message.rfind("Game saves", 0) == 0) {
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", snap_.message.c_str());
    }
    ImGui::PushStyleColor(ImGuiCol_Text, CurrentPalette().textDim);
    ImGui::TextWrapped("To restore: quit the game, then copy the files of a backup back into the save folder.");
    ImGui::PopStyleColor();
    if (env.pushAllToGame) {
        SectionTitle("Game");
        ImGui::BeginDisabled(!env.liveActive);
        if (ImGui::Button("Send all values to the game")) env.pushAllToGame(work_);
        ImGui::EndDisabled();
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", env.liveActive ? "Overwrites the values stored in this 'Hacked Animus' save."
                                                 : "Only available in a 'Hacked Animus' save.");
    }

    SectionTitle("Original values");
    if (ImGui::Button("Restore original values")) ImGui::OpenPopup("Restore original values?");
    if (ImGui::BeginPopupModal("Restore original values?", nullptr, ImGuiWindowFlags_AlwaysAutoResize)) {
        ImGui::TextUnformatted("All parameters will be set to their original values.");
        if (ImGui::Button("Restore")) {
            staged_ = {};
            RecordAll("Restore original values");
            sync_.ResetToDefaults();
            snapDeferred_ = true;
            ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
        }
        ImGui::SameLine();
        if (ImGui::Button("Cancel")) ImGui::CloseCurrentPopup();
        ImGui::SetItemDefaultFocus();
        ImGui::EndPopup();
    }
}

void Panel::DrawSettings(const PanelEnv& env) {
    const Palette& p = CurrentPalette();
    SectionTitle("Design");
    struct Opt {
        ThemeId id;
        const char* name;
        const char* desc;
    };
    const Opt opts[] = {{ThemeId::UplayClassic, "Uplay Classic", "The original Animus Control Panel look: anthracite and Animus blue."},
                        {ThemeId::Origins, "Origins", "Warm, clean and modern - gold accents in the style of the game."}};
    for (const Opt& o : opts) {
        const bool sel = settings_.theme == o.id;
        if (ImGui::RadioButton(o.name, sel) && !sel) {
            settings_.theme = o.id;
            SaveSettings();
        }
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", o.desc);
    }

    SectionTitle("Size");
    ImGui::SetNextItemWidth(Em() * 14);
    const float scaleMin = 0.75f, scaleMax = 1.5f;
    ValueSlider("##uiscale", ImGuiDataType_Float, &scaleEdit_, &scaleMin, &scaleMax, "%.2f", ImGuiSliderFlags_AlwaysClamp);
    bool scaleDone = ImGui::IsItemDeactivatedAfterEdit();
    if (PadRowFocus() && g_pad.adjust != 0) {  // 5 % steps
        scaleEdit_ = std::clamp(std::round((scaleEdit_ + 0.05f * static_cast<float>(g_pad.adjust)) * 20.0f) / 20.0f, scaleMin, scaleMax);
        scaleDone = true;
    }
    ImGui::SameLine();
    ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
    ImGui::Text("%.0f %%", scaleEdit_ * 100.0f);
    if (scaleDone) {
        settings_.scale = scaleEdit_;
        SaveSettings();
    }

    SectionTitle("Behaviour");
    if (LabeledToggle("Expert mode (wider ranges)", &settings_.expert)) SaveSettings();
    if (ImGui::IsItemHovered())
        ImGui::SetTooltip("Ctrl+click lets you type values outside the usual ranges. Note: the game itself caps every ACP value\n"
                          "at its own limits (e.g. movement speed at 400 %%) - higher values are stored but have no extra effect.\n"
                          "Extra speed (surface swimming, own mount) goes up to 400 %% in expert mode.");
    if (env.customFilterAvailable) {
        if (LabeledToggle("Custom filter (rendered by ACP Reloaded)", &settings_.customFilter)) SaveSettings();
    }
    if (env.overlay) {
        if (LabeledToggle("Open the panel only in a running 'Hacked Animus' save", &settings_.onlyInHackedSave)) SaveSettings();
        if (ImGui::IsItemHovered())
            ImGui::SetTooltip("Outside such a save (main menu, normal saves) the shortcut only shows a short hint.\n"
                              "The panel closes when you leave the save. The standalone editor is not affected.");
    }
    if (env.game.supported) {
        if (LabeledToggle("Game functions (experimental)", &settings_.gameFunctions)) SaveSettings();
        if (ImGui::IsItemHovered())
            ImGui::SetTooltip("Reads the game time and your progress directly from the game - only in a 'Hacked Animus' save.\n"
                              "Also sets the time of day and grants XP, levels and ability points.");
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("(%s)", settings_.gameFunctions ? env.game.stateText : "off");
        if (settings_.gameFunctions) {
            if (LabeledToggle("Teleport (experimental): to map marker or quest target", &settings_.teleport)) SaveSettings();
            if (ImGui::IsItemHovered())
                ImGui::SetTooltip("Shows your custom map marker, its distance and whether a teleport would be allowed.\n"
                                  "This test stage does not move the player yet.");
        }
    }

    SectionTitle("Controls");
    ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
    ImGui::TextDisabled("Open/close (keyboard):");
    Gap(0.6f);
    if (capturingKey_) {
        ImGui::TextColored(p.accent, "Press the new key combination...  (Esc = cancel)");
        // Record a new combination: first non-modifier key plus held Shift/Ctrl/Alt.
        const ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
        if (ImGui::IsKeyPressed(ImGuiKey_Escape, false)) {
            capturingKey_ = false;
        } else {
            for (const KeyMap& m : HotkeyKeys()) {
                if (!ImGui::IsKeyPressed(m.key, false)) continue;
                std::uint32_t hk = m.vk;
                if (io.KeyShift) hk |= 1u << 16;
                if (io.KeyCtrl) hk |= 1u << 17;
                if (io.KeyAlt) hk |= 1u << 18;
                settings_.toggleKey = hk;
                capturingKey_ = false;
                SaveSettings();
                break;
            }
        }
    } else {
        HotkeyCaps(settings_.toggleKey, ImGui::GetStyle().FramePadding.y);  // centred on the button row
        Gap(1.0f);
        if (ImGui::Button("Change")) capturingKey_ = true;
        if (ImGui::IsItemHovered())
            ImGui::SetTooltip("Press any key, optionally with Shift, Ctrl or Alt.\n"
                              "Avoid keys the game uses. Shift+F2 is the original Uplay overlay shortcut.");
        if (settings_.toggleKey != UiSettings::kDefaultToggleKey) {
            ImGui::SameLine();
            if (ImGui::Button("Default")) {
                settings_.toggleKey = UiSettings::kDefaultToggleKey;
                SaveSettings();
            }
        }
        const std::uint32_t vk = settings_.toggleKey & 0xFFFF;
        if ((settings_.toggleKey & 0x70000) == 0 && ((vk >= 'A' && vk <= 'Z') || (vk >= '0' && vk <= '9')))
            ImGui::TextColored(colors::Warn, "A single letter or digit is probably also used by the game - consider adding Shift or Ctrl.");
    }

    ImGui::TextDisabled("Open/close (controller):");
    Gap(0.6f);
    ImGui::TextDisabled("hold");
    Gap();
    PadIcon(Pad::View);
    Gap(0.15f);
    ImGui::TextDisabled("+");
    Gap(0.15f);
    PadIcon(Pad::Menu);

    ImGui::TextDisabled("Navigate:");
    Gap(0.6f);
    PadIcon(Pad::DPad);
    Gap(0.15f);
    PadIcon(Pad::LStick);
    HintText("Move");
    PadIcon(Pad::A);
    HintText("Select");
    PadIcon(Pad::B);
    HintText("Back / close");
    PadIcon(Pad::LB);
    Gap(0.15f);
    PadIcon(Pad::RB);
    HintText("Category");
    PadIcon(Pad::LT);
    Gap(0.15f);
    PadIcon(Pad::RT);
    Gap();
    ImGui::TextDisabled("Fine / coarse");

    ImGui::TextDisabled("Unload overlay:");
    Gap(0.6f);
    KeyCap("End");

    DrawPerformanceSection(env);
    DrawDataSection(env);

    SectionTitle("Status");
    ImGui::TextDisabled("Version:");
    ImGui::SameLine();
    ImGui::TextUnformatted(ACP_VERSION_LABEL);
    if (haveSnap_) {
        ImGui::TextDisabled("ACP.ini:");
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextUnformatted(snap_.path.empty() ? "(not found)" : snap_.path.string().c_str());
        ImGui::TextDisabled("Saves this session: %llu   External changes: %llu", static_cast<unsigned long long>(snap_.writes),
                            static_cast<unsigned long long>(snap_.externalChanges));
        if (!snap_.message.empty()) ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", snap_.message.c_str());
    }
    if (env.overlay) {
        ImGui::TextDisabled("Live channel:");
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::TextColored(env.liveActive ? p.accent : p.textDim, "%s", env.liveHint.c_str());
        ImGui::TextDisabled("Controller blocking for the game: %s", env.padBlockActive ? "active" : "not available");
    }
}

void Panel::DrawFooter(const PanelEnv& env) {
    ImGui::Separator();
    if (lastInputPad_) {
        if (portraitFocused_) {  // character picture selected: A opens the overview
            PadIcon(Pad::A);
            HintText("All characters");
        }
        PadIcon(Pad::LStick);
        Gap(0.15f);
        PadIcon(Pad::DPad);
        HintText("Choose / change value");
        PadIcon(Pad::B);
        HintText(env.overlay ? "Close" : "Back");
        PadIcon(Pad::X);
        HintText("Reset value");
        PadIcon(Pad::Y);
        HintText("Undo");
        PadIcon(Pad::LB);
        Gap(0.15f);
        PadIcon(Pad::RB);
        HintText("Category");
        PadIcon(Pad::LT);
        Gap(0.15f);
        PadIcon(Pad::RT);
        HintText("Fine / coarse");
        if (env.overlay) {
            PadIcon(Pad::View);
            Gap(0.15f);
            ImGui::TextDisabled("+");
            Gap(0.15f);
            PadIcon(Pad::Menu);
            Gap();
            ImGui::TextDisabled("hold: close");
        }
    } else {
        if (env.overlay) {
            HotkeyCaps(settings_.toggleKey);
            HintText("Open / close");
            KeyCap("Esc");
            HintText("Close");
            KeyCap("End");
            HintText("Unload");
        }
        KeyCap("Ctrl");
        Gap(0.15f);
        ImGui::TextDisabled("+");
        Gap(0.15f);
        KeyCap("Z");
        HintText("Undo");
        InlineIcon(Icon::Reset, CurrentPalette().textDim);
        HintText("Reset value");
        InlineIcon(Icon::Reload, CurrentPalette().textDim);
        Gap();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("Game reloads the world");
    }
}

}  // namespace acp::ui
```

```h
#pragma once

// Animus Control Panel: pure presentation layer over param_table + SyncService.
// Call only on the render thread with an active ImGui context.

#include <cstdio>
#include <array>
#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

#include "pad_feed.h"

#include "theme.h"

namespace acp::ui {

struct UiSettings {
    ThemeId theme = ThemeId::UplayClassic;
    float scale = 1.0f;          // user factor on top of host scaling
    bool expert = false;         // allow out-of-range values via Ctrl+click
    bool customFilter = false;   // custom image filter (overlay only)
    bool gameFunctions = false;  // game functions (phase 7, opt-in)
    bool ownClock = true;        // own time-lapse clock when a script locks the day lengths
    bool onlyInHackedSave = true;  // open the overlay only in a running "Hacked Animus" save
    int fpsLimit = 0;            // own frame rate limiter (0 = off)
    bool backgroundLimit = false;  // 30 FPS while the game is not the active window
    bool showFps = false;        // FPS counter
    bool showFrameGraph = false; // frame time graph
    int statsCorner = 0;         // 0 top left, 1 top right, 2 bottom left, 3 bottom right
    int sharpen = 0;             // sharpening 0..100 % (own filter pass)
    bool fpsCustom = false;      // FPS limit set freely via slider (even if the value matches a preset step)
    bool teleport = false;       // teleport to map marker (experimental, opt-in)
    int tpRange = 2;             // range: 0 = 300 m, 1 = 2 km, 2 = whole map (30 km, default)
    bool tpAllowCombat = false;  // experimental: also in combat
    bool tpAllowMounted = false; // experimental: mounted (mount and rider)
    bool tpQuick = true;         // quick teleport on the map: hold right mouse button / X (only with teleport enabled)
    std::uint32_t tpQuickKey = 0;        // optional key (same format as toggleKey), 0 = off (F3 and L3+R3 = game's photo mode)
    int swimBoost = 100;         // experimental: time scale of the player character while surface swimming (100..200 %)
    int mountBoost = 100;        // experimental: time scale of the player's mount (100..200 %)
    float TeleportRange() const { return tpRange == 2 ? 30000.0f : tpRange == 1 ? 2000.0f : 300.0f; }  // range in metres for the current setting
    std::uint32_t toggleKey = kDefaultToggleKey;  // VK | Shift<<16 | Ctrl<<17 | Alt<<18
    static constexpr std::uint32_t kDefaultToggleKey = 0x10071;  // Shift+F2 (like the Uplay overlay)
    std::string Serialize() const;
    static UiSettings Parse(std::string_view text);
};

// Game function display state (filled by the overlay; no dependency on the game module).
struct GameView {
    bool supported = false;  // overlay only
    const char* stateText = "";
    bool valid = false;
    float hours = 0;
    const char* timeText = "";
    bool timeRunning = true;
    bool displayOverride = false;
    bool acpTimeBlocked = false;  // script: ACP day lengths are currently ignored
    bool holding = false;         // "Hold time" active
    bool ownClock = false;        // own time-lapse clock is currently driving time
    bool weatherValid = false;    // weather readable
    float weather = 0;            // 0 = clear .. 1 = storm
    bool weatherHeld = false;
    bool weatherScripted = false; // mission/script controls the weather
    bool climateOff = false;
    // Teleport 8a (read only)
    bool tpValid = false;
    bool tpReady = false;          // not blocked
    const char* tpText = "";       // block reason or "ready"
    int tpSource = 0;              // marker source (diagnostics)
    bool tpM6A = false, tpModuleSet = false;
    int tpPosMode = 0, tpPlayerBits = 0, tpCombatDiag = 0;
    float tpDistance = 0, tpDistanceXY = 0;
    float tpMaxDistance = 0;       // test limit for the jump (0 = jump unavailable)
    bool questAvailable = false;   // quest target readable (round 8)
    bool questReady = false;
    const char* questText = "";
    float questDistance = 0;
    int questIcons = 0, questUsable = 0;
    bool speedAvailable = false;   // time scale (round 8) available
    bool hasPlayer = false;
    std::uint32_t level = 0, xp = 0, abilityPoints = 0;
    std::string resultText;       // result of the last action
    bool resultIsTime = false;    // result belongs to "Set time" (otherwise to the progression actions)
};

// Game function actions (values as in acp::game::Action).
enum class GameAction : int { GrantXp = 0, LevelAtLeast = 1, GrantPoints = 2, SetTime = 3 };

struct PanelEnv {
    bool overlay = false;        // in game (B/Esc closes) rather than standalone
    bool fillViewport = false;   // window fills the whole viewport (standalone)
    float baseScale = 1.0f;      // host scaling (DPI/resolution)
    std::string liveHint;        // effect hint for the banner
    bool padBlockActive = false; // overlay: game sees no pad input while the panel is open
    bool liveActive = false;     // overlay: game accepts live values (Hacked Animus, in game)
    bool customFilterAvailable = false;  // overlay can draw the custom image filter
    GameView game;
    // Overlay: send all current values to the game (empty = unavailable)
    std::function<void(const std::array<ParamValue, kParamCount>&)> pushAllToGame;
    // Overlay: queue a game action (value, hours, XP multiplier in %) and hold time
    std::function<void(GameAction, int, float, float)> gameAction;
    std::function<void(bool, float)> holdTime;
    std::function<void(int)> teleportRequest;  // overlay: request a jump (0 = marker, 1 = quest target)
    // Host: portrait of playable character n (0..20, -1 = backdrop) as a texture, 0 = none
    std::function<ImTextureID(int)> portrait;
};

class Panel {
public:
    explicit Panel(SyncService& sync) : sync_(sync) {}

    // Every frame (even with the panel closed): take over the new state.
    void Update();
    // Call when the overlay opens (resets focus/navigation).
    void OnOpened();
    // Draws the panel. closeRequested is set when B/Esc should close the overlay.
    void Draw(const PanelEnv& env, const PadActions& pad, bool* closeRequested);
    // Commit an in-progress slider edit immediately (on close).
    void FlushActiveEdit();
    // Set the start page (0 = presets, 1..5 = categories, 6 = settings).
    void SetPage(int page) { category_ = page >= 0 && page <= 6 ? page : 1; }
    // Test/editor: render as after controller input (footer, single column, visible focus).
    void DebugOpenCharacterGrid() { openCharGrid_ = true; }
    void DebugScroll(float em) { debugScroll_ = em; }  // editor: scroll the page (in em) for screenshots
    void DebugSetSearch(const char* text) { std::snprintf(search_, sizeof(search_), "%s", text); }  // editor: screenshots  // editor: open the tile overview for screenshots
    void ForcePadLayout() { lastInputPad_ = true; }

    const std::array<ParamValue, kParamCount>& Values() const { return work_; }
    const UiSettings& Settings() const { return settings_; }
    // Currently recording a new key combination (overlay: hotkey is suspended meanwhile).
    bool CapturingKey() const { return capturingKey_; }
    // Requested weather for this session (not saved; the game doesn't save weather either)
    bool WeatherHold() const { return weatherHold_; }
    float WeatherTarget() const { return static_cast<float>(weatherValue_) / 100.0f; }
    // Controller: a value row has focus, left/right change the value (for PadFeeder::SetHorizontalCapture).
    bool WantsPadHorizontal() const { return wantsHorizontal_; }

private:
    void ApplySettingsIfNeeded(const PanelEnv& env);
    void SaveSettings();
    void DrawHeader(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawSidebar(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawToolbar(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawPresetControls(float comboW);
    void DrawGroupColumns(Category cat, const std::vector<std::string_view>& groups, const PanelEnv& env, bool& first);
    int GroupWeight(Category cat, std::string_view group, const PanelEnv& env) const;

    void DrawMissingIniBar();
    void DrawCategory(Category cat, const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawSearchResults(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawGroup(Category cat, std::string_view group, const PanelEnv& env, bool& first);
    void DrawParam(const ParamDesc& d, const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawCharacterPreview(const ParamDesc& d, const PanelEnv& env);
    bool BeginCard(const std::string& id, const char* title, bool warn, const char* note = nullptr);
    void EndCard(bool tableOpen);
    void DrawTimeLockRow();
    void DrawSandstormQuickRow();
    void DrawWeatherRows(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawTeleportCard(const PanelEnv& env, bool& first);
    void DrawSpeedCard(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawCustomFilterRow(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawDayLengthRow();
    void DrawGameTimeRow(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawProgressRow(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawSetTimeRows(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawGrantRows(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawDataSection(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawPerformanceSection(const PanelEnv& env);
    void DrawSettings(const PanelEnv& env);

    void Submit(ParamId id, ParamValue v, bool record = true);
    // Undo: changes are recorded with the previously committed value (groups = one step).
    void Record(ParamId id, const ParamValue& newValue);
    void BeginUndoGroup(std::string label);
    void EndUndoGroup();

    bool IsDisabled(const ParamDesc& d) const;
    bool Visible(const ParamDesc& d) const;

    SyncService& sync_;
    Snapshot snap_;
    bool haveSnap_ = false;
    bool snapDeferred_ = false;
    std::array<ParamValue, kParamCount> work_{};
    std::array<std::optional<ParamValue>, kParamCount> localEdit_{};
    std::array<std::uint64_t, kParamCount> localEditTick_{};
    std::array<std::optional<ParamValue>, kParamCount> staged_{};  // waiting for "Apply" (world reload)
    std::optional<ParamId> activeEdit_;

    bool settingsLoaded_ = false;
    bool themeDirty_ = true;
    float appliedScale_ = 0.0f;

    int category_ = 1;  // 0 = presets, 1..5 = categories, 6 = settings
    bool focusContent_ = true;
    bool wasAnyItemActive_ = false;
    bool lastInputPad_ = false;
    char search_[64] = {};
    bool changedOnly_ = false;
    int sandQuick_ = -1;  // in-progress edit of the sandstorm quick slider
    int selectedPreset_ = -1;

    std::uint64_t tpArmedUntil_ = 0;  // teleport: first press arms for 3 s (second press confirms)
```

`src/ui/portraits.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "portraits.h"

#include <windows.h>
#include <wincodec.h>

#include <array>
#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

#include "portraits_ids.h"

namespace acp::ui::portraits {
namespace {

struct Image {
    UINT w = 0, h = 0;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> rgba;
};

constexpr int kSlots = kCount + 1;  // characters + backdrop (last slot)
std::array<Image, kSlots> g_images;
std::atomic<bool> g_decoded{false};
std::array<ID3D11ShaderResourceView*, kSlots> g_srv{};  // render thread only
std::array<bool, kSlots> g_failed{};                     // render thread only: creation failed, don't retry

int Slot(int n) { return n == kBackdrop ? kCount : n; }

bool DecodeOne(IWICImagingFactory* f, HMODULE module, int resId, Image& out) {
    HRSRC res = FindResourceW(module, MAKEINTRESOURCEW(resId), MAKEINTRESOURCEW(10) /* RT_RCDATA */);
    if (!res) return false;
    HGLOBAL h = LoadResource(module, res);
    const DWORD size = SizeofResource(module, res);
    const void* data = h ? LockResource(h) : nullptr;
    if (!data || !size) return false;
    bool ok = false;
    IWICStream* stream = nullptr;
    IWICBitmapDecoder* dec = nullptr;
    IWICBitmapFrameDecode* frame = nullptr;
    IWICFormatConverter* conv = nullptr;
    if (SUCCEEDED(f->CreateStream(&stream)) &&
        SUCCEEDED(stream->InitializeFromMemory(static_cast<BYTE*>(const_cast<void*>(data)), size)) &&
        SUCCEEDED(f->CreateDecoderFromStream(stream, nullptr, WICDecodeMetadataCacheOnLoad, &dec)) &&
        SUCCEEDED(dec->GetFrame(0, &frame)) && SUCCEEDED(f->CreateFormatConverter(&conv)) &&
        SUCCEEDED(conv->Initialize(frame, GUID_WICPixelFormat32bppRGBA, WICBitmapDitherTypeNone, nullptr, 0,
                                   WICBitmapPaletteTypeCustom)) &&
        SUCCEEDED(conv->GetSize(&out.w, &out.h)) && out.w && out.h && out.w <= 4096 && out.h <= 4096) {
        out.rgba.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(out.w) * out.h * 4);
        ok = SUCCEEDED(conv->CopyPixels(nullptr, out.w * 4, static_cast<UINT>(out.rgba.size()), out.rgba.data()));
    }
    if (conv) conv->Release();
    if (frame) frame->Release();
    if (dec) dec->Release();
    if (stream) stream->Release();
    if (!ok) out = Image{};
    return ok;
}

}  // namespace

int Decode(HMODULE module) {
    if (g_decoded.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        int n = 0;
        for (const Image& i : g_images) n += i.rgba.empty() ? 0 : 1;
        return n;
    }
    IWICImagingFactory* f = nullptr;
    if (FAILED(CoCreateInstance(CLSID_WICImagingFactory, nullptr, CLSCTX_INPROC_SERVER, IID_PPV_ARGS(&f)))) return 0;
    int n = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < kCount; ++i) n += DecodeOne(f, module, IDR_PORTRAIT_FIRST + i, g_images[i]) ? 1 : 0;
    n += DecodeOne(f, module, IDR_PORTRAIT_BACKDROP, g_images[kCount]) ? 1 : 0;
    f->Release();
    g_decoded.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
    return n;
}

ImTextureID Get(ID3D11Device* device, int n) {
    if (!device || (n != kBackdrop && (n < 0 || n >= kCount)) || !g_decoded.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) return ImTextureID{};
    const int s = Slot(n);
    if (!g_srv[s] && !g_failed[s]) {
        const Image& img = g_images[s];
        g_failed[s] = true;
        if (!img.rgba.empty()) {
            D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC td{};
            td.Width = img.w;
            td.Height = img.h;
            td.MipLevels = 1;
            td.ArraySize = 1;
            td.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM;
            td.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
            td.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_IMMUTABLE;
            td.BindFlags = D3D11_BIND_SHADER_RESOURCE;
            const D3D11_SUBRESOURCE_DATA sd{img.rgba.data(), img.w * 4, 0};
            ID3D11Texture2D* tex = nullptr;
            if (SUCCEEDED(device->CreateTexture2D(&td, &sd, &tex)) && tex) {
                if (SUCCEEDED(device->CreateShaderResourceView(tex, nullptr, &g_srv[s]))) g_failed[s] = false;
                tex->Release();
            }
        }
    }
    return reinterpret_cast<ImTextureID>(g_srv[s]);
}

void Release() {
    for (auto& v : g_srv) {
        if (v) v->Release();
        v = nullptr;
    }
    g_failed.fill(false);
}

}  // namespace acp::ui::portraits

```

`src/ui/portraits.h`:

```h
#pragma once

// Character portraits (playable ACP characters 0..20) and the Origins theme backdrop, embedded as RCDATA resources
// (assets/portraits, see portraits.rc). Images: our own cut-out screenshots of the characters; see THIRD_PARTY.md.
//
// Flow: Decode() on a separate thread with COM (WIC to RGBA), then Get() creates textures on demand on the
// render thread. Call Release() before the D3D device is torn down (render thread).

#include <d3d11.h>

#include "imgui.h"

namespace acp::ui::portraits {

inline constexpr int kCount = 21;
inline constexpr int kBackdrop = -1;  // backdrop behind the characters (Origins)

// Decodes all images embedded in the module. COM must be initialised on the calling thread.
// Returns the number of images decoded successfully. Safe to call more than once.
int Decode(HMODULE module);
// Render thread: texture for character n (0..20) or kBackdrop; 0 if not available (yet).
ImTextureID Get(ID3D11Device* device, int n);
// Render thread: release all textures (decoded data is kept for a new device).
void Release();

}  // namespace acp::ui::portraits

```

`src/ui/theme.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "theme.h"

namespace acp::ui {
namespace {

#include "font_cinzel.inc"  // kCinzelTtf (SIL Open Font License 1.1, third_party/fonts/Cinzel_OFL.txt)

ImFont* g_heading = nullptr;

// Uplay look: charcoal, Animus blue, soft rounding.
constexpr Palette kUplay{
    {0.08f, 0.09f, 0.11f, 0.97f},  // background
    {0.05f, 0.06f, 0.08f, 0.98f},  // sidebar
    {0.13f, 0.15f, 0.19f, 1.00f},  // frame
    {0.18f, 0.22f, 0.28f, 0.80f},  // border
    {0.105f, 0.118f, 0.145f, 1.00f},  // card
    {0.00f, 0.62f, 0.98f, 0.13f},     // highlight
    {0.00f, 0.62f, 0.98f, 1.00f},  // accent
    {0.00f, 0.42f, 0.68f, 1.00f},  // accentDim
    {1.00f, 1.00f, 1.00f, 1.00f},  // accentText
    {0.86f, 0.89f, 0.93f, 1.00f},  // text
    {0.52f, 0.56f, 0.62f, 1.00f},  // textDim
    1.0f,
    "ANIMUS CONTROL PANEL",
    "Reloaded",
};

// Origins look based on the game's main menu: dark slate teal, thin light borders, white sans-serif text,
// gold only for focus, selection and fill levels (like the focused button in the game menu: gold frame + teal).
constexpr Palette kOrigins{
    {0.040f, 0.068f, 0.074f, 0.95f},  // background
    {0.030f, 0.056f, 0.061f, 0.70f},  // sidebar (slightly translucent, same tone)
    {0.070f, 0.110f, 0.116f, 1.00f},  // frame
    {0.86f, 0.88f, 0.86f, 0.20f},     // border (thin light line)
    {0.050f, 0.088f, 0.094f, 0.80f},  // card
    {0.16f, 0.37f, 0.36f, 0.55f},     // highlight (teal)
    {0.88f, 0.72f, 0.42f, 1.00f},     // accent (gold)
    {0.52f, 0.43f, 0.26f, 1.00f},     // accentDim
    {0.05f, 0.07f, 0.07f, 1.00f},     // accentText
    {0.95f, 0.94f, 0.91f, 1.00f},     // text
    {0.60f, 0.65f, 0.63f, 1.00f},     // textDim
    0.0f,
    "ANIMUS",
    "Control Panel",
};
ThemeId g_theme = ThemeId::UplayClassic;

ImVec4 Mix(const ImVec4& a, const ImVec4& b, float t) {
    return ImVec4(a.x + (b.x - a.x) * t, a.y + (b.y - a.y) * t, a.z + (b.z - a.z) * t, a.w + (b.w - a.w) * t);
}
ImVec4 WithAlpha(ImVec4 c, float a) {
    c.w = a;
    return c;
}

}  // namespace

const Palette& CurrentPalette() { return g_theme == ThemeId::Origins ? kOrigins : kUplay; }
ThemeId CurrentTheme() { return g_theme; }

void ApplyTheme(ThemeId id, float scale) {
    g_theme = id;
    const Palette& p = CurrentPalette();
    ImGuiStyle& s = ImGui::GetStyle();
    // Keep font values (owned by the host: DPI/resolution scaling).
    const float fontBase = s.FontSizeBase, fontDpi = s.FontScaleDpi, fontMain = s.FontScaleMain;
    s = ImGuiStyle();
    ImGui::StyleColorsDark(&s);
    s.FontSizeBase = fontBase;
    s.FontScaleDpi = fontDpi;
    s.FontScaleMain = fontMain;

    const bool origins = id == ThemeId::Origins;
    s.WindowRounding = origins ? 0.0f : 4.0f;
    s.ChildRounding = origins ? 0.0f : 3.0f;
    s.FrameRounding = origins ? 1.0f : 3.0f;
    s.GrabRounding = origins ? 0.0f : 3.0f;
    s.PopupRounding = origins ? 0.0f : 3.0f;
    s.ScrollbarRounding = origins ? 0.0f : 3.0f;
    s.TabRounding = origins ? 0.0f : 3.0f;
    s.WindowBorderSize = 1.0f;
    s.ChildBorderSize = 0.0f;  // borders only where wanted (cards in the Origins theme)
    s.FrameBorderSize = origins ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
    s.WindowPadding = origins ? ImVec2(18, 16) : ImVec2(14, 12);
    s.FramePadding = origins ? ImVec2(10, 7) : ImVec2(10, 6);
    s.ItemSpacing = origins ? ImVec2(12, 9) : ImVec2(10, 8);
    s.ItemInnerSpacing = ImVec2(8, 6);
    s.CellPadding = origins ? ImVec2(10, 6) : ImVec2(8, 5);
    s.ScrollbarSize = origins ? 10.0f : 14.0f;
    s.GrabMinSize = origins ? 6.0f : 14.0f;
    s.SeparatorTextBorderSize = origins ? 1.0f : 2.0f;
    s.SeparatorTextPadding = ImVec2(0, 4);
    s.DisabledAlpha = 0.38f;
    s.ScaleAllSizes(scale);

    ImVec4* c = s.Colors;
    c[ImGuiCol_Text] = p.text;
    c[ImGuiCol_TextDisabled] = p.textDim;
    c[ImGuiCol_WindowBg] = p.background;
    c[ImGuiCol_ChildBg] = ImVec4(0, 0, 0, 0);
    c[ImGuiCol_PopupBg] = WithAlpha(p.sidebar, 0.98f);
    c[ImGuiCol_Border] = p.border;
    c[ImGuiCol_FrameBg] = p.frame;
    c[ImGuiCol_FrameBgHovered] = Mix(p.frame, p.accentDim, 0.30f);
    c[ImGuiCol_FrameBgActive] = Mix(p.frame, p.accentDim, 0.45f);
    c[ImGuiCol_TitleBg] = p.sidebar;
    c[ImGuiCol_TitleBgActive] = p.sidebar;
    c[ImGuiCol_TitleBgCollapsed] = p.sidebar;
    c[ImGuiCol_ScrollbarBg] = ImVec4(0, 0, 0, 0.2f);
    c[ImGuiCol_ScrollbarGrab] = Mix(p.frame, p.text, 0.15f);
    c[ImGuiCol_ScrollbarGrabHovered] = p.accentDim;
    c[ImGuiCol_ScrollbarGrabActive] = p.accent;
    c[ImGuiCol_CheckMark] = p.accent;
    c[ImGuiCol_SliderGrab] = p.accent;
    c[ImGuiCol_SliderGrabActive] = Mix(p.accent, ImVec4(1, 1, 1, 1), 0.3f);
    c[ImGuiCol_Button] = Mix(p.frame, p.accentDim, 0.15f);
    c[ImGuiCol_ButtonHovered] = p.accentDim;
    c[ImGuiCol_ButtonActive] = p.accent;
    c[ImGuiCol_Header] = WithAlpha(p.accentDim, 0.55f);
    c[ImGuiCol_HeaderHovered] = WithAlpha(p.accentDim, 0.75f);
    c[ImGuiCol_HeaderActive] = p.accent;
    c[ImGuiCol_Separator] = p.border;
    c[ImGuiCol_TableRowBg] = ImVec4(0, 0, 0, 0);
    c[ImGuiCol_TableRowBgAlt] = ImVec4(1, 1, 1, origins ? 0.018f : 0.025f);
    c[ImGuiCol_TableBorderLight] = WithAlpha(p.border, 0.5f);
    c[ImGuiCol_NavCursor] = p.accent;
    c[ImGuiCol_TextSelectedBg] = WithAlpha(p.accent, 0.35f);
    c[ImGuiCol_ModalWindowDimBg] = ImVec4(0, 0, 0, 0.55f);
    if (origins) {
        // Like the game menu buttons: semi-transparent with a light line, focus/hover in teal
        c[ImGuiCol_Button] = ImVec4(p.frame.x, p.frame.y, p.frame.z, 0.55f);
        c[ImGuiCol_ButtonHovered] = p.highlight;
        c[ImGuiCol_ButtonActive] = WithAlpha(p.highlight, 0.85f);
        c[ImGuiCol_FrameBg] = ImVec4(p.frame.x, p.frame.y, p.frame.z, 0.70f);
        c[ImGuiCol_FrameBgHovered] = p.highlight;
        c[ImGuiCol_FrameBgActive] = WithAlpha(p.highlight, 0.85f);
        c[ImGuiCol_Header] = p.highlight;
        c[ImGuiCol_HeaderHovered] = WithAlpha(p.highlight, 0.75f);
        c[ImGuiCol_HeaderActive] = WithAlpha(p.highlight, 0.95f);
        c[ImGuiCol_PopupBg] = ImVec4(0.035f, 0.060f, 0.066f, 0.98f);
        c[ImGuiCol_Separator] = p.border;
    }
}

ImFont* HeadingFont() { return g_heading; }

namespace {
void AddHeadingFont(float sizePixels) {
    ImFontConfig cfg;
    cfg.FontDataOwnedByAtlas = false;  // static data
    g_heading = ImGui::GetIO().Fonts->AddFontFromMemoryTTF(const_cast<unsigned char*>(kCinzelTtf),
                                                            static_cast<int>(sizeof(kCinzelTtf)), sizePixels, &cfg);
}
}  // namespace

bool LoadUiFont(const std::string* ttfData, float sizePixels) {
    ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    io.Fonts->Clear();
    g_heading = nullptr;
    if (ttfData && !ttfData->empty()) {
        ImFontConfig cfg;
        cfg.FontDataOwnedByAtlas = false;  // data owned by the caller
        if (io.Fonts->AddFontFromMemoryTTF(const_cast<char*>(ttfData->data()), static_cast<int>(ttfData->size()),
                                           sizePixels, &cfg)) {
            ImGui::GetStyle().FontSizeBase = sizePixels;
            AddHeadingFont(sizePixels);
            return true;
        }
    }
    ImFontConfig cfg;
    cfg.SizePixels = sizePixels;
    io.Fonts->AddFontDefault(&cfg);
    ImGui::GetStyle().FontSizeBase = sizePixels;
    AddHeadingFont(sizePixels);
    return false;
}

}  // namespace acp::ui

```

`src/ui/theme.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include <string>

#include "imgui.h"

namespace acp::ui {

enum class ThemeId : int { UplayClassic = 0, Origins = 1 };

// Colours of the active theme (for custom widgets and accents).
struct Palette {
    ImVec4 background, sidebar, frame, border;
    ImVec4 card;                           // group cards in the content area
    ImVec4 highlight;                      // fill of the focused row / selected category
    ImVec4 accent, accentDim, accentText;  // accentText: text on accent fill
    ImVec4 text, textDim;
    float toggleRounding;  // 0..1 relative to height (1 = pill)
    const char* title;
    const char* subtitle;
};

namespace colors {
inline constexpr ImVec4 Ok{0.30f, 0.80f, 0.45f, 1.00f};
inline constexpr ImVec4 Warn{0.98f, 0.66f, 0.18f, 1.00f};
inline constexpr ImVec4 Error{0.95f, 0.30f, 0.28f, 1.00f};
}  // namespace colors

const Palette& CurrentPalette();
ThemeId CurrentTheme();

// Applies theme, colours and (scaled) spacing to the current ImGui context.
// Font size and font scaling (FontSizeBase/FontScaleDpi) are left unchanged.
void ApplyTheme(ThemeId id, float scale);

// Loads the UI font from already-read TTF data (data must outlive the context).
// Without data: ImGui default font. Returns true if the TTF font is used.
bool LoadUiFont(const std::string* ttfData, float sizePixels);

// Heading font (Cinzel, embedded); nullptr if it could not be loaded.
ImFont* HeadingFont();

}  // namespace acp::ui

```

`src/ui/widgets.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "widgets.h"

#include <algorithm>

#include "imgui_internal.h"  // TempInputIsActive
#include "icons.h"
#include "theme.h"

namespace acp::ui {

bool ToggleSwitch(const char* id, bool* value, bool showText) {
    const Palette& p = CurrentPalette();
    const float h = ImGui::GetFrameHeight();
    const float w = h * 1.9f;
    const ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
    // EnableNav: since ImGui 1.91 InvisibleButton is otherwise not a nav target (controller/keyboard would skip the switch)
    const bool pressed = ImGui::InvisibleButton(id, ImVec2(w, h), ImGuiButtonFlags_EnableNav);
    if (pressed) *value = !*value;

    const bool hovered = ImGui::IsItemHovered();
    ImDrawList* dl = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
    const float alpha = ImGui::GetStyle().Alpha;  // BeginDisabled() has already reduced alpha
    if (CurrentTheme() == ThemeId::Origins) {
        // On: teal with gold frame and gold grab; off: dark with a light line
        const ImVec2 b(pos.x + w, pos.y + h);
        dl->AddRectFilled(pos, b, ImGui::GetColorU32(*value ? p.highlight : (hovered ? p.highlight : p.frame)));
        dl->AddRect(pos, b, ImGui::GetColorU32(*value ? p.accent : p.border), 0.0f, 1.0f);
        const float pad = h * 0.26f, knob = h - pad * 2;
        const float kx = *value ? pos.x + w - pad - knob : pos.x + pad;
        const ImVec4 off(p.textDim.x, p.textDim.y, p.textDim.z, 0.75f);
        dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(kx, pos.y + pad), ImVec2(kx + knob, pos.y + pad + knob), ImGui::GetColorU32(*value ? p.accent : off));
        if (showText) {
            ImGui::SameLine();
            ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
            ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", *value ? "On" : "Off");
        }
        return pressed;
    }
    ImVec4 bg = *value ? p.accent : p.frame;
    if (hovered) bg = *value ? ImVec4(p.accent.x * 1.1f, p.accent.y * 1.1f, p.accent.z * 1.1f, 1.0f) : p.accentDim;
    bg.w *= alpha;
    const float r = h * 0.5f * p.toggleRounding;
    dl->AddRectFilled(pos, ImVec2(pos.x + w, pos.y + h), ImGui::GetColorU32(bg), r);
    if (!*value) dl->AddRect(pos, ImVec2(pos.x + w, pos.y + h), ImGui::GetColorU32(ImVec4(p.border.x, p.border.y, p.border.z, alpha)), r);
    const float pad = 3.0f;
    const float knob = h - pad * 2;
    const float kx = *value ? pos.x + w - pad - knob : pos.x + pad;
    const ImVec4 knobColor = *value ? ImVec4(p.accentText.x, p.accentText.y, p.accentText.z, alpha)
                                    : ImVec4(p.text.x, p.text.y, p.text.z, alpha * 0.85f);
    dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(kx, pos.y + pad), ImVec2(kx + knob, pos.y + pad + knob), ImGui::GetColorU32(knobColor),
                      knob * 0.5f * p.toggleRounding);

    if (showText) {
        ImGui::SameLine();
        ImGui::AlignTextToFramePadding();
        ImGui::TextDisabled("%s", *value ? "On" : "Off");
    }
    return pressed;
}

bool ValueSlider(const char* id, ImGuiDataType type, void* value, const void* min, const void* max, const char* format,
                 ImGuiSliderFlags flags) {
    const Palette& p = CurrentPalette();
    const bool textInput = ImGui::TempInputIsActive(ImGui::GetID(id));  // Ctrl+click: show a normal input field
    const float h = ImGui::GetFrameHeight();
    const float r = h * 0.24f;
    if (!textInput) {
        const ImVec4 none(0, 0, 0, 0);
        for (ImGuiCol c : {ImGuiCol_FrameBg, ImGuiCol_FrameBgHovered, ImGuiCol_FrameBgActive, ImGuiCol_SliderGrab,
                           ImGuiCol_SliderGrabActive, ImGuiCol_Text})
            ImGui::PushStyleColor(c, none);
    }
    ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_GrabMinSize, r * 2.0f);
    ImGui::PushStyleVar(ImGuiStyleVar_FrameBorderSize, textInput ? ImGui::GetStyle().FrameBorderSize : 0.0f);
    const bool changed = ImGui::SliderScalar(id, type, value, min, max, format, flags);
    ImGui::PopStyleVar(2);
    // Console style: release sliders activated by controller right away (left/right changes the value directly;
    // A must not open a hidden edit mode that has to be left with A/B).
    if (ImGui::IsItemActive() && GImGui->ActiveIdSource == ImGuiInputSource_Gamepad) ImGui::ClearActiveID();
    if (textInput) return changed;
    ImGui::PopStyleColor(6);

    double t = 0.0;
    if (type == ImGuiDataType_S32) {
        const int v = *static_cast<const int*>(value), lo = *static_cast<const int*>(min), hi = *static_cast<const int*>(max);
        t = hi > lo ? static_cast<double>(v - lo) / static_cast<double>(hi - lo) : 0.0;
    } else if (type == ImGuiDataType_Float) {
        const float v = *static_cast<const float*>(value), lo = *static_cast<const float*>(min), hi = *static_cast<const float*>(max);
        t = hi > lo ? static_cast<double>(v - lo) / static_cast<double>(hi - lo) : 0.0;
    }
    t = std::clamp(t, 0.0, 1.0);

    const ImVec2 a = ImGui::GetItemRectMin(), b = ImGui::GetItemRectMax();
    const bool hot = ImGui::IsItemHovered() || ImGui::IsItemActive();
    const float pad = 2.0f;  // as in ImGui (grab inset from edge) so mouse and grab line up
    const float x0 = a.x + pad + r, x1 = b.x - pad - r;
    const float kx = x0 + static_cast<float>(t) * (x1 - x0);
    const float cy = (a.y + b.y) * 0.5f;
    const float th = std::max(2.0f, h * 0.09f);
    ImDrawList* dl = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
    const ImVec4 track(p.text.x, p.text.y, p.text.z, 0.16f);
    dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x0, cy - th * 0.5f), ImVec2(x1, cy + th * 0.5f), ImGui::GetColorU32(track), th * 0.5f);
    dl->AddRectFilled(ImVec2(x0, cy - th * 0.5f), ImVec2(kx, cy + th * 0.5f), ImGui::GetColorU32(p.accent), th * 0.5f);
    const float kr = hot ? r * 1.12f : r;
    if (CurrentTheme() == ThemeId::Origins) {
        // slim white grab, gold ring when focused (like focus in the game menu)
        dl->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(kx, cy), kr * 0.82f, ImGui::GetColorU32(p.text), 24);
        if (hot) dl->AddCircle(ImVec2(kx, cy), kr * 1.25f, ImGui::GetColorU32(p.accent), 24, 1.5f);
    } else {
        dl->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(kx, cy), kr, ImGui::GetColorU32(hot ? p.accent : p.text), 24);
    }
    return changed;
}
bool ComboBox(const char* id, const char* preview) {
    const Palette& p = CurrentPalette();
    const ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
    const float w = ImGui::CalcItemWidth();
    const float h = ImGui::GetFrameHeight();
    ImDrawList* dl = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();  // before BeginCombo: afterwards the popup may be current
    const bool open = ImGui::BeginCombo(id, preview, ImGuiComboFlags_NoArrowButton);
    DrawIcon(dl, Icon::ChevronDown, ImVec2(pos.x + w - h * 0.55f, pos.y + h * 0.5f), h * 0.5f,
             ImGui::GetColorU32(open ? p.accent : p.textDim));
    return open;
}
float HeadingWidth(const char* text, float scale) {
    const bool origins = CurrentTheme() == ThemeId::Origins && HeadingFont();
    const float size = ImGui::GetStyle().FontSizeBase * scale;
    if (!origins) {
        ImGui::PushFont(nullptr, size);
        const float w = ImGui::CalcTextSize(text).x;
        ImGui::PopFont();
        return w;
    }
    ImGui::PushFont(HeadingFont(), size * 1.05f);
    const float tracking = ImGui::GetFontSize() * 0.12f;
    float w = 0.0f;
    char ch[2] = {0, 0};
    for (const char* c = text; *c; ++c) {
        ch[0] = (*c >= 'a' && *c <= 'z') ? static_cast<char>(*c - 'a' + 'A') : *c;
        w += ImGui::CalcTextSize(ch).x + tracking;
    }
    ImGui::PopFont();
    return w > 0.0f ? w - tracking : 0.0f;
}

void HeadingText(const char* text, float scale, const ImVec4& color) {
    const bool origins = CurrentTheme() == ThemeId::Origins && HeadingFont();
    const float size = ImGui::GetStyle().FontSizeBase * scale;
    if (!origins) {
        ImGui::PushFont(nullptr, size);
        ImGui::TextColored(color, "%s", text);
        ImGui::PopFont();
        return;
    }
    // letter-spaced uppercase (drawn letter by letter)
    ImGui::PushFont(HeadingFont(), size * 1.05f);
    const float tracking = ImGui::GetFontSize() * 0.12f;
    const ImVec2 pos = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
    ImDrawList* dl = ImGui::GetWindowDrawList();
    const ImU32 col = ImGui::GetColorU32(color);
    float x = pos.x;
    char ch[2] = {0, 0};
    for (const char* c = text; *c; ++c) {
        ch[0] = (*c >= 'a' && *c <= 'z') ? static_cast<char>(*c - 'a' + 'A') : *c;
        dl->AddText(ImVec2(x, pos.y), col, ch);
        x += ImGui::CalcTextSize(ch).x + tracking;
    }
    const float h = ImGui::GetTextLineHeight();
    ImGui::PopFont();
    ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(x - pos.x - tracking, h));
}
void StatusDot(const ImVec4& color, float radius) {
    const ImVec2 p = ImGui::GetCursorScreenPos();
    const float y = p.y + ImGui::GetTextLineHeight() * 0.5f;
    ImGui::GetWindowDrawList()->AddCircleFilled(ImVec2(p.x + radius, y), radius, ImGui::GetColorU32(color));
    ImGui::Dummy(ImVec2(radius * 2.0f, ImGui::GetTextLineHeight()));
}

}  // namespace acp::ui

```

`src/ui/widgets.h`:

```h
#pragma once

#include "imgui.h"

namespace acp::ui {

// Uplay-style toggle switch. Navigable (A/Enter/click). Returns true when toggled.
// showText: show "On"/"Off" to its right.
bool ToggleSwitch(const char* id, bool* value, bool showText = true);

// Panel-style slider: thin track, filled part in accent colour, round grab; the value is not shown inside the
// slider (it sits next to it). Ctrl+click still opens exact input. Returns like ImGui::SliderScalar.
bool ValueSlider(const char* id, ImGuiDataType type, void* value, const void* min, const void* max, const char* format,
                 ImGuiSliderFlags flags);

// Combo with a slim chevron instead of an arrow button (otherwise like ImGui::BeginCombo; EndCombo only if true).
bool ComboBox(const char* id, const char* preview);

// Heading: in the Origins theme uses the serif font, uppercase and letter-spaced; otherwise the normal font.
// scale: size relative to the base font.
void HeadingText(const char* text, float scale, const ImVec4& color);
// Width HeadingText() will take for this text and scale.
float HeadingWidth(const char* text, float scale);

// Small filled circle at the current cursor position (no layout advance beyond SameLine).
void StatusDot(const ImVec4& color, float radius);

}  // namespace acp::ui

```

`tests/acp_tests.cpp`:

```cpp
// acp_tests - unit tests for acp_core (no test framework, run via CTest).
#include <clocale>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <fstream>
#include <functional>
#include <locale>
#include <sstream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "ini_document.h"
#include "test_main.h"
#include "param_table.h"

using namespace acp;

namespace {

std::string ReadFile(const char* path) {
    std::ifstream f(path, std::ios::binary);
    std::ostringstream ss;
    ss << f.rdbuf();
    return ss.str();
}

const std::string& Reference() {
    static const std::string bytes = ReadFile(ACP_REFERENCE_INI);
    return bytes;
}

std::vector<std::string> SplitCrlf(const std::string& s) {
    std::vector<std::string> out;
    std::size_t start = 0, pos;
    while ((pos = s.find("\r\n", start)) != std::string::npos) {
        out.push_back(s.substr(start, pos - start));
        start = pos + 2;
    }
    return out;
}

std::size_t CountDiffBytes(const std::string& a, const std::string& b) {
    if (a.size() != b.size()) return static_cast<std::size_t>(-1);
    std::size_t n = 0;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < a.size(); ++i) n += (a[i] != b[i]);
    return n;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

TEST(ReferenceFileIsPresent) {
    CHECK(Reference().size() == 3518);
}

TEST(RoundtripReferenceIsByteIdentical) {
    ParseReport rep;
    const auto doc = IniDocument::Parse(Reference(), &rep);
    CHECK(doc.Serialize() == Reference());
    CHECK(rep.issues.empty());
}

TEST(TableMatchesReferenceKeyTypeDefaultAndOrder) {
    const auto lines = SplitCrlf(Reference());
    CHECK(lines.size() == kParamCount);
    static_assert(kParamCount == 109);
    const auto doc = IniDocument::Parse(Reference());
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < lines.size() && i < kParamCount; ++i) {
        const ParamDesc& d = ParamTable()[i];
        const auto eq = lines[i].find(" = ");
        CHECK(eq != std::string::npos);
        const std::string key = lines[i].substr(0, eq);
        const std::string tok = lines[i].substr(eq + 3);
        CHECK(d.key == key);
        const bool isBoolToken = tok == "true" || tok == "false";
        CHECK((d.type == ParamType::Bool) == isBoolToken);
        const auto v = doc.Get(d.id);
        CHECK(v.has_value());
        if (!v) continue;
        if (d.type == ParamType::Float) {
            // The game writes decimals truncated ("%d"); status values are not compared.
            if (!d.readOnly) CHECK(std::trunc(d.def.f) == v->f);
        } else {
            CHECK(*v == d.def);
            CHECK(IniDocument::FormatValue(d.def) == tok);  // default exactly in the game's format
        }
    }
}

TEST(TypeCounts) {
    int b = 0, i = 0, f = 0;
    for (const auto& d : ParamTable()) {
        b += d.type == ParamType::Bool;
        i += d.type == ParamType::Int;
        f += d.type == ParamType::Float;
    }
    CHECK(b == 20);
    CHECK(i == 77);
    CHECK(f == 12);
    int ro = 0;
    for (const auto& d : ParamTable()) ro += d.readOnly;
    CHECK(ro == 3);  // DayNightTime, DamageInSandstorm (status values), PostProcessCustomFilterIndex (no effect)
    int reload = 0, local = 0;
    for (const auto& d : ParamTable()) {
        reload += d.reloadsWorld;
        local += d.local;
    }
    CHECK(reload == 11);
    CHECK(local == 6);
    CHECK(Desc(ParamId::PlayerHealthFactor).reloadsWorld && Desc(ParamId::PostProcessCustomTint).local);
    CHECK(Desc(ParamId::DayNightTime).readOnly && Desc(ParamId::DamageInSandstorm).readOnly);
}

TEST(CategoryCountsAndRules) {
    int counts[5] = {};
    int rules = 0;
    for (const auto& d : ParamTable()) {
        ++counts[static_cast<int>(d.category)];
        bool hasRule = false;
        for (const auto& w : d.disabledWhen) {
            if (!w.Active()) continue;
            hasRule = true;
            CHECK(Desc(w.param).type == ParamType::Bool);
            CHECK(w.param != d.id);
        }
        rules += hasRule;
        CHECK(InUiRange(d, d.def));
        if (d.type != ParamType::Bool) CHECK(d.uiMin.type == d.type && d.uiMax.type == d.type);
    }
    CHECK(counts[static_cast<int>(Category::HeroGeneral)] == 19);
    CHECK(counts[static_cast<int>(Category::HeroFighting)] == 18);
    CHECK(counts[static_cast<int>(Category::NpcGeneral)] == 14);
    CHECK(counts[static_cast<int>(Category::NpcFighting)] == 18);
    CHECK(counts[static_cast<int>(Category::World)] == 40);
    CHECK(rules == 24);  // custom filter values depend on their own switch (a setting), not on a parameter
}

TEST(TableMatchesCsvKeys) {
    const auto csv = ReadFile(ACP_PARAM_CSV);
    std::istringstream in(csv);
    std::string line;
    std::getline(in, line);  // header line
    std::size_t n = 0;
    while (std::getline(in, line)) {
        if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\r') line.pop_back();
        if (line.empty()) continue;
        const std::string key = line.substr(0, line.find(';'));
        CHECK(n < kParamCount && ParamTable()[n].key == key);
        ++n;
    }
    CHECK(n == kParamCount);
}

TEST(FindParamByKey) {
    CHECK(FindParam("PlayerMaxLevel") == ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel);
    CHECK(!FindParam("playermaxlevel").has_value());
    CHECK(!FindParam("DoesNotExist").has_value());
}

TEST(SetChangesOnlyValueToken) {
    auto doc = IniDocument::Parse(Reference());
    doc.Set(ParamId::PlayerMovementSpeedMultiplier, ParamValue::Int(300));
    const auto changed = doc.Serialize();
    CHECK(CountDiffBytes(changed, Reference()) == 1);  // "100" -> "300"
    CHECK(changed.find("\r\nPlayerMovementSpeedMultiplier = 300\r\n") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(doc.Get(ParamId::PlayerMovementSpeedMultiplier) == ParamValue::Int(300));

    doc.Set(ParamId::PlayerMovementSpeedMultiplier, ParamValue::Int(100));
    CHECK(doc.Serialize() == Reference());
}

TEST(SetLengthChangingValuesAndBool) {
    auto doc = IniDocument::Parse(Reference());
    doc.Set(ParamId::PlayerHealthFactor, ParamValue::Int(1000));
    doc.Set(ParamId::WorldMapUnfogged, ParamValue::Bool(true));
    doc.Set(ParamId::RebelsLevelScale, ParamValue::Int(-5));
    doc.Set(ParamId::DamageInSandstorm, ParamValue::Float(2.5f));
    const auto s = doc.Serialize();
    CHECK(s.find("\r\nPlayerHealthFactor = 1000\r\n") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(s.find("\r\nWorldMapUnfogged = true\r\n") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(s.find("\r\nRebelsLevelScale = -5\r\n") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(s.find("\r\nDamageInSandstorm = 2.5\r\n") != std::string::npos);
    // +1 (100->1000), -1 (false->true), +1 (0->-5), -5 (0.000000->2.5)
    CHECK(s.size() == Reference().size() - 4);
    // Readable again and stable
    const auto again = IniDocument::Parse(s);
    CHECK(again.Serialize() == s);
    CHECK(again.Get(ParamId::RebelsLevelScale) == ParamValue::Int(-5));
}

TEST(WrongTypeIsIgnored) {
    auto doc = IniDocument::Parse(Reference());
    doc.Set(ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel, ParamValue::Bool(true));
    CHECK(doc.Serialize() == Reference());
}

TEST(UnknownAndMalformedLinesArePreserved) {
    const std::string in =
        "PlayerMaxLevel = 40\r\n"
        "SomeFutureKey = 7\r\n"
        "\r\n"
        "; Kommentar\r\n"
        "garbage line\r\n"
        "  PlayerHealthFactor\t=   150  \r\n"
        "[AnimusControlPanel Settings]\r\n"
        "ActivePreset = 2\r\n";
    ParseReport rep;
    auto doc = IniDocument::Parse(in, &rep);
    CHECK(doc.Serialize() == in);
    CHECK(rep.Has(ParseIssue::Kind::UnknownKey));
    CHECK(rep.Has(ParseIssue::Kind::Malformed));
    CHECK(rep.Has(ParseIssue::Kind::Section));
    CHECK(rep.Has(ParseIssue::Kind::MissingKey));
    CHECK(doc.Get(ParamId::PlayerHealthFactor) == ParamValue::Int(150));

    doc.Set(ParamId::PlayerHealthFactor, ParamValue::Int(200));
    const auto s = doc.Serialize();
    CHECK(s.find("  PlayerHealthFactor\t=   200  \r\n") != std::string::npos);  // only the token changed

    // A missing key is inserted before the first section.
    doc.Set(ParamId::WorldMapUnfogged, ParamValue::Bool(true));
    const auto s2 = doc.Serialize();
    const auto ins = s2.find("WorldMapUnfogged = true\r\n");
    CHECK(ins != std::string::npos && ins < s2.find("[AnimusControlPanel Settings]"));
}

TEST(LfFileReadAndWrittenWithLf) {
    std::string lf = Reference();
    std::string out;
    for (char c : lf)
        if (c != '\r') out += c;
    auto doc = IniDocument::Parse(out);
    CHECK(doc.Serialize() == out);
    CHECK(doc.Get(ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel) == ParamValue::Int(40));
    doc.Set(ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel, ParamValue::Int(55));
    CHECK(doc.Serialize().find("\nPlayerMaxLevel = 55\n") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(doc.Serialize().find('\r') == std::string::npos);
}

TEST(MissingKeyAppendedWithCrlfEvenWithoutTrailingNewline) {
    auto doc = IniDocument::Parse("PlayerMaxLevel = 40\r\nPlayerHealthFactor = 100");
    CHECK(!doc.Contains(ParamId::NPCHealthFactor));
    CHECK(!doc.Get(ParamId::NPCHealthFactor).has_value());
    doc.Set(ParamId::NPCHealthFactor, ParamValue::Int(250));
    CHECK(doc.Serialize() == "PlayerMaxLevel = 40\r\nPlayerHealthFactor = 100\r\nNPCHealthFactor = 250\r\n");
}

TEST(EmptyDocument) {
    auto doc = IniDocument::Parse("");
    CHECK(doc.Serialize().empty());
    doc.Set(ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel, ParamValue::Int(45));
    CHECK(doc.Serialize() == "PlayerMaxLevel = 45\r\n");
}

TEST(BomPreserved) {
    const std::string in = "\xEF\xBB\xBFPlayerMaxLevel = 40\r\n";
    ParseReport rep;
    const auto doc = IniDocument::Parse(in, &rep);
    CHECK(doc.Serialize() == in);
    CHECK(doc.Get(ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel) == ParamValue::Int(40));
    CHECK(!rep.Has(ParseIssue::Kind::Malformed));
}

TEST(TolerantValueParsing) {
    CHECK(IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Bool, "True") == ParamValue::Bool(true));
    CHECK(IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Bool, "0") == ParamValue::Bool(false));
    CHECK(!IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Bool, "yes").has_value());
    CHECK(IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Int, "-12") == ParamValue::Int(-12));
    CHECK(IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Int, "+7") == ParamValue::Int(7));
    CHECK(IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Int, "100.000000") == ParamValue::Int(100));
    CHECK(!IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Int, "1.5").has_value());
    CHECK(!IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Int, "abc").has_value());
    CHECK(!IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Int, "").has_value());
    CHECK(IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Float, "3") == ParamValue::Float(3.0f));
    CHECK(!IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Float, "3,5").has_value());
}

TEST(InvalidValueAndDuplicateReported) {
    ParseReport rep;
    const auto doc = IniDocument::Parse("PlayerMaxLevel = abc\r\nPlayerMaxLevel = 50\r\nPlayerHealthFactor =\r\n", &rep);
    CHECK(rep.Has(ParseIssue::Kind::InvalidValue));
    CHECK(rep.Has(ParseIssue::Kind::Duplicate));
    CHECK(!doc.Get(ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel).has_value());  // first line wins
    auto copy = doc;
    copy.Set(ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel, ParamValue::Int(45));
    copy.Set(ParamId::PlayerHealthFactor, ParamValue::Int(120));
    CHECK(copy.Serialize() == "PlayerMaxLevel = 45\r\nPlayerMaxLevel = 45\r\nPlayerHealthFactor = 120\r\n");
}

TEST(FloatFormatIsLocaleIndependent) {
    const char* prev = std::setlocale(LC_ALL, nullptr);
    const std::string saved = prev ? prev : "C";
    std::setlocale(LC_ALL, "de-DE");
    try {
        std::locale::global(std::locale("de-DE"));
    } catch (...) {
    }
    CHECK(IniDocument::FormatValue(ParamValue::Float(0.0f)) == "0");
    CHECK(IniDocument::FormatValue(ParamValue::Float(-0.0f)) == "0");
    CHECK(IniDocument::FormatValue(ParamValue::Float(0.5f)) == "0.5");
    CHECK(IniDocument::FormatValue(ParamValue::Float(6.0f)) == "6");
    CHECK(IniDocument::FormatValue(ParamValue::Float(1234.5f)) == "1234.5");
    CHECK(IniDocument::ParseValue(ParamType::Float, "1.250000") == ParamValue::Float(1.25f));
    std::locale::global(std::locale::classic());
    std::setlocale(LC_ALL, saved.c_str());
}

}  // namespace

int main() {
    for (const auto& [name, fn] : acptest::Registry()) {
        const int before = acptest::g_failures;
        fn();
        std::printf("%s %s\n", acptest::g_failures == before ? "[ OK ]" : "[FAIL]", name);
    }
    std::printf("\n%zu tests, %d failed checks\n", acptest::Registry().size(), acptest::g_failures);
    return acptest::g_failures == 0 ? 0 : 1;
}
```

`tests/pacing_tests.cpp`:

```cpp
// Tests for the frame-rate limiter (math part and a rough timing check).
#include <windows.h>

#include <chrono>

#include "frame_pacer.h"
#include "test_main.h"

using namespace acp::hook::pacing;

TEST(PacingOnePercentLow) {
    float ms[200];
    for (float& v : ms) v = 10.0f;  // 100 FPS
    ms[17] = 50.0f;                  // two stutters of 50 ms each
    ms[123] = 50.0f;
    CHECK(OnePercentLowFps(ms, 200) > 19.9f && OnePercentLowFps(ms, 200) < 20.1f);  // 1 % of 200 = 2 frames
    CHECK(OnePercentLowFps(ms, 0) == 0.0f);
}

TEST(PacingHoldsTargetRate) {
    SetLimits(100, 0);
    BeforePresent(true);  // first call only syncs the clock
    const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    for (int i = 0; i < 30; ++i) BeforePresent(true);
    const auto ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();
    CHECK(ms >= 280 && ms <= 360);  // 30 frames of 10 ms each (generous for busy test machines)
    Stats st;
    GetStats(st);
    CHECK(st.limit == 100);
    CHECK(st.fps > 90.0f && st.fps < 110.0f);
    SetLimits(0, 0);
}
```

`tests/pad_filter_tests.cpp`:

```cpp
// Tests for PadFilter (unit-testable part).
#include "pad_filter.h"
#include "test_main.h"

using namespace acp::input;

namespace {

struct Sim {
    PadFilter f;
    PadFilterConfig cfg;
    std::uint64_t t = 1000;
    int toggles = 0;
    PadFilterOut last;
    // One poll step every `dt` ms
    PadFilterOut Step(std::uint16_t buttons, PadGate gate = PadGate::Pass, std::uint64_t dt = 8, std::uint8_t lt = 0) {
        t += dt;
        PadRaw r;
        r.buttons = buttons;
        r.lt = lt;
        last = f.Step(t, true, r, gate, cfg);
        toggles += last.toggleFired;
        return last;
    }
};

}  // namespace

TEST(PadChordTogglesOnceAndIsNeverVisible) {
    Sim s;
    bool sawChordButton = false;
    s.Step(pad::Back);  // View first
    sawChordButton |= (s.last.visible.buttons & (pad::Back | pad::Start)) != 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 100; ++i) {  // both held for 800 ms
        s.Step(pad::Back | pad::Start);
        sawChordButton |= (s.last.visible.buttons & (pad::Back | pad::Start)) != 0;
    }
    s.Step(pad::Start);  // release one button first
    sawChordButton |= (s.last.visible.buttons & (pad::Back | pad::Start)) != 0;
    s.Step(0);
    CHECK(s.toggles == 1);
    CHECK(!sawChordButton);
}

TEST(PadShortTapIsReplayedAtLeastTwoPollsAnd60ms) {
    for (int tapMs : {30, 60, 100}) {
        Sim s;
        int visiblePolls = 0;
        std::uint64_t firstVisible = 0, lastVisible = 0;
        const int pollsHeld = tapMs / 8;
        for (int i = 0; i < pollsHeld; ++i) {
            s.Step(pad::Start);
            if (s.last.visible.buttons & pad::Start) {
                if (!visiblePolls) firstVisible = s.t;
                lastVisible = s.t;
                ++visiblePolls;
            }
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < 30; ++i) {
            s.Step(0);
            if (s.last.visible.buttons & pad::Start) {
                if (!visiblePolls) firstVisible = s.t;
                lastVisible = s.t;
                ++visiblePolls;
            }
        }
        CHECK(visiblePolls >= 2);
        CHECK(lastVisible - firstVisible + 8 >= 60);
        CHECK(s.toggles == 0);
    }
}

TEST(PadLongSingleHoldPassesThroughAfterDefer) {
    Sim s;
    bool visibleLate = false, visibleEarly = false;
    for (int i = 0; i < 40; ++i) {  // 320 ms View only
        s.Step(pad::Back);
        const bool vis = s.last.visible.buttons & pad::Back;
        if (s.t - 1000 < 120) visibleEarly |= vis;
        else visibleLate |= vis;
    }
    CHECK(!visibleEarly);
    CHECK(visibleLate);
    CHECK(s.toggles == 0);
}

TEST(PadBlockZeroesEverythingAndPacketCountsVisibleChanges) {
    Sim s;
    const auto a = s.Step(pad::A);
    CHECK(a.visible.buttons == pad::A);
    const auto b = s.Step(pad::A, PadGate::Block);
    CHECK(b.visible.buttons == 0 && b.visible.lt == 0);
    const auto c = s.Step(pad::A | pad::X, PadGate::Block, 8, 200);
    CHECK(c.visible == PadRaw{});
    CHECK(c.packet == b.packet);  // visible state unchanged
    CHECK(b.packet != a.packet);
}

TEST(PadReleaseLatchHidesHeldButtonsAfterClose) {
    Sim s;
    s.Step(pad::B, PadGate::Block, 8, 255);
    const auto closed = s.Step(pad::B, PadGate::Pass, 8, 255);  // closed, B and RT/LT still held
    CHECK((closed.visible.buttons & pad::B) == 0);
    CHECK(closed.visible.lt == 0);
    const auto other = s.Step(pad::B | pad::A, PadGate::Pass, 8, 255);
    CHECK((other.visible.buttons & pad::A) != 0);  // newly pressed button is visible
    CHECK((other.visible.buttons & pad::B) == 0);
    s.Step(pad::A);                                  // B released, triggers released
    const auto again = s.Step(pad::B | pad::A, PadGate::Pass, 8, 255);
    CHECK((again.visible.buttons & pad::B) != 0);  // normal again after release
    CHECK(again.visible.lt == 255);
}

TEST(PadDisconnectResets) {
    Sim s;
    s.Step(pad::Back);
    PadRaw none;
    const auto d = s.f.Step(s.t + 8, false, none, PadGate::Pass, s.cfg);
    CHECK(d.visible == PadRaw{});
    const auto r = s.Step(pad::A);
    CHECK(r.visible.buttons == pad::A);
}

TEST(PadChordDisabledPassesEverything) {
    Sim s;
    s.cfg.chordEnabled = false;
    const auto o = s.Step(pad::Back | pad::Start);
    CHECK(o.visible.buttons == (pad::Back | pad::Start));
    for (int i = 0; i < 100; ++i) s.Step(pad::Back | pad::Start);
    CHECK(s.toggles == 0);
}
```
