`tests/proxy_tests.cpp`:

```cpp
// dinput8 proxy test: loads the proxy DLL by path and creates a real IDirectInput8W.
#define DIRECTINPUT_VERSION 0x0800
#include <windows.h>
#include <dinput.h>

#include <string>

#include "test_main.h"

TEST(ProxyForwardsDirectInput8Create) {
    HMODULE proxy = LoadLibraryW(ACP_PROXY_DLL);
    CHECK(proxy != nullptr);
    if (!proxy) return;
    using Fn = HRESULT(WINAPI*)(HINSTANCE, DWORD, REFIID, LPVOID*, LPUNKNOWN);
    auto create = reinterpret_cast<Fn>(GetProcAddress(proxy, "DirectInput8Create"));
    CHECK(create != nullptr);
    CHECK(GetProcAddress(proxy, "GetdfDIJoystick") != nullptr);
    if (create) {
        IDirectInput8W* di = nullptr;
        const HRESULT hr = create(GetModuleHandleW(nullptr), DIRECTINPUT_VERSION, IID_IDirectInput8W,
                                  reinterpret_cast<void**>(&di), nullptr);
        CHECK(SUCCEEDED(hr));
        CHECK(di != nullptr);
        if (di) di->Release();
    }
    // The real system DLL must be loaded (forwarding), not a second proxy.
    wchar_t sys[MAX_PATH]{};
    GetSystemDirectoryW(sys, MAX_PATH);
    bool realLoaded = false;
    wchar_t path[MAX_PATH]{};
    HMODULE real = nullptr;
    std::wstring sysDll = std::wstring(sys) + L"\\dinput8.dll";
    if (GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT, sysDll.c_str(), &real) && real != proxy) {
        GetModuleFileNameW(real, path, MAX_PATH);
        realLoaded = _wcsicmp(path, sysDll.c_str()) == 0;
    }
    CHECK(realLoaded);
}

TEST(ProxyLoadsChainedDll) {
    // Put the proxy and a "foreign" DLL (copy of System32\version.dll) as dinput8_chain.dll into a temp folder.
    wchar_t tmp[MAX_PATH]{};
    GetTempPathW(MAX_PATH, tmp);
    const std::wstring dir = std::wstring(tmp) + L"acp_chain_" + std::to_wstring(GetCurrentProcessId()) + L"\\";
    CreateDirectoryW(dir.c_str(), nullptr);
    const std::wstring proxyCopy = dir + L"dinput8.dll";
    const std::wstring chain = dir + L"dinput8_chain.dll";
    wchar_t sys[MAX_PATH]{};
    GetSystemDirectoryW(sys, MAX_PATH);
    CHECK(CopyFileW(ACP_PROXY_DLL, proxyCopy.c_str(), FALSE));
    CHECK(CopyFileW((std::wstring(sys) + L"\\version.dll").c_str(), chain.c_str(), FALSE));
    HMODULE proxy = LoadLibraryW(proxyCopy.c_str());
    CHECK(proxy != nullptr);
    bool loaded = false;
    for (int i = 0; i < 100 && !loaded; ++i) {  // the proxy loads on its own thread
        HMODULE m = nullptr;
        loaded = GetModuleHandleExW(GET_MODULE_HANDLE_EX_FLAG_UNCHANGED_REFCOUNT, chain.c_str(), &m) && m != nullptr;
        if (!loaded) Sleep(20);
    }
    CHECK(loaded);
}
```

`tests/sync_tests.cpp`:

```cpp
// Tests for SyncService (real files in a temp folder).
#include <windows.h>

#include <chrono>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
#include <string>
#include <thread>

#include "ini_document.h"
#include "platform.h"
#include "sync_service.h"
#include "test_main.h"

using namespace acp;
namespace fs = std::filesystem;

namespace {

std::string Slurp(const fs::path& p) {
    std::ifstream f(p, std::ios::binary);
    std::ostringstream ss;
    ss << f.rdbuf();
    return ss.str();
}

void Spit(const fs::path& p, const std::string& s) {
    std::ofstream f(p, std::ios::binary | std::ios::trunc);
    f << s;
}

struct TempDir {
    fs::path dir;
    TempDir() {
        wchar_t tmp[MAX_PATH]{};
        GetTempPathW(MAX_PATH, tmp);
        dir = fs::path(tmp) / (L"acp_tests_" + std::to_wstring(GetCurrentProcessId()) + L"_" +
                               std::to_wstring(GetTickCount64()));
        fs::create_directories(dir / L"docs");
    }
    ~TempDir() {
        std::error_code ec;
        fs::remove_all(dir, ec);
    }
    fs::path Ini() const { return dir / L"docs" / L"ACP.ini"; }
    fs::path Data() const { return dir / L"data"; }
};

template <typename Pred>
bool WaitSnap(SyncService& s, Snapshot& snap, Pred pred, int timeoutMs = 3000) {
    const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::milliseconds(timeoutMs);
    while (std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < end) {
        s.TryConsumeSnapshot(snap);
        if (pred(snap)) return true;
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
    }
    s.TryConsumeSnapshot(snap);
    return pred(snap);
}

SyncConfig Config(const TempDir& t) {
    SyncConfig c;
    c.candidates = {t.Ini()};
    c.userDataDir = t.Data();
    return c;
}

const std::string& Ref() {
    static const std::string s = Slurp(ACP_REFERENCE_INI);
    return s;
}

}  // namespace

TEST(SyncLoadsAndWritesOnlyToken) {
    TempDir t;
    Spit(t.Ini(), Ref());
    SyncService s;
    s.Start(Config(t));
    Snapshot snap;
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.status == SyncStatus::Ok; }));
    CHECK(snap.present.all());
    CHECK(snap.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel)] == ParamValue::Int(40));

    s.SubmitEdit(ParamId::PlayerMovementSpeedMultiplier, ParamValue::Int(300));
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.writes == 1 && x.status == SyncStatus::Ok; }));
    const std::string after = Slurp(t.Ini());
    CHECK(after.size() == Ref().size());
    CHECK(after.find("\r\nPlayerMovementSpeedMultiplier = 300\r\n") != std::string::npos);
    // backup created
    int backups = 0;
    std::error_code ec;
    for (const auto& e : fs::directory_iterator(t.Data() / L"Backup", ec)) backups += e.path().extension() == L".bak";
    CHECK(backups == 1);
    s.Stop(true);
}

TEST(SyncNoWriteWithoutChange) {
    TempDir t;
    Spit(t.Ini(), Ref());
    const auto before = platform::StatFile(t.Ini());
    SyncService s;
    s.Start(Config(t));
    Snapshot snap;
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.status == SyncStatus::Ok; }));
    s.SubmitEdit(ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel, ParamValue::Int(40));  // same value
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(400));
    s.Stop(true);
    s.TryConsumeSnapshot(snap);
    CHECK(snap.writes == 0);
    CHECK(platform::StatFile(t.Ini()) == before);
    CHECK(!fs::exists(t.Data() / L"Backup"));
}

TEST(SyncCoalescesRapidEdits) {
    TempDir t;
    Spit(t.Ini(), Ref());
    SyncService s;
    s.Start(Config(t));
    Snapshot snap;
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.status == SyncStatus::Ok; }));
    for (int i = 0; i < 100; ++i) s.SubmitEdit(ParamId::PlayerHealthFactor, ParamValue::Int(101 + i));
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) {
        return x.status == SyncStatus::Ok && x.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(ParamId::PlayerHealthFactor)] == ParamValue::Int(200);
    }));
    s.Stop(true);
    CHECK(snap.writes >= 1 && snap.writes <= 2);
    CHECK(Slurp(t.Ini()).find("\r\nPlayerHealthFactor = 200\r\n") != std::string::npos);
}

TEST(SyncDetectsExternalChangeAndKeepsIt) {
    TempDir t;
    Spit(t.Ini(), Ref());
    SyncService s;
    s.Start(Config(t));
    Snapshot snap;
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.status == SyncStatus::Ok; }));
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(50));
    auto doc = IniDocument::Parse(Ref());
    doc.Set(ParamId::NPCHealthFactor, ParamValue::Int(555));
    Spit(t.Ini(), doc.Serialize());
    const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) {
        return x.externalChanges >= 1 && x.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(ParamId::NPCHealthFactor)] == ParamValue::Int(555);
    }));
    CHECK(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start < std::chrono::milliseconds(1500));
    // Our change to another key keeps the external one
    s.SubmitEdit(ParamId::PlayerHealthFactor, ParamValue::Int(150));
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.writes == 1; }));
    const auto txt = Slurp(t.Ini());
    CHECK(txt.find("NPCHealthFactor = 555\r\n") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(txt.find("PlayerHealthFactor = 150\r\n") != std::string::npos);
    s.Stop(true);
}

TEST(SyncRetriesWhenLocked) {
    TempDir t;
    Spit(t.Ini(), Ref());
    SyncService s;
    s.Start(Config(t));
    Snapshot snap;
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.status == SyncStatus::Ok; }));
    HANDLE lock = CreateFileW(t.Ini().c_str(), GENERIC_READ, 0, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, 0, nullptr);
    CHECK(lock != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE);
    s.SubmitEdit(ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel, ParamValue::Int(45));
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(300));
    CloseHandle(lock);
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.writes == 1 && x.status == SyncStatus::Ok; }, 4000));
    CHECK(Slurp(t.Ini()).find("\r\nPlayerMaxLevel = 45\r\n") != std::string::npos);
    s.Stop(true);
}

TEST(SyncNeverCreatesMissingIni) {
    TempDir t;
    SyncService s;
    s.Start(Config(t));
    Snapshot snap;
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.status == SyncStatus::NotFound; }));
    s.SubmitEdit(ParamId::PlayerMaxLevel, ParamValue::Int(45));
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(300));
    s.Stop(true);
    CHECK(!fs::exists(t.Ini()));
}

TEST(SyncCreatesMissingIniOnRequest) {
    TempDir t;
    SyncService s;
    s.Start(Config(t));
    Snapshot snap;
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.status == SyncStatus::NotFound; }));
    s.CreateMissingIni();
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.status == SyncStatus::Ok; }));
    CHECK(snap.present.all());
    // Same format as the game-written reference (keys, order, spelling). Only intended difference:
    // the two sandstorm intervals get the real default 0.5 instead of the 0 from the game's file.
    std::string expected = Ref();
    for (const char* k : {"PlayerDamageInSandstormInterval", "NPCDamageInSandstormInterval"}) {
        const std::string from = std::string("\r\n") + k + " = 0\r\n", to = std::string("\r\n") + k + " = 0.5\r\n";
        const auto pos = expected.find(from);
        CHECK(pos != std::string::npos);
        if (pos != std::string::npos) expected.replace(pos, from.size(), to);
    }
    const std::string created = Slurp(t.Ini());
    CHECK(created == expected);
    s.CreateMissingIni();  // second call: file exists, overwrite nothing
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(200));
    CHECK(Slurp(t.Ini()) == created);
    s.Stop(true);
}

TEST(SyncBacksUpGameSaves) {
    // Only reads the real save games (copies into a temp folder). Without saves: a clean message.
    TempDir t;
    Spit(t.Ini(), Ref());
    SyncService s;
    s.Start(Config(t));
    Snapshot snap;
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.status == SyncStatus::Ok; }));
    s.BackupGameSaves();
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) {
        return x.message.rfind("Game saves backed up", 0) == 0 || x.message == "No game saves found";
    }, 10000));
    if (!platform::GameSaveDirs().empty()) {
        int files = 0;
        std::error_code ec;
        for (const auto& e : fs::recursive_directory_iterator(t.Data() / L"SaveBackups", ec)) files += e.is_regular_file(ec);
        CHECK(files > 0);
    }
    s.Stop(true);
}

TEST(SyncPresetsAndReset) {
    TempDir t;
    Spit(t.Ini(), Ref());
    SyncService s;
    s.Start(Config(t));
    Snapshot snap;
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.status == SyncStatus::Ok; }));

    s.SubmitEdit(ParamId::PlayerHealthFactor, ParamValue::Int(300));
    s.SavePreset("My: Preset?");  // invalid characters are removed
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.presets.size() == 1; }));
    CHECK(!snap.presets.empty() && snap.presets[0].name == "My Preset");
    CHECK(fs::exists(t.Data() / L"Presets" / L"My Preset.ini"));

    s.ResetToDefaults();
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) {
        return x.status == SyncStatus::Ok && x.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(ParamId::PlayerHealthFactor)] == ParamValue::Int(100);
    }));
    {
        // After the reset all settable values are at the game defaults; status values stay untouched.
        const auto doc = IniDocument::Parse(Slurp(t.Ini()));
        for (const ParamDesc& d : ParamTable()) {
            if (d.readOnly) CHECK(doc.GetRaw(d.id) == IniDocument::Parse(Ref()).GetRaw(d.id));
            else CHECK(doc.Get(d.id) == d.def);
        }
    }

    s.ApplyPreset("My Preset");
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) {
        return x.status == SyncStatus::Ok && x.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(ParamId::PlayerHealthFactor)] == ParamValue::Int(300);
    }));
    s.DeletePreset("My Preset");
    CHECK(WaitSnap(s, snap, [](const Snapshot& x) { return x.presets.empty(); }));
    s.Stop(true);
}

TEST(AtomicWriteRoundtrip) {
    TempDir t;
    const fs::path p = t.dir / L"x.ini";
    CHECK(!platform::WriteFileAtomic(p, "a", platform::WriteStrategy::AtomicReplace).ok);  // creates nothing
    CHECK(platform::WriteFileAtomic(p, "abc", platform::WriteStrategy::AtomicReplace, true).ok);
    CHECK(Slurp(p) == "abc");
    CHECK(platform::WriteFileAtomic(p, "de", platform::WriteStrategy::AtomicReplace).ok);
    CHECK(Slurp(p) == "de");
    CHECK(platform::WriteFileAtomic(p, "fgh", platform::WriteStrategy::InPlace).ok);
    CHECK(Slurp(p) == "fgh");
    CHECK(!fs::exists(t.dir / L"x.ini.acpr.tmp"));
}
```

`tests/test_main.h`:

```h
#pragma once
// Minimal test framework (run via CTest).
#include <cstdio>
#include <functional>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

namespace acptest {
inline int g_failures = 0;
inline std::vector<std::pair<const char*, std::function<void()>>>& Registry() {
    static std::vector<std::pair<const char*, std::function<void()>>> r;
    return r;
}
struct Register {
    Register(const char* name, std::function<void()> fn) { Registry().emplace_back(name, std::move(fn)); }
};
}  // namespace acptest

#define TEST(name)                                           \
    static void name();                                      \
    static const acptest::Register reg_##name{#name, name};  \
    static void name()

#define CHECK(cond)                                                             \
    do {                                                                        \
        if (!(cond)) {                                                          \
            ++acptest::g_failures;                                              \
            std::printf("  FAILED %s:%d: %s\n", __FILE__, __LINE__, #cond);     \
        }                                                                       \
    } while (0)
```

`tools/gen_param_table.py`:

```py
"""Generates src/core/param_table_data.inc and src/core/param_ids.inc from docs/param_map.csv.

Usage (from the project root):  python -I tools/gen_param_table.py
The CSV is the source of the parameter data; the generated files are checked in
and never edited by hand.
"""
import csv
import pathlib
import re
import sys

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
CSV_PATH = ROOT / "docs" / "param_map.csv"
OUT_DATA = ROOT / "src" / "core" / "param_table_data.inc"
OUT_IDS = ROOT / "src" / "core" / "param_ids.inc"

TYPES = {"B": "Bool", "I": "Int", "F": "Float"}
UNITS = {"": "None", "%": "Percent", "s": "Seconds", "DPS": "Dps", "Lvl": "Level",
         "n": "Count", "enum": "Enum", "?": "Unknown"}
CONF = {"D": "NotApplicable", "G": "Game", "X": "ExeLabel", "C": "Community", "A": "Assumed", "?": "Unknown"}
CATS = {"HG": "HeroGeneral", "HF": "HeroFighting", "NG": "NpcGeneral", "NF": "NpcFighting", "W": "World"}
GROUPS = {
    "Vital": "Vitality", "Movement": "Movement", "WaterHeat": "Water & Heat",
    "Progress": "Progression", "Gear": "Equipment", "Character": "Character & Companions",
    "Damage": "Damage", "Assassination": "Assassination", "Adrenaline": "Adrenaline",
    "Desync": "Desynchronization", "Perception": "Perception", "LevelScale": "Level Scaling",
    "Combat": "Combat", "RelPlayer": "Relations to Player", "RelFactions": "Faction Relations",
    "Time": "Time of Day", "Sandstorm": "Sandstorm", "Map": "World Map",
    "Vehicles": "Vehicles & Siege", "Environment": "Environment & Loot", "PostFx": "Game Look (Post-Processing)",
    "CustomFx": "Custom Filter (ACP Reloaded)",
}
# Tooltip explanations, based on analysis of the game code.
HINTS = {
    "PlayerMaxLevelEnabled": "Level cap: once you reach the level below, you gain no more XP. Does not grant levels.",
    "PlayerMaxLevel": "Level at which XP gain stops (only with the level cap enabled). Never raises or lowers your level.",
    "CanAcquireAllSkills": "Unlocks the whole skill tree: any ability can be bought without its prerequisites. Ability points are still required.",
    "MaxPurchasableSkillsNumberEnabled": "Skill cap: limits how many abilities can be bought in total.",
    "MaxPurchasableSkillsNumber": "Maximum number of abilities (only with the skill cap enabled). Removes nothing already owned.",
    "PlayerExperienceGainingMultiplier": "XP gain in percent. 100 is the maximum - this can only reduce XP gain.",
    "PlayableCharacterIndex": "Character you play as. Switches immediately.",
    "DayPeriodTimeModifier": "Higher = slower time. 100% = normal, 5% = 20x faster, 400% = 4x slower. Applies immediately.",
    "DayPeriodsMorningDuration": "Real-time minutes for the morning (4-8 h), scaled by 'Length of the day'.",
    "DayPeriodsMiddayDuration": "Real-time minutes for midday (8-16 h), scaled by 'Length of the day'.",
    "DayPeriodsAfternoonDuration": "Real-time minutes for the afternoon (16-20 h), scaled by 'Length of the day'.",
    "DayPeriodsNightDuration": "Real-time minutes for the night (20-4 h), scaled by 'Length of the day'.",
    "PostProcessFilterSettings": "One of eleven built-in looks (colour filter, saturation, tint).",
    "PostProcessFilterEnabled": "Enables the game's built-in look selected below.",
    "DesyncOnAssassinationEnabled": "May have no effect in the current game version (no reader found).",
    "DesyncOnKillEnabled": "May have no effect in the current game version (no reader found).",
    "WorldMapUnfogged": "Removes the fog on the world map. Reportedly cannot be undone in this save.",
    "PostProcessCustomFilmGrain": "Film grain. Rendered by ACP Reloaded (the game ignores this value).",
    "PostProcessCustomVignette": "Darkens the screen edges. Rendered by ACP Reloaded.",
    "PostProcessCustomBrightness": "Brightness multiplier. Rendered by ACP Reloaded.",
    "PostProcessCustomTemperature": "Colour temperature: negative = cooler, positive = warmer. Rendered by ACP Reloaded.",
    "PostProcessCustomTint": "Tint: negative = magenta, positive = green. Rendered by ACP Reloaded.",
    "PostProcessCustomSaturation": "Saturation: -100 = black and white. Rendered by ACP Reloaded.",
}

# Original labels from the EXE where they differ from the key.
LABELS = {
    "PostProcessCustomFilmGrain": "Film Grain",
    "PostProcessCustomVignette": "Vignette",
    "PostProcessCustomBrightness": "Brightness",
    "PostProcessCustomTemperature": "Temperature",
    "PostProcessCustomTint": "Tint",
    "PostProcessCustomSaturation": "Saturation",
    "PostProcessFilterSettings": "Look",
    "PlayableCharacterIndex": "Playable Character",
    "AssassinationEnabled": "Assassination Enable",
    "NPCHurtboxScaleFactor": "Hurtbox Scale Factor",
    "SandstormBaseProbability": "Base Probability",
    "SandstormSpeedFactor": "Speed Factor",
    "SandstormCooldownFactor": "Cooldown Factor",
    "SandstormLifetimeFactor": "Lifetime Factor",
    "DayPeriodTimeModifier": "Length of the day",
    "DayPeriodsMorningDuration": "Morning (4-8 h), minutes",
    "DayPeriodsMiddayDuration": "Midday (8-16 h), minutes",
    "DayPeriodsAfternoonDuration": "Afternoon (16-20 h), minutes",
    "DayPeriodsNightDuration": "Night (20-4 h), minutes",
    "DesyncInSandstormEnabled": "Desynchronization in sandstorm",
    "DesyncOnAssassinationEnabled": "Desynchronization after assassinations",
    "DesyncOnKillEnabled": "Desynchronization after kills",
    "DesyncOnAssassinationNumber": "Number of assassinations before desync",
    "DesyncOnKillNumber": "Number of kills before desync",
    "DesyncOnAssassinationCooldownTime": "Cooldown time before reset (sec)",
    "DesyncOnKillCooldownTime": "Cooldown time before reset (sec)",
    "PlayerDamageInSandstormInterval": "Player damage interval (sec)",
    "NPCDamageInSandstormInterval": "NPC damage interval (sec)",
    "PlayerDamageInSandstormLightDPS": "Player light zone DPS",
    "PlayerDamageInSandstormHeavyDPS": "Player heavy zone DPS",
    "PlayerDamageInSandstormInsideDPS": "Player center zone DPS",
    "NPCDamageInSandstormLightDPS": "NPC light zone DPS",
    "NPCDamageInSandstormHeavyDPS": "NPC heavy zone DPS",
    "NPCDamageInSandstormInsideDPS": "NPC center zone DPS",
    "PostProcessFilterEnabled": "Post Process Filter",
}


def auto_label(key: str) -> str:
    s = re.sub(r"(?<=[a-z0-9])(?=[A-Z])", " ", key)
    s = re.sub(r"(?<=[A-Z])(?=[A-Z][a-z])", " ", s)
    return s


def value_literal(type_code: str, text: str) -> str:
    if type_code == "B":
        return f"ParamValue::Bool({'true' if text == 'true' else 'false'})"
    if type_code == "I":
        return f"ParamValue::Int({int(text)})"
    return f"ParamValue::Float({float(text)!r}f)"


def cpp_str(s: str) -> str:
    return '"' + s.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace('"', '\\"') + '"'


def main() -> int:
    with CSV_PATH.open(newline="", encoding="ascii") as f:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(f, delimiter=";"))
    keys = [r["Key"] for r in rows]
    if len(keys) != len(set(keys)):
        print("Duplicate keys in the CSV", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    index = {k: i for i, k in enumerate(keys)}

    ids = ["// Generated by tools/gen_param_table.py from docs/param_map.csv - do not edit.\n"]
    data = ["// Generated by tools/gen_param_table.py from docs/param_map.csv - do not edit.\n"]
    for r in rows:
        k, t = r["Key"], r["Type"]
        ids.append(f"{k},\n")
        conds = []
        if r["DisabledWhen"]:
            for part in r["DisabledWhen"].split("||"):
                m = re.fullmatch(r"(\w+)==(true|false)", part.strip())
                if not m or m.group(1) not in index or rows[index[m.group(1)]]["Type"] != "B":
                    print(f"Invalid condition for {k}: {part}", file=sys.stderr)
                    return 1
                conds.append(f"DisabledWhen{{ParamId::{m.group(1)}, {m.group(2)}}}")
        while len(conds) < 2:
            conds.append("DisabledWhen{}")
        if t == "B":
            lo, hi = "ParamValue::Bool(false)", "ParamValue::Bool(true)"
        else:
            lo, hi = value_literal(t, r["Min"]), value_literal(t, r["Max"])
        data.append(
            "    ParamDesc{"
            f"ParamId::{k}, {cpp_str(k)}, {cpp_str(LABELS.get(k, auto_label(k)))}, "
            f"{cpp_str(GROUPS[r['Group']])}, ParamType::{TYPES[t]}, Category::{CATS[r['Cat']]}, "
            f"Unit::{UNITS[r['Unit']]}, Confidence::{CONF[r['Conf']]}, "
            f"{value_literal(t, r['Default'])}, {lo}, {hi}, "
            f"{{{conds[0]}, {conds[1]}}}, {'true' if r.get('ReadOnly') == '1' else 'false'}, "
            f"{'true' if r.get('Reload') == '1' else 'false'}, {'true' if r.get('Local') == '1' else 'false'}, "
            f"{cpp_str(HINTS.get(k, ''))}"
            "},\n"
        )
    OUT_IDS.write_text("".join(ids), encoding="ascii", newline="\n")
    OUT_DATA.write_text("".join(data), encoding="ascii", newline="\n")
    print(f"{len(rows)} parameters written.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`tools/ui_sandbox/main.cpp`:

```cpp
// ACPReloaded_Editor.exe - standalone ACP.ini editor with the same UI as the overlay.
// Options:   --ini <path>     edit a different ACP.ini (tests)
//            --data <folder>  different user data folder (presets/backups)
//            --frames <n>     exit after n frames (self-test)
//            --load-overlay   load ACPReloaded.dll into this process (hook self-test)
#include <windows.h>
#include <d3d11.h>
#include <dxgi.h>
#include <xinput.h>
#include <shellapi.h>

#include <cstdlib>
#include <string>

#include "imgui.h"
#include "imgui_impl_dx11.h"
#include "imgui_impl_win32.h"
#include "pad_feed.h"
#include "panel.h"
#include "platform.h"
#include "sync_service.h"
#include "portraits.h"
#include "theme.h"

extern IMGUI_IMPL_API LRESULT ImGui_ImplWin32_WndProcHandler(HWND hWnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wParam, LPARAM lParam);

namespace {

ID3D11Device* g_device = nullptr;

ID3D11DeviceContext* g_context = nullptr;
IDXGISwapChain* g_swapChain = nullptr;
ID3D11RenderTargetView* g_rtv = nullptr;
UINT g_resizeW = 0, g_resizeH = 0;

void CreateRtv() {
    ID3D11Texture2D* back = nullptr;
    if (SUCCEEDED(g_swapChain->GetBuffer(0, IID_PPV_ARGS(&back)))) {
        g_device->CreateRenderTargetView(back, nullptr, &g_rtv);
        back->Release();
    }
}

void ReleaseRtv() {
    if (g_rtv) {
        g_rtv->Release();
        g_rtv = nullptr;
    }
}

bool CreateDevice(HWND hwnd) {
    DXGI_SWAP_CHAIN_DESC sd{};
    sd.BufferCount = 2;
    sd.BufferDesc.Format = DXGI_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_UNORM;
    sd.BufferUsage = DXGI_USAGE_RENDER_TARGET_OUTPUT;
    sd.OutputWindow = hwnd;
    sd.SampleDesc.Count = 1;
    sd.Windowed = TRUE;
    sd.SwapEffect = DXGI_SWAP_EFFECT_FLIP_DISCARD;
    const D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL levels[] = {D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL_11_0, D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL_10_0};
    D3D_FEATURE_LEVEL got{};
    HRESULT hr = D3D11CreateDeviceAndSwapChain(nullptr, D3D_DRIVER_TYPE_HARDWARE, nullptr, 0, levels, 2,
                                               D3D11_SDK_VERSION, &sd, &g_swapChain, &g_device, &got, &g_context);
    if (hr == DXGI_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED)
        hr = D3D11CreateDeviceAndSwapChain(nullptr, D3D_DRIVER_TYPE_WARP, nullptr, 0, levels, 2, D3D11_SDK_VERSION, &sd,
                                           &g_swapChain, &g_device, &got, &g_context);
    if (FAILED(hr)) return false;
    CreateRtv();
    return true;
}

void DestroyDevice() {
    ReleaseRtv();
    if (g_swapChain) g_swapChain->Release();
    if (g_context) g_context->Release();
    if (g_device) g_device->Release();
    g_swapChain = nullptr;
    g_context = nullptr;
    g_device = nullptr;
}

LRESULT CALLBACK WndProc(HWND hwnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wParam, LPARAM lParam) {
    if (ImGui_ImplWin32_WndProcHandler(hwnd, msg, wParam, lParam)) return TRUE;
    switch (msg) {
        case WM_SIZE:
            if (wParam != SIZE_MINIMIZED) {
                g_resizeW = LOWORD(lParam);
                g_resizeH = HIWORD(lParam);
            }
            return 0;
        case WM_SYSCOMMAND:
            if ((wParam & 0xfff0) == SC_KEYMENU) return 0;
            break;
        case WM_DESTROY:
            PostQuitMessage(0);
            return 0;
    }
    return DefWindowProcW(hwnd, msg, wParam, lParam);
}

// Self-test: save our own frame (before Present) as a 32-bit BMP - not a screen capture.
void SaveBackbufferBmp(const std::wstring& path) {
    ID3D11Texture2D* back = nullptr;
    if (FAILED(g_swapChain->GetBuffer(0, IID_PPV_ARGS(&back)))) return;
    D3D11_TEXTURE2D_DESC td{};
    back->GetDesc(&td);
    td.Usage = D3D11_USAGE_STAGING;
    td.BindFlags = 0;
    td.CPUAccessFlags = D3D11_CPU_ACCESS_READ;
    td.MiscFlags = 0;
    ID3D11Texture2D* staging = nullptr;
    if (SUCCEEDED(g_device->CreateTexture2D(&td, nullptr, &staging))) {
        g_context->CopyResource(staging, back);
        D3D11_MAPPED_SUBRESOURCE m{};
        if (SUCCEEDED(g_context->Map(staging, 0, D3D11_MAP_READ, 0, &m))) {
            const UINT w = td.Width, h = td.Height;
            std::string data(static_cast<std::size_t>(w) * h * 4, char(0));
            for (UINT y = 0; y < h; ++y) {
                const auto* src = static_cast<const unsigned char*>(m.pData) + static_cast<std::size_t>(y) * m.RowPitch;
                auto* dst = reinterpret_cast<unsigned char*>(data.data()) + static_cast<std::size_t>(h - 1 - y) * w * 4;
                for (UINT x = 0; x < w; ++x) {  // RGBA -> BGRA
                    dst[x * 4 + 0] = src[x * 4 + 2];
                    dst[x * 4 + 1] = src[x * 4 + 1];
                    dst[x * 4 + 2] = src[x * 4 + 0];
                    dst[x * 4 + 3] = 255;
                }
            }
            g_context->Unmap(staging, 0);
            BITMAPFILEHEADER fh{};
            BITMAPINFOHEADER ih{};
            ih.biSize = sizeof(ih);
            ih.biWidth = static_cast<LONG>(w);
            ih.biHeight = static_cast<LONG>(h);
            ih.biPlanes = 1;
            ih.biBitCount = 32;
            ih.biCompression = BI_RGB;
            fh.bfType = 0x4D42;
            fh.bfOffBits = sizeof(fh) + sizeof(ih);
            fh.bfSize = fh.bfOffBits + static_cast<DWORD>(data.size());
            std::string file(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&fh), sizeof(fh));
            file.append(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&ih), sizeof(ih));
            file += data;
            acp::platform::WriteFileAtomic(path, file, acp::platform::WriteStrategy::AtomicReplace, true);
        }
        staging->Release();
    }
    back->Release();
}

std::wstring ArgValue(int argc, wchar_t** argv, const wchar_t* name) {
    for (int i = 1; i + 1 < argc; ++i)
        if (wcscmp(argv[i], name) == 0) return argv[i + 1];
    return {};
}
bool HasArg(int argc, wchar_t** argv, const wchar_t* name) {
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i)
        if (wcscmp(argv[i], name) == 0) return true;
    return false;
}

}  // namespace

int WINAPI wWinMain(HINSTANCE hInst, HINSTANCE, PWSTR, int) {
    int argc = 0;
    wchar_t** argv = CommandLineToArgvW(GetCommandLineW(), &argc);
    const std::wstring iniArg = ArgValue(argc, argv, L"--ini");
    const std::wstring dataArg = ArgValue(argc, argv, L"--data");
    const std::wstring framesArg = ArgValue(argc, argv, L"--frames");
    const long maxFrames = framesArg.empty() ? -1 : std::wcstol(framesArg.c_str(), nullptr, 10);
    const bool loadOverlay = HasArg(argc, argv, L"--load-overlay");
    const std::wstring shotArg = ArgValue(argc, argv, L"--screenshot");

    ImGui_ImplWin32_EnableDpiAwareness();
    const float dpi = ImGui_ImplWin32_GetDpiScaleForMonitor(MonitorFromPoint(POINT{0, 0}, MONITOR_DEFAULTTOPRIMARY));

    WNDCLASSEXW wc{sizeof(wc), CS_CLASSDC, WndProc, 0, 0, hInst, nullptr, LoadCursorW(nullptr, IDC_ARROW), nullptr, nullptr,
                   L"ACPReloadedEditor", nullptr};
    RegisterClassExW(&wc);
    // --size WxH: window size in pixels (tests the layout in small/large windows)
    int winW = static_cast<int>(1100 * dpi), winH = static_cast<int>(760 * dpi);
    if (const std::wstring sz = ArgValue(argc, argv, L"--size"); !sz.empty()) swscanf_s(sz.c_str(), L"%dx%d", &winW, &winH);
    HWND hwnd = CreateWindowW(wc.lpszClassName, L"ACP Reloaded - Animus Control Panel Editor", WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW, 100,
                              100, winW, winH, nullptr, nullptr, hInst,
                              nullptr);
    if (!CreateDevice(hwnd)) {
        MessageBoxW(nullptr, L"Direct3D 11 could not be initialized.", L"ACP Reloaded", MB_ICONERROR);
        return 1;
    }
    ShowWindow(hwnd, maxFrames > 0 ? SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE : SW_SHOWDEFAULT);
    UpdateWindow(hwnd);

    // Logging + Sync
    acp::SyncConfig cfg;
    if (!iniArg.empty()) cfg.candidates = {std::filesystem::path(iniArg)};
    if (!dataArg.empty()) cfg.userDataDir = dataArg;
    else if (auto d = acp::platform::UserDataDir()) cfg.userDataDir = *d;
    cfg.bundledPresetsDir = acp::platform::ThisModuleDir() / L"ACP_Presets";
    acp::platform::log::SetFile(cfg.userDataDir / L"ACPReloaded_Editor.log");
    acp::platform::log::Write("Editor started");
    acp::SyncService sync;
    sync.Start(cfg);

    IMGUI_CHECKVERSION();
    ImGui::CreateContext();
    ImGuiIO& io = ImGui::GetIO();
    io.IniFilename = nullptr;
    io.LogFilename = nullptr;
    io.ConfigFlags |= ImGuiConfigFlags_NavEnableKeyboard | ImGuiConfigFlags_NavEnableGamepad;
    wchar_t winDir[MAX_PATH]{};
    GetWindowsDirectoryW(winDir, MAX_PATH);
    static std::string fontData;
    if (auto f = acp::platform::ReadFileBytes(std::filesystem::path(winDir) / L"Fonts" / L"segoeui.ttf")) fontData = *f;
    acp::ui::LoadUiFont(&fontData, 17.0f);
    ImGui::GetStyle().FontScaleDpi = dpi;
    acp::ui::ApplyTheme(acp::ui::ThemeId::UplayClassic, dpi);
    ImGui_ImplWin32_Init(hwnd);
    ImGui_ImplDX11_Init(g_device, g_context);

    if (loadOverlay) {
        // --overlay-file <name>: different file name in the editor folder (e.g. ACPReloaded.asi for the ASI loader test)
        const std::wstring overlayFile = ArgValue(argc, argv, L"--overlay-file");
        const auto dll = acp::platform::ThisModuleDir() / (overlayFile.empty() ? std::wstring(L"ACPReloaded.dll") : overlayFile);
        SetEnvironmentVariableW(L"ACP_RELOADED_ANY_HOST", L"1");
        if (!LoadLibraryW(dll.c_str())) acp::platform::log::Write("ACPReloaded.dll could not be loaded");
    }

    acp::ui::Panel panel(sync);
    if (const std::wstring page = ArgValue(argc, argv, L"--page"); !page.empty()) panel.SetPage(_wtoi(page.c_str()));
    if (HasArg(argc, argv, L"--pad")) panel.ForcePadLayout();
    if (HasArg(argc, argv, L"--char-grid")) panel.DebugOpenCharacterGrid();
    if (const std::wstring sc = ArgValue(argc, argv, L"--scroll"); !sc.empty()) panel.DebugScroll(static_cast<float>(_wtof(sc.c_str())));
    if (const std::wstring q = ArgValue(argc, argv, L"--search"); !q.empty()) panel.DebugSetSearch(acp::platform::Narrow(q).c_str());
    acp::ui::PadFeeder feeder;
    acp::ui::PanelEnv env;
    env.fillViewport = true;
    env.baseScale = dpi;
    env.liveHint = "Changes are saved to ACP.ini immediately";
    // Character portraits (embedded resources of this program)
    CoInitializeEx(nullptr, COINIT_APARTMENTTHREADED);
    acp::platform::log::Write("Portraits: " + std::to_string(acp::ui::portraits::Decode(GetModuleHandleW(nullptr))) + "/22");
    env.portrait = [](int n) { return acp::ui::portraits::Get(g_device, n); };
    if (HasArg(argc, argv, L"--fake-game")) {
        // Test the game-function rows without the game: sample values, actions have no effect
        env.game.supported = true;
        env.game.valid = true;
        env.game.stateText = "active (editor sample)";
        env.game.hours = 14.5f;
        env.game.timeText = "running";
        env.game.acpTimeBlocked = HasArg(argc, argv, L"--time-blocked");  // show the "Normal speed" hint
        if (env.game.acpTimeBlocked) {
            env.game.resultText = "Time set to 07:30.";
            env.game.resultIsTime = true;
        }
        env.game.hasPlayer = true;
        env.game.level = 23;
        env.game.xp = 48250;
        env.game.abilityPoints = 3;
        env.game.weatherValid = true;
        env.game.weather = 0.35f;
        env.game.tpValid = true;
        env.game.tpText = "ready";
        env.game.tpReady = true;
        env.game.tpSource = 1;
        env.game.tpM6A = true;
        env.game.tpModuleSet = true;
        env.game.tpDistance = 1834.0f;
        env.game.tpDistanceXY = 1829.0f;
        env.game.tpMaxDistance = 30000.0f;
        env.game.questAvailable = true;
        env.game.questReady = true;
        env.game.questText = "ready";
        env.game.questDistance = 742.0f;
        env.game.questIcons = 2;
        env.game.questUsable = 1;
        env.game.speedAvailable = true;
        env.teleportRequest = [](int) {};
        env.gameAction = [](acp::ui::GameAction, int, float, float) {};
        env.holdTime = [](bool, float) {};
    }

    long frame = 0;
    bool done = false;
    while (!done) {
        MSG msg;
        while (PeekMessageW(&msg, nullptr, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE)) {
            TranslateMessage(&msg);
            DispatchMessageW(&msg);
            if (msg.message == WM_QUIT) done = true;
        }
        if (done) break;
        if (g_resizeW != 0 && g_resizeH != 0) {
            ReleaseRtv();
            g_swapChain->ResizeBuffers(0, g_resizeW, g_resizeH, DXGI_FORMAT_UNKNOWN, 0);
            g_resizeW = g_resizeH = 0;
            CreateRtv();
        }

        // Controller: first connected pad, only while the window is in the foreground.
        acp::input::PadRaw raw{};
        bool connected = false;
        if (GetForegroundWindow() == hwnd) {
            for (DWORD i = 0; i < XUSER_MAX_COUNT && !connected; ++i) {
                XINPUT_STATE st{};
                if (XInputGetState(i, &st) == ERROR_SUCCESS) {
                    connected = true;
                    raw = {st.Gamepad.wButtons, st.Gamepad.bLeftTrigger, st.Gamepad.bRightTrigger, st.Gamepad.sThumbLX,
                           st.Gamepad.sThumbLY, st.Gamepad.sThumbRX, st.Gamepad.sThumbRY};
                }
            }
        }
        acp::ui::PadActions actions;
        feeder.SetHorizontalCapture(panel.WantsPadHorizontal());
        feeder.Feed(io, connected, raw, actions);

        ImGui_ImplDX11_NewFrame();
        ImGui_ImplWin32_NewFrame();
        ImGui::NewFrame();
        panel.Draw(env, actions, nullptr);
        ImGui::Render();

        const float clear[4] = {0.03f, 0.035f, 0.045f, 1.0f};
        g_context->OMSetRenderTargets(1, &g_rtv, nullptr);
        g_context->ClearRenderTargetView(g_rtv, clear);
        ImGui_ImplDX11_RenderDrawData(ImGui::GetDrawData());
        if (!shotArg.empty() && maxFrames > 0 && frame + 1 == maxFrames) SaveBackbufferBmp(shotArg);
        g_swapChain->Present(1, 0);

        if (maxFrames > 0 && ++frame >= maxFrames) done = true;
    }

    panel.FlushActiveEdit();
    acp::ui::portraits::Release();
    ImGui_ImplDX11_Shutdown();
    ImGui_ImplWin32_Shutdown();
    ImGui::DestroyContext();
    sync.Stop(true);
    acp::platform::log::Write("Editor stopped");
    acp::platform::log::Flush();
    DestroyDevice();
    DestroyWindow(hwnd);
    UnregisterClassW(wc.lpszClassName, hInst);
    LocalFree(argv);
    return 0;
}

```
